### [1] [Evaluation as Evolution: Transforming Adversarial Diffusion into Closed-Loop Curricula for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07378)
*Yicheng Guo,Jiaqi Liu,Chengkai Xu,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E²框架将对抗性场景生成从静态验证转变为自适应进化课程，通过传输正则化稀疏控制和拓扑驱动支持选择，在保持真实性的同时高效发现安全临界故障案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在交互交通环境中面临静态数据集安全临界尾部事件稀缺的问题，导致学习策略偏向平均情况行为，降低鲁棒性。现有对抗性压力测试方法多为开环和事后处理，难以将发现的故障案例整合到训练过程中。

Method: 提出E²（Evaluation as Evolution）闭环框架，将对抗性场景合成建模为基于学习反向时间SDE先验的传输正则化稀疏控制问题。采用拓扑驱动支持选择识别关键交互智能体，引入拓扑锚定稳定生成过程，在约束偏离真实数据分布的同时实现针对性故障发现。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上碰撞故障发现提升9.01%，在nuPlan数据集上提升高达21.43%，同时保持低无效性和高真实性。将发现的边界案例用于闭环策略微调可获得显著的鲁棒性增益。

Conclusion: E²框架成功将对抗性生成从静态验证转变为自适应进化课程，在保持真实性的前提下高效发现安全临界故障案例，并能通过闭环微调提升自动驾驶策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles in interactive traffic environments are often limited by the scarcity of safety-critical tail events in static datasets, which biases learned policies toward average-case behaviors and reduces robustness. Existing evaluation methods attempt to address this through adversarial stress testing, but are predominantly open-loop and post-hoc, making it difficult to incorporate discovered failures back into the training process. We introduce Evaluation as Evolution ($E^2$), a closed-loop framework that transforms adversarial generation from a static validation step into an adaptive evolutionary curriculum. Specifically, $E^2$ formulates adversarial scenario synthesis as transport-regularized sparse control over a learned reverse-time SDE prior. To make this high-dimensional generation tractable, we utilize topology-driven support selection to identify critical interacting agents, and introduce Topological Anchoring to stabilize the process. This approach enables the targeted discovery of failure cases while strictly constraining deviations from realistic data distributions. Empirically, $E^2$ improves collision failure discovery by 9.01% on the nuScenes dataset and up to 21.43% on the nuPlan dataset over the strongest baselines, while maintaining low invalidity and high realism. It further yields substantial robustness gains when the resulting boundary cases are recycled for closed-loop policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [2] [Safe Large-Scale Robust Nonlinear MPC in Milliseconds via Reachability-Constrained System Level Synthesis on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07644)
*Jeffrey Fang,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPU-SLS：基于GPU并行化的安全鲁棒非线性模型预测控制框架，针对高维不确定机器人系统，实现实时联合优化名义轨迹、跟踪控制器和闭环可达集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理高维不确定机器人系统的实时安全控制问题，特别是需要同时优化名义轨迹、跟踪控制器和可达集时计算复杂度高，无法满足实时性要求

Method: 开发GPU并行化框架，采用顺序二次规划结合新型GPU加速QP求解器（使用并行关联扫描和自适应缓存的ADMM框架），并通过系统级合成优化鲁棒跟踪控制器和闭环可达集

Result: 性能显著提升：名义轨迹求解时间比CPU求解器减少97.7%，比GPU求解器减少71.8%；SLS控制可达性加速237倍；在复杂非线性系统（四足机器人61D、人形机器人75D）上实现100%经验安全性，平均20毫秒在线合成鲁棒控制策略

Conclusion: GPU-SLS框架成功实现了高维不确定机器人系统的实时安全鲁棒控制，通过GPU并行化显著提升计算效率，为复杂机器人系统的在线安全控制提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: We present GPU-SLS, a GPU-parallelized framework for safe, robust nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that scales to high-dimensional uncertain robotic systems and long planning horizons. Our method jointly optimizes an inequality-constrained, dynamically-feasible nominal trajectory, a tracking controller, and a closed-loop reachable set under disturbance, all in real-time. To efficiently compute nominal trajectories, we develop a sequential quadratic programming procedure with a novel GPU-accelerated quadratic program (QP) solver that uses parallel associative scans and adaptive caching within an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework. The same GPU QP backend is used to optimize robust tracking controllers and closed-loop reachable sets via system level synthesis (SLS), enabling reachability-constrained control in both fixed- and receding-horizon settings. We achieve substantial performance gains, reducing nominal trajectory solve times by 97.7% relative to state-of-the-art CPU solvers and 71.8% compared to GPU solvers, while accelerating SLS-based control and reachability by 237x. Despite large problem scales, our method achieves 100% empirical safety, unlike high-dimensional learning-based reachability baselines. We validate our approach on complex nonlinear systems, including whole-body quadrupeds (61D) and humanoids (75D), synthesizing robust control policies online on the GPU in 20 milliseconds on average and scaling to problems with 2 x 10^5 decision variables and 8 x 10^4 constraints. The implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/Jeff300fang/gpu_sls.

</details>


### [3] [OpenPRC: A Unified Open-Source Framework for Physics-to-Task Evaluation in Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07423)
*Yogesh Phalak,Wen Sin Lor,Apoorva Khairnar,Benjamin Jantzen,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenPRC是一个开源Python框架，为物理储层计算提供统一的物理到任务流程，通过五个模块实现GPU加速模拟、实验数据摄取、学习、分析和优化，使用通用HDF5模式确保可重复性和互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算利用物理基板的固有非线性动力学作为固定计算储层，提供能效高、具身化的机器学习范式。然而，现有工具通常只处理流程的孤立部分，缺乏能够统一表示高保真模拟轨迹和真实实验测量的框架，无法实现跨基板和数据源的可重复评估、分析和物理感知优化。

Method: OpenPRC采用模式驱动的物理到任务流程，包含五个核心模块：1) GPU加速的混合RK4-PBD物理引擎(demlat)；2) 基于视频的实验数据摄取层(openprc.vision)；3) 模块化学习层(reservoir)；4) 信息论分析和基准测试工具(analysis)；5) 物理感知优化(optimize)。通用HDF5模式确保GPU模拟和实验获取的轨迹能够无缝进入下游工作流程。

Result: 框架已展示的能力包括：折纸镶嵌结构的模拟、从物理储层中基于视频的轨迹提取、标准化的PRC基准测试、相关性诊断和容量分析的通用接口。该框架能够处理不同物理基板的模拟和实验数据。

Conclusion: OpenPRC填补了物理储层计算领域统一框架的空白，通过标准化数据接口和模块化设计，实现了跨基板和数据源的可重复评估、分析和优化。长期愿景是成为PRC社区的标准化层，兼容外部物理引擎如PyBullet、PyElastica和MERLIN。

Abstract: Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) leverages the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of physical substrates, mechanical, optical, spintronic, and beyond, as fixed computational reservoirs, offering a compelling paradigm for energy-efficient and embodied machine learning. However, the practical workflow for developing and evaluating PRC systems remains fragmented: existing tools typically address only isolated parts of the pipeline, such as substrate-specific simulation, digital reservoir benchmarking, or readout training. What is missing is a unified framework that can represent both high-fidelity simulated trajectories and real experimental measurements through the same data interface, enabling reproducible evaluation, analysis, and physics-aware optimization across substrates and data sources. We present OpenPRC, an open-source Python framework that fills this gap through a schema-driven physics-to-task pipeline built around five modules: a GPU-accelerated hybrid RK4-PBD physics engine (demlat), a video-based experimental ingestion layer (openprc.vision), a modular learning layer (reservoir), information-theoretic analysis and benchmarking tools (analysis), and physics-aware optimization (optimize). A universal HDF5 schema enforces reproducibility and interoperability, allowing GPU-simulated and experimentally acquired trajectories to enter the same downstream workflow without modification. Demonstrated capabilities include simulations of Origami tessellations, video-based trajectory extraction from a physical reservoir, and a common interface for standardized PRC benchmarking, correlation diagnostics, and capacity analysis. The longer-term vision is to serve as a standardizing layer for the PRC community, compatible with external physics engines including PyBullet, PyElastica, and MERLIN.

</details>


### [4] [CMP: Robust Whole-Body Tracking for Loco-Manipulation via Competence Manifold Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07457)
*Ziyang Cheng,Haoyu Wei,Hang Yin,Xiuwei Xu,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)方法，通过能力流形投影提高足式移动机械臂全身控制策略对分布外输入的鲁棒性，同时保持任务性能连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦控制方案对足式移动机械臂已展现鲁棒性，但学习型全身控制策略在跟踪全局末端执行器位姿时，对传感器噪声或不可行用户命令引起的分布外输入仍显脆弱。需要在不牺牲任务性能和连续性的前提下提高对这些扰动的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)框架：1) 采用Frame-Wise Safety Scheme将无限时域安全约束转化为计算高效的单步流形包含问题；2) 使用Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator区分训练分布外的未掌握意图；3) 引入Isomorphic Latent Space (ILS)对齐流形几何与安全概率，实现O(1)复杂度对任意分布外意图的无缝防御。

Result: 实验显示CMP在典型分布外场景中实现高达10倍的生存率提升（基线方法遭受灾难性失败），同时跟踪性能退化低于10%。系统展现出对分布外目标的"尽力而为"泛化行为，通过遵循能力边界逐步完成任务。

Conclusion: CMP方法有效提高了学习型全身控制策略对分布外输入的鲁棒性，在保持任务性能的同时实现了安全边界内的渐进式目标达成，为足式移动机械臂的鲁棒控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: While decoupled control schemes for legged mobile manipulators have shown robustness, learning holistic whole-body control policies for tracking global end-effector poses remains fragile against Out-of-Distribution (OOD) inputs induced by sensor noise or infeasible user commands. To improve robustness against these perturbations without sacrificing task performance and continuity, we propose Competence Manifold Projection (CMP). Specifically, we utilize a Frame-Wise Safety Scheme that transforms the infinite-horizon safety constraint into a computationally efficient single-step manifold inclusion. To instantiate this competence manifold, we employ a Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator that distinguishes unmastered intentions from the training distribution. We then introduce an Isomorphic Latent Space (ILS) that aligns manifold geometry with safety probability, enabling efficient O(1) seamless defense against arbitrary OOD intents. Experiments demonstrate that CMP achieves up to a 10-fold survival rate improvement in typical OOD scenarios where baselines suffer catastrophic failure, incurring under 10% tracking degradation. Notably, the system exhibits emergent ``best-effort'' generalization behaviors to progressively accomplish OOD goals by adhering to the competence boundaries. Result videos are available at: https://shepherd1226.github.io/CMP.

</details>


### [5] [On-Policy Distillation of Language Models for Autonomous Vehicle Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07944)
*Amirhossein Afsharrad,Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Sanjay Lall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何将大型语言模型（LLM）的运动规划知识有效迁移到更小、更可部署的学生模型中，提出了基于GPT-Driver框架的广义知识蒸馏方法，在模型规模减小5倍的情况下仍能接近教师模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动驾驶运动规划中展现出潜力，但将其部署到资源受限的车载系统存在挑战。需要研究如何将大型教师LLM的运动规划知识有效迁移到更小、更可部署的学生模型中。

Method: 基于GPT-Driver框架（将驾驶场景表示为语言提示，通过思维链推理生成轨迹点），研究了两种学生训练范式：1）策略内广义知识蒸馏（GKD），使用教师模型的密集令牌级反馈训练学生模型；2）密集反馈强化学习基线，将教师模型的log概率作为每令牌奖励信号。

Result: 在nuScenes基准测试中，GKD方法显著优于RL基线，在模型规模减小5倍的情况下仍能接近教师级性能，证明了策略内蒸馏作为部署LLM规划器的有效方法。

Conclusion: 策略内知识蒸馏是一种原则性且有效的方法，可用于在自动驾驶系统中部署基于LLM的规划器，在保持性能的同时显著减小模型规模，提高部署可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong potential for autonomous vehicle motion planning by reformulating trajectory prediction as a language generation problem. However, deploying capable LLMs in resource-constrained onboard systems remains a fundamental challenge. In this paper, we study how to effectively transfer motion planning knowledge from a large teacher LLM to a smaller, more deployable student model. We build on the GPT-Driver framework, which represents driving scenes as language prompts and generates waypoint trajectories with chain-of-thought reasoning, and investigate two student training paradigms: (i) on-policy generalized knowledge distillation (GKD), which trains the student on its own self-generated outputs using dense token-level feedback from the teacher, and (ii) a dense-feedback reinforcement learning (RL) baseline that uses the teacher's log-probabilities as per-token reward signals in a policy gradient framework. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that GKD substantially outperforms the RL baseline and closely approaches teacher-level performance despite a 5$\times$ reduction in model size. These results highlight the practical value of on-policy distillation as a principled and effective approach to deploying LLM-based planners in autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [6] [Active Reward Machine Inference From Raw State Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07480)
*Mohamad Louai Shehab,Antoine Aspeel,Necmiye Ozay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究在缺乏奖励、标签和状态机节点观测信息的情况下，直接从原始状态和策略信息中学习奖励机器，并扩展到主动学习设置以提高数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励机器是用于捕捉多阶段任务所需记忆的自动机结构，但手动指定奖励机器（包括捕捉决策依据的高级特征的标签函数）是一项艰巨任务。现有方法通常需要访问奖励、标签或机器节点观测信息，而在信息稀缺情况下如何学习奖励机器是一个重要问题。

Method: 提出在无法访问奖励、标签或机器节点观测信息的情况下，直接从原始状态和策略信息中学习奖励机器的方法。首先确定在这种信息稀缺情况下，何种轨迹数据足以学习奖励机器。然后扩展到主动学习设置，通过增量查询轨迹扩展来改进数据效率和计算效率。

Result: 在多个网格世界示例中验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了在信息稀缺情况下学习奖励机器的可行性，以及主动学习在提高数据效率和计算效率方面的优势。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在缺乏奖励、标签和状态机节点观测信息的情况下，直接从原始状态和策略信息中学习奖励机器是可行的。主动学习扩展进一步提高了数据效率和计算效率，为在实际机器人应用中自动学习复杂任务结构提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reward machines are automaton-like structures that capture the memory required to accomplish a multi-stage task. When combined with reinforcement learning or optimal control methods, they can be used to synthesize robot policies to achieve such tasks. However, specifying a reward machine by hand, including a labeling function capturing high-level features that the decisions are based on, can be a daunting task. This paper deals with the problem of learning reward machines directly from raw state and policy information. As opposed to existing works, we assume no access to observations of rewards, labels, or machine nodes, and show what trajectory data is sufficient for learning the reward machine in this information-scarce regime. We then extend the result to an active learning setting where we incrementally query trajectory extensions to improve data (and indirectly computational) efficiency. Results are demonstrated with several grid world examples.

</details>


### [7] [Grasp as You Dream: Imitating Functional Grasping from Generated Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07517)
*Chao Tang,Jiacheng Xu,Haofei Lu,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Hong Zhang,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspDreamer利用视觉生成模型合成的人类演示数据实现零样本功能抓取，无需大规模数据收集，在多种机器人手上表现出优越的数据效率和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能够在开放世界环境中执行功能抓取的通用机器人面临巨大挑战，现有方法要么局限于狭窄的对象/任务集，要么依赖大规模数据收集来捕捉现实世界的变化性。

Method: GraspDreamer利用在互联网规模人类数据上预训练的视觉生成模型（如视频生成模型）合成人类演示，这些模型隐式编码了人类与物理世界交互的通用先验知识，再结合特定具身的动作优化来实现功能抓取。

Result: 在公共基准测试中，GraspDreamer在不同机器人手上展现出比先前方法更优越的数据效率和泛化性能。真实世界评估进一步验证了其在真实机器人上的有效性。

Conclusion: GraspDreamer提供了一种无需大规模数据收集的零样本功能抓取方法，能够自然扩展到下游操作任务，并生成支持视觉运动策略学习的数据。

Abstract: Building generalist robots capable of performing functional grasping in everyday, open-world environments remains a significant challenge due to the vast diversity of objects and tasks. Existing methods are either constrained to narrow object/task sets or rely on prohibitively large-scale data collection to capture real-world variability. In this work, we present an alternative approach, GraspDreamer, a method that leverages human demonstrations synthesized by visual generative models (VGMs) (e.g., video generation models) to enable zero-shot functional grasping without labor-intensive data collection. The key idea is that VGMs pre-trained on internet-scale human data implicitly encode generalized priors about how humans interact with the physical world, which can be combined with embodiment-specific action optimization to enable functional grasping with minimal effort. Extensive experiments on the public benchmarks with different robot hands demonstrate the superior data efficiency and generalization performance of GraspDreamer compared to previous methods. Real-world evaluations further validate the effectiveness on real robots. Additionally, we showcase that GraspDreamer can (1) be naturally extended to downstream manipulation tasks, and (2) can generate data to support visuomotor policy learning.

</details>


### [8] [SANDO: Safe Autonomous Trajectory Planning for Dynamic Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07599)
*Kota Kondo,Jesús Tordesillas,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SANDO是一种用于3D动态未知环境的安全轨迹规划器，通过热图A*全局规划、时空安全飞行走廊和混合整数二次规划优化，在保证安全的同时实现快速重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D动态未知环境中，障碍物位置和运动先验未知，碰撞自由计划可能随时变得不安全，需要快速重规划。现有软约束规划器速度快但不能保证无碰撞路径，而硬约束方法确保安全但计算时间长，需要解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 1. 热图A*全局规划器使用软成本引导路径远离高风险区域；时空安全飞行走廊生成器产生时间分层多面体，仅按每个时间层的最坏情况可达集膨胀障碍物。2. 将轨迹优化表述为具有硬碰撞避免约束的混合整数二次规划，采用变量消除技术减少决策变量。3. 在明确的速度边界和估计误差假设下进行形式化安全分析。

Result: 变量消除使优化时间加速高达7.4倍；时空安全飞行走廊在密集动态环境中对可行性至关重要；在标准化静态基准、障碍物丰富的静态森林和动态环境的基准测试中，SANDO在所有难度级别上始终实现最高成功率且无约束违反；感知实验在无地面真实障碍物信息下表现稳健；硬件实验在静态环境中完成6次安全飞行，在动态障碍物中完成10次安全飞行。

Conclusion: SANDO成功解决了3D动态未知环境中安全与计算效率的权衡问题，通过创新的热图规划、时空安全走廊和优化技术，在保证形式化安全的同时实现了快速重规划，在仿真和硬件实验中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: SANDO is a safe trajectory planner for 3D dynamic unknown environments, where obstacle locations and motions are unknown a priori and a collision-free plan can become unsafe at any moment, requiring fast replanning. Existing soft-constraint planners are fast but cannot guarantee collision-free paths, while hard-constraint methods ensure safety at the cost of longer computation. SANDO addresses this trade-off through three contributions. First, a heat map-based A* global planner steers paths away from high-risk regions using soft costs, and a spatiotemporal safe flight corridor (STSFC) generator produces time-layered polytopes that inflate obstacles only by their worst-case reachable set at each time layer, rather than by the worst case over the entire horizon. Second, trajectory optimization is formulated as a Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) with hard collision-avoidance constraints, and a variable elimination technique reduces the number of decision variables, enabling fast computation. Third, a formal safety analysis establishes collision-free guarantees under explicit velocity-bound and estimation-error assumptions. Ablation studies show that variable elimination yields up to 7.4x speedup in optimization time, and that STSFCs are critical for feasibility in dense dynamic environments. Benchmark simulations against state-of-the-art methods across standardized static benchmarks, obstacle-rich static forests, and dynamic environments show that SANDO consistently achieves the highest success rate with no constraint violations across all difficulty levels; perception-only experiments without ground truth obstacle information confirm robust performance under realistic sensing. Hardware experiments on a UAV with fully onboard planning, perception, and localization demonstrate six safe flights in static environments and ten safe flights among dynamic obstacles.

</details>


### [9] [EgoVerse: An Egocentric Human Dataset for Robot Learning from Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07607)
*Ryan Punamiya,Simar Kareer,Zeyi Liu,Josh Citron,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Xiongyi Cai,Alexey Gavryushin,Jiaqi Chen,Davide Liconti,Lawrence Y. Zhu,Patcharapong Aphiwetsa,Baoyu Li,Aniketh Cheluva,Pranav Kuppili,Yangcen Liu,Dhruv Patel,Aidan Gao,Hye-Young Chung,Ryan Co,Renee Zbizika,Jeff Liu,Xiaomeng Xu,Haoyu Xiong,Geng Chen,Sebastiano Oliani,Chenyu Yang,Xi Wang,James Fort,Richard Newcombe,Josh Gao,Jason Chong,Garrett Matsuda,Aseem Doriwala,Marc Pollefeys,Robert Katzschmann,Xiaolong Wang,Shuran Song,Judy Hoffman,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoVerse是一个用于机器人学习的协作平台，统一了人类数据收集、处理和访问，包含1,362小时人类演示数据，并通过大规模研究探索人类到机器人的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模多样化数据，但机器人数据收集成本高且难以扩展。人类自我中心数据提供了丰富的日常操作行为，但现有数据集范围有限、难以扩展且分散在不同机构。

Method: 建立EgoVerse协作平台，统一数据收集、处理和访问框架；收集1,362小时人类演示数据（80k片段），涵盖1,965个任务、240个场景和2,087名演示者；进行大规模人类到机器人迁移研究，在多个实验室、任务和机器人形态下使用共享协议进行实验。

Result: 当前版本包含大规模标准化人类演示数据集；研究发现策略性能通常随人类数据增加而提升，但有效扩展取决于人类数据与机器人学习目标的对齐程度。

Conclusion: EgoVerse的数据集、平台和研究为人类数据驱动的机器人学习建立了可重复进展的基础，解决了数据碎片化和可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Robot learning increasingly depends on large and diverse data, yet robot data collection remains expensive and difficult to scale. Egocentric human data offer a promising alternative by capturing rich manipulation behavior across everyday environments. However, existing human datasets are often limited in scope, difficult to extend, and fragmented across institutions. We introduce EgoVerse, a collaborative platform for human data-driven robot learning that unifies data collection, processing, and access under a shared framework, enabling contributions from individual researchers, academic labs, and industry partners. The current release includes 1,362 hours (80k episodes) of human demonstrations spanning 1,965 tasks, 240 scenes, and 2,087 unique demonstrators, with standardized formats, manipulation-relevant annotations, and tooling for downstream learning. Beyond the dataset, we conduct a large-scale study of human-to-robot transfer with experiments replicated across multiple labs, tasks, and robot embodiments under shared protocols. We find that policy performance generally improves with increased human data, but that effective scaling depends on alignment between human data and robot learning objectives. Together, the dataset, platform, and study establish a foundation for reproducible progress in human data-driven robot learning. Videos and additional information can be found at https://egoverse.ai/

</details>


### [10] [Reset-Free Reinforcement Learning for Real-World Agile Driving: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07672)
*Kohei Honda,Hirotaka Hosogaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文实证研究了无重置强化学习在真实世界敏捷驾驶中的应用，发现仿真与真实世界存在显著性能差距，TD-MPC2在物理平台上表现最佳，而残差学习在真实世界中未能带来预期优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究真实世界高速极限驾驶场景下的无重置强化学习挑战，因为复杂的车辆动力学、执行延迟和未建模效应阻碍了精确仿真和直接的仿真到真实策略迁移。

Method: 使用模型预测路径积分控制作为重置策略和残差学习的基础策略，系统比较PPO、SAC和TD-MPC2三种代表性RL算法在仿真和真实世界实验中的表现，包括有无残差学习的情况。

Result: 仿真与真实世界存在明显差距：SAC+残差学习在仿真中表现最佳，但只有TD-MPC2在物理平台上持续优于MPPI基线；残差学习在仿真中明显有益，但在真实世界中未能迁移其优势，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 真实世界的无重置强化学习面临仿真中不存在的独特挑战，需要针对野外训练环境开发专门的算法。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical study of reset-free reinforcement learning (RL) for real-world agile driving, in which a physical 1/10-scale vehicle learns continuously on a slippery indoor track without manual resets. High-speed driving near the limits of tire friction is particularly challenging for learning-based methods because complex vehicle dynamics, actuation delays, and other unmodeled effects hinder both accurate simulation and direct sim-to-real transfer of learned policies. To enable autonomous training on a physical platform, we employ Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) as both the reset policy and the base policy for residual learning, and systematically compare three representative RL algorithms, i.e., PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2, with and without residual learning in simulation and real-world experiments. Our results reveal a clear gap between simulation and real-world: SAC with residual learning achieves the highest returns in simulation, yet only TD-MPC2 consistently outperforms the MPPI baseline on the physical platform. Moreover, residual learning, while clearly beneficial in simulation, fails to transfer its advantage to the real world and can even degrade performance. These findings reveal that reset-free RL in the real world poses unique challenges absent from simulation, calling for further algorithmic development tailored to training in the wild.

</details>


### [11] [Bird-Inspired Spatial Flapping Wing Mechanism via Coupled Linkages with Single Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07677)
*Daniel Huczala,Sun-Pill Jung,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Bennett连杆的仿鸟扑翼机构，采用两个耦合的空间四杆机构，单电机驱动实现空间扫掠和被动折叠协调运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间单环机构如Bennett连杆具有单自由度驱动和非平凡空间轨迹的优点，适合轻量化仿生机器人设计，但其几何任务综合因数学复杂性而难以应用

Method: 提出简化运动学方法，从包含所需表面积的四边形构建Bennett连杆；采用两个耦合的空间四杆机构，一个主动驱动产生空间扫掠，另一个串联耦合的连杆被动切换伸展和折叠状态

Result: 实现了单驱动输入的协调扫掠-折叠翼运动，降低了重量和控制复杂度；3D打印原型验证了预期的空间行程和被动折叠行为

Conclusion: 该架构成功展示了Bennett连杆在仿生机器人中的应用潜力，通过简化综合方法和被动状态切换机制，实现了轻量化、单驱动的扑翼运动

Abstract: Spatial single-loop mechanisms such as Bennett linkages offer a unique combination of one-degree-of-freedom actuation and nontrivial spatial trajectories, making them attractive for lightweight bio-inspired robotic design. However, although they appear simple and elegant, the geometric task-based synthesis is rather complicated and often avoided in engineering tasks due to the mathematical complexity involved. This paper presents a bird-inspired flapping-wing mechanism built from two coupled spatial four-bars, driven by a single motor. One linkage is actuated to generate the desired spatial sweeping stroke, while the serially coupled linkage remains unactuated and passively switches between extended and folded wing configurations over the stroke cycle. We introduce a simplified kinematic methodology for constructing Bennett linkages from quadrilaterals that contain a desired surface area and further leverage mechanically induced passive state switching. This architecture realizes a coordinated sweep-and-fold wing motion with a single actuation input, reducing weight and control complexity. A 3D-printed prototype is assembled and tested, demonstrating the intended spatial stroke and passive folding behavior.

</details>


### [12] [Vision-Language Navigation for Aerial Robots: Towards the Era of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07705)
*Xingyu Xia,Lekai Zhou,Yujie Tang,Xiaozhou Zhu,Hai Zhu,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对空中视觉语言导航领域进行了系统性综述，重点关注大语言模型和视觉语言模型的整合，提出了分类框架、评估了现有方法、识别了关键挑战，并指出了七个开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机技术和多模态人工智能的发展，空中视觉语言导航成为重要研究方向。本文旨在系统梳理该领域的研究现状，特别是LLM/VLM整合带来的新进展，为研究者提供全面的技术分析和未来方向指引。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法：1) 形式化定义Aerial VLN问题及两种交互范式；2) 将现有方法分为五类架构；3) 分析评估基础设施；4) 比较不同方法在共享基准上的表现；5) 识别关键架构权衡；6) 综合提出开放性问题。

Result: 建立了Aerial VLN方法的五类分类体系，系统分析了各类方法的设计原理和技术权衡，评估了现有数据集、仿真平台和指标的局限性，识别了离散vs连续动作、端到端vs分层设计等关键架构权衡。

Conclusion: Aerial VLN领域在LLM/VLM整合下取得显著进展，但仍面临七大开放性问题：长时指令接地、视点鲁棒性、可扩展空间表示、连续6-DoF动作执行、机载部署、基准标准化和多无人机集群导航，需要跨学科协作解决。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (Aerial VLN) aims to enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to interpret natural language instructions and autonomously navigate complex three-dimensional environments by grounding language in visual perception. This survey provides a critical and analytical review of the Aerial VLN field, with particular attention to the recent integration of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). We first formally introduce the Aerial VLN problem and define two interaction paradigms: single-instruction and dialog-based, as foundational axes. We then organize the body of Aerial VLN methods into a taxonomy of five architectural categories: sequence-to-sequence and attention-based methods, end-to-end LLM/VLM methods, hierarchical methods, multi-agent methods, and dialog-based navigation methods. For each category, we systematically analyze design rationales, technical trade-offs, and reported performance. We critically assess the evaluation infrastructure for Aerial VLN, including datasets, simulation platforms, and metrics, and identify their gaps in scale, environmental diversity, real-world grounding, and metric coverage. We consolidate cross-method comparisons on shared benchmarks and analyze key architectural trade-offs, including discrete versus continuous actions, end-to-end versus hierarchical designs, and the simulation-to-reality gap. Finally, we synthesize seven concrete open problems: long-horizon instruction grounding, viewpoint robustness, scalable spatial representation, continuous 6-DoF action execution, onboard deployment, benchmark standardization, and multi-UAV swarm navigation, with specific research directions grounded in the evidence presented throughout the survey.

</details>


### [13] [RoboAgent: Chaining Basic Capabilities for Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07774)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAgent：一种基于能力驱动的具身任务规划框架，通过调度器调用不同子能力分解复杂规划任务，使用单一VLM实现，无需外部工具，在多阶段训练下显著提升具身规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在多模态理解和推理方面表现出色，但在涉及多轮交互、长时程推理和扩展上下文分析的具身任务规划中性能有限。需要一种能够分解复杂规划、实现更透明可控推理过程的框架。

Method: 提出RoboAgent能力驱动规划流水线：1）调度器调用不同子能力，每个能力维护自身上下文；2）将复杂规划分解为VLM能更好处理的基本视觉语言问题序列；3）使用单一VLM实现调度器和所有能力；4）采用三阶段训练范式：行为克隆、DAgger训练、专家策略指导的强化学习；5）利用环境模拟器内部信息构建高质量监督，引入增强和合成数据提升多样性。

Result: 在广泛使用的具身任务规划基准测试中进行了大量实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。代码将在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: RoboAgent框架通过能力驱动的方法有效解决了VLM在具身规划中的局限性，实现了更透明可控的推理过程，为复杂具身任务规划提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on embodied task planning, where an agent acquires visual observations from the environment and executes atomic actions to accomplish a given task. Although recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results in multimodal understanding and reasoning, their performance remains limited when applied to embodied planning that involves multi-turn interaction, long-horizon reasoning, and extended context analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose RoboAgent, a capability-driven planning pipeline in which the model actively invokes different sub-capabilities. Each capability maintains its own context, and produces intermediate reasoning results or interacts with the environment according to the query given by a scheduler. This framework decomposes complex planning into a sequence of basic vision-language problems that VLMs can better address, enabling a more transparent and controllable reasoning process. The scheduler and all capabilities are implemented with a single VLM, without relying on external tools. To train this VLM, we adopt a multi-stage paradigm that consists of: (1) behavior cloning with expert plans, (2) DAgger training using trajectories collected by the model, and (3) reinforcement learning guided by an expert policy. Across these stages, we exploit the internal information of the environment simulator to construct high-quality supervision for each capability, and we further introduce augmented and synthetic data to enhance the model's performance in more diverse scenarios. Extensive experiments on widely used embodied task planning benchmarks validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Our codes will be available at https://github.com/woyut/RoboAgent_CVPR26.

</details>


### [14] [Learning Without Losing Identity: Capability Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07799)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向具身智能体的能力中心化进化范式，通过解耦智能体身份与能力进化，实现长期稳定、安全的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过修改智能体本身（提示工程、策略更新或结构重设计）来提升性能，导致长期系统中不稳定和身份丢失。需要一种既能持续改进又能保持智能体认知身份的方法。

Method: 引入具身能力模块（ECMs）作为模块化、版本化的具身功能单元，提出统一框架将能力进化与智能体身份解耦。能力通过任务执行、经验收集、模型精炼和模块更新的闭环过程进化，所有执行由运行时层管理以确保安全和策略约束。

Result: 在模拟具身任务中，能力进化在20次迭代后将任务成功率从32.4%提升至91.3%，优于智能体修改基线和现有技能学习方法（SPiRL、SkiMo），同时保持零策略漂移和零安全违规。

Conclusion: 将智能体身份与能力进化分离为长期具身智能提供了可扩展且安全的基础，支持持续能力改进而不损害系统稳定性或身份一致性。

Abstract: Embodied agents are expected to operate persistently in dynamic physical environments, continuously acquiring new capabilities over time. Existing approaches to improving agent performance often rely on modifying the agent itself -- through prompt engineering, policy updates, or structural redesign -- leading to instability and loss of identity in long-lived systems. In this work, we propose a capability-centric evolution paradigm for embodied agents. We argue that a robot should maintain a persistent agent as its cognitive identity, while enabling continuous improvement through the evolution of its capabilities. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Embodied Capability Modules (ECMs), which represent modular, versioned units of embodied functionality that can be learned, refined, and composed over time. We present a unified framework in which capability evolution is decoupled from agent identity. Capabilities evolve through a closed-loop process involving task execution, experience collection, model refinement, and module updating, while all executions are governed by a runtime layer that enforces safety and policy constraints. We demonstrate through simulated embodied tasks that capability evolution improves task success rates from 32.4% to 91.3% over 20 iterations, outperforming both agent-modification baselines and established skill-learning methods (SPiRL, SkiMo), while preserving zero policy drift and zero safety violations. Our results suggest that separating agent identity from capability evolution provides a scalable and safe foundation for long-term embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [15] [Harnessing Embodied Agents: Runtime Governance for Policy-Constrained Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07833)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于具身智能体的策略约束执行框架，将认知与执行监管分离，通过外部化运行时治理层实现策略检查、能力准入、执行监控、回滚处理和人工干预，显著提升动作可控性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身智能体从被动推理系统演变为与工具、机器人和物理环境交互的主动执行器，一旦授予执行权限，核心挑战就变成了如何在运行时保持动作的可治理性。现有方法将安全性和恢复逻辑嵌入到智能体循环内部，使得执行控制难以标准化、审计和适应。

Method: 提出策略约束执行框架，将智能体认知与执行监管分离。治理被外部化为专门的运行时层，执行策略检查、能力准入、执行监控、回滚处理和人工干预。形式化了具身智能体、具身能力模块和运行时治理层之间的控制边界。

Result: 通过1000次随机模拟试验验证了三个治理维度：96.2%的未授权动作被拦截；在运行时漂移情况下，不安全延续从100%降低到22.2%；91.4%的恢复成功率且完全符合策略要求。所有结果均显著优于基线（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 通过将运行时治理重新定义为系统级问题，本文确立了策略约束执行作为具身智能体系统关键设计原则的地位，为构建更安全、可控的具身智能系统提供了框架性解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents are evolving from passive reasoning systems into active executors that interact with tools, robots, and physical environments. Once granted execution authority, the central challenge becomes how to keep actions governable at runtime. Existing approaches embed safety and recovery logic inside the agent loop, making execution control difficult to standardize, audit, and adapt.
  This paper argues that embodied intelligence requires not only stronger agents, but stronger runtime governance. We propose a framework for policy-constrained execution that separates agent cognition from execution oversight. Governance is externalized into a dedicated runtime layer performing policy checking, capability admission, execution monitoring, rollback handling, and human override.
  We formalize the control boundary among the embodied agent, Embodied Capability Modules (ECMs), and runtime governance layer, and validate through 1000 randomized simulation trials across three governance dimensions. Results show 96.2% interception of unauthorized actions, reduction of unsafe continuation from 100% to 22.2% under runtime drift, and 91.4% recovery success with full policy compliance, substantially outperforming all baselines (p<0.001). By reframing runtime governance as a first-class systems problem, this paper positions policy-constrained execution as a key design principle for embodied agent systems.

</details>


### [16] [The Sustainability Gap in Robotics: A Large-Scale Survey of Sustainability Awareness in 50,000 Research Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07921)
*Antun Skuric,Leandro Von Werra,Thomas Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对arXiv cs.RO类别近5万篇论文的大规模调查显示，机器人学研究中的可持续性沟通和动机存在显著差距：尽管许多研究可映射到可持续发展目标相关领域，但明确提及可持续性影响的比例低于2%，SDG引用低于0.1%，可持续性动机论文低于5%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查机器人学研究领域中可持续性沟通和动机的现状，量化该领域在可持续发展目标（SDGs）方面的关注程度，揭示机器人学研究的潜在社会、生态和可持续性影响与实际研究意图之间的差距

Method: 对arXiv cs.RO类别2015年至2026年初发表的近50,000篇开放获取论文进行大规模调查分析，量化论文中提及社会、生态和可持续性影响的频率，分析其与联合国可持续发展目标（SDGs）的对齐程度

Result: 研究结果显示机器人学领域存在显著的可持续性沟通差距：可持续性相关影响提及率通常低于2%，明确SDG引用低于0.1%，可持续性动机论文比例低于5%。虽然大量机器人学论文可映射到SDG相关领域，但明确的可持续性动机仍然极低

Conclusion: 机器人学领域虽然快速发展，但可持续性尚未成为研究框架的标准组成部分。作者建议研究人员、会议和机构采取具体行动来弥合意识和动机差距，支持向更有意识和负责任的创新转变

Abstract: We present a large-scale survey of sustainability communication and motivation in robotics research. Our analysis covers nearly 50,000 open-access papers from arXiv's cs.RO category published between 2015 and early 2026. In this study, we quantify how often papers mention social, ecological, and sustainability impacts, and we analyse their alignment with the UN Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).
  The results reveal a persistent gap between the field's potential and its stated intent. While a large fraction of robotics papers can be mapped to SDG-relevant domains, explicit sustainability motivation remains remarkably low. Specifically, mentions of sustainability-related impacts are typically below 2%, explicit SDG references stay below 0.1%, and the proportion of sustainability-motivated papers remains below 5%. These trends suggest that while the field of robotics is advancing rapidly, sustainability is not yet a standard part of research framing.
  We conclude by proposing concrete actions for researchers, conferences, and institutions to close these awareness and motivation gaps, supporting a shift toward more intentional and responsible innovation.

</details>


### [17] [RAGE-XY: RADAR-Aided Longitudinal and Lateral Forces Estimation For Autonomous Race Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07939)
*Davide Malvezzi,Nicola Musiu,Eugenio Mascaro,Francesco Iacovacci,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGE-XY扩展了RAGE实时估计框架，通过在线雷达校准模块和三轮车模型，提高了车辆横向速度估计精度，并能估计后轮纵向轮胎力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始RAGE框架存在传感器未对准时的横向速度估计精度问题，且仅使用单轨模型限制了纵向力的估计能力。需要改进以同时准确估计车辆速度、轮胎滑移角和作用力。

Method: 1. 引入在线雷达校准模块，处理传感器未对准问题；2. 将基础车辆模型从单轨近似扩展到三轮车模型；3. 仅使用标准车载传感器（IMU和雷达）实现实时估计。

Result: 通过高保真仿真和EAV-24自动驾驶赛车真实实验验证，在横向和纵向车辆动力学估计方面表现出更高的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RAGE-XY框架通过在线校准和改进的车辆模型，显著提升了车辆状态估计性能，为自动驾驶系统提供了更可靠的动力学信息。

Abstract: In this work, we present RAGE-XY, an extended version of RAGE, a real-time estimation framework that simultaneously infers vehicle velocity, tire slip angles, and the forces acting on the vehicle using only standard onboard sensors such as IMUs and RADARs. Compared to the original formulation, the proposed method incorporates an online RADAR calibration module, improving the accuracy of lateral velocity estimation in the presence of sensor misalignment. Furthermore, we extend the underlying vehicle model from a single-track approximation to a tricycle model, enabling the estimation of rear longitudinal tire forces in addition to lateral dynamics. We validate the proposed approach through both high-fidelity simulations and real-world experiments conducted on the EAV-24 autonomous race car, demonstrating improved accuracy and robustness in estimating both lateral and longitudinal vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [18] [Incremental Residual Reinforcement Learning Toward Real-World Learning for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07945)
*Haruto Nagahisa,Kohei Matsumoto,Yuki Tomita,Yuki Hyodo,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IRRL方法，将增量学习与残差强化学习结合，解决移动机器人社会导航中计算资源受限和效率问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人社会导航需求增长，但模拟无法涵盖所有真实场景。真实世界RL面临边缘设备计算资源受限和学习效率低的挑战

Method: IRRL方法：结合增量学习（无需回放缓冲区或批量更新）和残差RL（仅训练相对于基础策略的残差）

Result: 仿真实验：IRRL性能与传统回放缓冲区方法相当，优于现有增量学习方法。真实实验：机器人能通过真实世界学习有效适应新环境

Conclusion: IRRL为解决移动机器人社会导航中的计算资源受限和学习效率问题提供了有效方案，支持在真实环境中直接学习适应

Abstract: As the demand for mobile robots continues to increase, social navigation has emerged as a critical task, driving active research into deep reinforcement learning (RL) approaches. However, because pedestrian dynamics and social conventions vary widely across different regions, simulations cannot easily encompass all possible real-world scenarios. Real-world RL, in which agents learn while operating directly in physical environments, presents a promising solution to this issue. Nevertheless, this approach faces significant challenges, particularly regarding constrained computational resources on edge devices and learning efficiency. In this study, we propose incremental residual RL (IRRL). This method integrates incremental learning, which is a lightweight process that operates without a replay buffer or batch updates, with residual RL, which enhances learning efficiency by training only on the residuals relative to a base policy. Through the simulation experiments, we demonstrated that, despite lacking a replay buffer, IRRL achieved performance comparable to those of conventional replay buffer-based methods and outperformed existing incremental learning approaches. Furthermore, the real-world experiments confirmed that IRRL can enable robots to effectively adapt to previously unseen environments through the real-world learning.

</details>


### [19] [HEX: Humanoid-Aligned Experts for Cross-Embodiment Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07993)
*Shuanghao Bai,Meng Li,Xinyuan Lv,Jiawei Wang,Xinhua Wang,Fei Liao,Chengkai Hou,Langzhe Gu,Wanqi Zhou,Kun Wu,Ziluo Ding,Zhiyuan Xu,Lei Sun,Shanghang Zhang,Zhengping Che,Jian Tang,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEX是一个面向双足人形机器人的状态中心化协调操控框架，通过统一状态表示、混合专家本体感知预测器和轻量历史标记等技术，实现全身协调控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过全身协调控制实现复杂操作，而现有视觉-语言-动作模型通常独立处理机器人身体部位，导致高自由度人形机器人控制困难且不稳定

Method: 1) 引入人形对齐的通用状态表示；2) 混合专家统一本体感知预测器建模全身协调和时序运动动态；3) 轻量历史标记总结过去观察；4) 残差门控融合机制和流匹配动作头整合视觉-语言线索与本体感知动态

Result: 在真实世界人形机器人操控任务中，HEX在任务成功率和泛化能力上达到最先进性能，特别是在快速反应和长时域场景中表现优异

Conclusion: HEX框架通过状态中心化设计和协调建模方法，有效解决了高自由度人形机器人的稳定控制问题，为复杂全身协调操控提供了可扩展的解决方案

Abstract: Humans achieve complex manipulation through coordinated whole-body control, whereas most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robot body parts largely independently, making high-DoF humanoid control challenging and often unstable. We present HEX, a state-centric framework for coordinated manipulation on full-sized bipedal humanoid robots. HEX introduces a humanoid-aligned universal state representation for scalable learning across heterogeneous embodiments, and incorporates a Mixture-of-Experts Unified Proprioceptive Predictor to model whole-body coordination and temporal motion dynamics from large-scale multi-embodiment trajectory data. To efficiently capture temporal visual context, HEX uses lightweight history tokens to summarize past observations, avoiding repeated encoding of historical images during inference. It further employs a residual-gated fusion mechanism with a flow-matching action head to adaptively integrate visual-language cues with proprioceptive dynamics for action generation. Experiments on real-world humanoid manipulation tasks show that HEX achieves state-of-the-art performance in task success rate and generalization, particularly in fast-reaction and long-horizon scenarios.

</details>


### [20] [AgiPIX: Bridging Simulation and Reality in Indoor Aerial Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08009)
*Sasanka Kuruppu Arachchige,Juan Jose Garcia,Changda Tian,Lauri Suomela,Panos Trahanias,Adriana Tapus,Joni-Kristian Kämäräinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agipix是一个开源的室内无人机自主平台，集成了紧凑的主动感知硬件、容器化软件栈和数字孪生仿真，支持仿真到实机的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏一个紧凑、主动感知、开源且能在仿真和真实世界操作中复现的室内自主飞行平台，用于关键资产检查。

Method: 设计了紧凑的硬件同步主动感知平台，配备机载GPU加速计算；开发了基于ROS 2的容器化模块化自主软件栈；创建了硬件平台的逼真数字孪生和可靠UI。

Result: 在工业室内环境中展示了基于机载感知的轨迹跟踪和探索性能，实现了容器化自主组件在仿真和真实飞行之间的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: Agipix平台填补了室内空中自主性和关键资产检查领域的空白，所有硬件设计、仿真资源和容器化软件均已开源发布。

Abstract: Autonomous indoor flight for critical asset inspection presents fundamental challenges in perception, planning, control, and learning. Despite rapid progress, there is still a lack of a compact, active-sensing, open-source platform that is reproducible across simulation and real-world operation. To address this gap, we present Agipix, a co-designed open hardware and software platform for indoor aerial autonomy and critical asset inspection. Agipix features a compact, hardware-synchronized active-sensing platform with onboard GPU-accelerated compute that is capable of agile flight; a containerized ROS~2-based modular autonomy stack; and a photorealistic digital twin of the hardware platform together with a reliable UI. These elements enable rapid iteration via zero-shot transfer of containerized autonomy components between simulation and real flights. We demonstrate trajectory tracking and exploration performance using onboard sensing in industrial indoor environments. All hardware designs, simulation assets, and containerized software are released openly together with documentation.

</details>


### [21] [Open-Ended Instruction Realization with LLM-Enabled Multi-Planner Scheduling in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08031)
*Jiawei Liu,Xun Gong,Fen Fang,Muli Yang,Bohao Qu,Yunfeng Hu,Hong Chen,Xulei Yang,Qing Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的指令实现框架，将乘客自然语言指令转化为自动驾驶控制信号，通过调度多个MPC运动规划器实现透明可追溯的决策链


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HMI研究忽视乘客在自动驾驶中的操控需求，自然语言作为直观接口面临挑战：如何将开放式指令转化为控制信号而不牺牲可解释性和可追溯性

Method: 提出指令实现框架：1) 使用LLM解释指令；2) 生成可执行脚本调度多个基于MPC的运动规划器；3) 将规划轨迹转化为控制信号；采用调度中心设计，在不同时间尺度解耦语义推理与车辆控制

Result: 框架显著提高任务完成率，降低LLM查询成本，安全性与合规性达到专业AD方法水平，对LLM推理延迟具有较高容忍度；建立了闭环环境下的开放式指令实现基准

Conclusion: 该框架为自动驾驶中自然语言指令实现提供了透明、可追溯的解决方案，平衡了语义理解与实时控制需求，为HMI研究提供了新方向

Abstract: Most Human-Machine Interaction (HMI) research overlooks the maneuvering needs of passengers in autonomous driving (AD). Natural language offers an intuitive interface, yet translating passenger open-ended instructions into control signals, without sacrificing interpretability and traceability, remains a challenge. This study proposes an instruction-realization framework that leverages a large language model (LLM) to interpret instructions, generates executable scripts that schedule multiple model predictive control (MPC)-based motion planners based on real-time feedback, and converts planned trajectories into control signals. This scheduling-centric design decouples semantic reasoning from vehicle control at different timescales, establishing a transparent, traceable decision-making chain from high-level instructions to low-level actions. Due to the absence of high-fidelity evaluation tools, this study introduces a benchmark for open-ended instruction realization in a closed-loop setting. Comprehensive experiments reveal that the framework significantly improves task-completion rates over instruction-realization baselines, reduces LLM query costs, achieves safety and compliance on par with specialized AD approaches, and exhibits considerable tolerance to LLM inference latency. For more qualitative illustrations and a clearer understanding.

</details>


### [22] [Governed Capability Evolution for Embodied Agents: Safe Upgrade, Compatibility Checking, and Runtime Rollback for Embodied Capability Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08059)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个面向具身智能体的治理能力演化系统框架，通过生命周期感知的升级流程确保能力模块安全部署，避免破坏策略约束和执行假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身智能体需要通过更新可执行能力而非重写整个系统来持续改进，但能力模块演化后的安全部署问题尚未得到充分研究。一旦能力模块演化为新版本，如何在不破坏策略约束、执行假设或恢复保证的情况下安全部署成为关键系统问题。

Method: 提出生命周期感知的升级框架，将每个新能力版本视为治理部署候选而非立即可执行的替换。框架引入四种升级兼容性检查（接口、策略、行为、恢复），并组织为分阶段的运行时管道，包括候选验证、沙盒评估、影子部署、门控激活、在线监控和回滚。

Result: 在6轮能力升级和15个随机种子的评估中：朴素升级实现72.9%任务成功率，但最终轮次不安全激活率达到60%；治理升级保持可比较的成功率（67.4%），同时在所有轮次中维持零不安全激活（Wilcoxon p=0.003）。影子部署揭示了40%仅靠沙盒评估无法发现的回归问题，回滚在79.8%的激活后漂移场景中成功。

Conclusion: 治理能力演化应作为具身智能体的首要系统问题，提出的生命周期感知升级框架能够有效确保能力模块的安全部署，通过分阶段验证和监控机制显著降低不安全激活风险，同时保持系统性能。

Abstract: Embodied agents are increasingly expected to improve over time by updating their executable capabilities rather than rewriting the agent itself. Prior work has separately studied modular capability packaging, capability evolution, and runtime governance. However, a key systems problem remains underexplored: once an embodied capability module evolves into a new version, how can the hosting system deploy it safely without breaking policy constraints, execution assumptions, or recovery guarantees?
  We formulate governed capability evolution as a first-class systems problem for embodied agents. We propose a lifecycle-aware upgrade framework in which every new capability version is treated as a governed deployment candidate rather than an immediately executable replacement. The framework introduces four upgrade compatibility checks -- interface, policy, behavioral, and recovery -- and organizes them into a staged runtime pipeline comprising candidate validation, sandbox evaluation, shadow deployment, gated activation, online monitoring, and rollback.
  We evaluate over 6 rounds of capability upgrade with 15 random seeds. Naive upgrade achieves 72.9% task success but drives unsafe activation to 60% by the final round; governed upgrade retains comparable success (67.4%) while maintaining zero unsafe activations across all rounds (Wilcoxon p=0.003). Shadow deployment reveals 40% of regressions invisible to sandbox evaluation alone, and rollback succeeds in 79.8% of post-activation drift scenarios.

</details>


### [23] [ViVa: A Video-Generative Value Model for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08168)
*Jindi Lv,Hao Li,Jie Li,Yifei Nie,Fankun Kong,Yang Wang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Chaojun Ni,Qiuping Deng,Hengtao Li,Jiancheng Lv,Guan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViVa是一个视频生成价值模型，通过重新利用预训练视频生成器进行价值估计，将价值评估建立在预期的具身动力学基础上，在真实世界机器人操作任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的价值模型难以捕捉时间动态，在长视野任务中价值估计不可靠。视觉语言动作（VLA）模型虽然通过大规模预训练推进了机器人操作，但部分可观测性和延迟反馈使真实世界部署面临挑战。

Method: 提出ViVa模型，重新利用预训练视频生成器进行价值估计。以当前观测和机器人本体感知为输入，联合预测未来本体感知和当前状态的标量价值。通过利用预训练视频生成器的时空先验，将价值估计建立在预期的具身动力学基础上。

Result: ViVa集成到RECAP中，在真实世界盒子组装任务上带来显著改进。所有三个任务的定性分析确认ViVa产生更可靠的价值信号，准确反映任务进展。通过利用视频语料库的时空先验，ViVa还能泛化到新物体。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型在价值估计方面具有前景，ViVa通过将价值与前瞻性内在耦合，超越了静态快照方法，为机器人操作任务提供了更可靠的价值评估框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robot manipulation through large-scale pretraining, but real-world deployment remains challenging due to partial observability and delayed feedback. Reinforcement learning addresses this via value functions, which assess task progress and guide policy improvement. However, existing value models built on vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to capture temporal dynamics, undermining reliable value estimation in long-horizon tasks. In this paper, we propose ViVa, a video-generative value model that repurposes a pretrained video generator for value estimation. Taking the current observation and robot proprioception as input, ViVa jointly predicts future proprioception and a scalar value for the current state. By leveraging the spatiotemporal priors of a pretrained video generator, our approach grounds value estimation in anticipated embodiment dynamics, moving beyond static snapshots to intrinsically couple value with foresight. Integrated into RECAP, ViVa delivers substantial improvements on real-world box assembly. Qualitative analysis across all three tasks confirms that ViVa produces more reliable value signals, accurately reflecting task progress. By leveraging spatiotemporal priors from video corpora, ViVa also generalizes to novel objects, highlighting the promise of video-generative models for value estimation.

</details>


### [24] [State and Trajectory Estimation of Tensegrity Robots via Factor Graphs and Chebyshev Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08185)
*Edgar Granados,Patrick Meng,Charles Tang,Shrimed Sangani,William R. Johnson,Rebecca Kramer-Bottiglio,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于因子图的两阶段方法，用于张拉整体机器人的鲁棒状态/轨迹估计，融合RGB-D相机和缆绳长度传感器数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体机器人具有柔顺性和适应性，但其非线性、欠约束动力学使状态估计具有挑战性。可靠的连续时间刚性连杆估计对闭环控制、系统辨识和机器学习至关重要，而传统方法往往不足

Method: 提出两阶段方法：1) 基于因子图的在线状态估计，融合RGB-D相机和缆绳长度传感器数据；2) 使用马氏距离聚类算法处理噪声，切比雪夫多项式方法估计最可能速度和中间状态

Result: 与基于ICP的算法相比，该方法在模拟和真实数据上表现良好，能够为复杂张拉整体机器人运动提供高保真度的连续时间状态和轨迹估计

Conclusion: 因子图方法在张拉整体机器人状态估计领域具有创新性，利用机器人结构特性提供有效的传感器融合解决方案，能够实际处理非线性问题

Abstract: Tensegrity robots offer compliance and adaptability, but their nonlinear, and underconstrained dynamics make state estimation challenging. Reliable continuous-time estimation of all rigid links is crucial for closed-loop control, system identification, and machine learning; however, conventional methods often fall short. This paper proposes a two-stage approach for robust state or trajectory estimation (i.e., filtering or smoothing) of a cable-driven tensegrity robot. For online state estimation, this work introduces a factor-graph-based method, which fuses measurements from an RGB-D camera with on-board cable length sensors. To the best of the authors' knowledge, this is the first application of factor graphs in this domain. Factor graphs are a natural choice, as they exploit the robot's structural properties and provide effective sensor fusion solutions capable of handling nonlinearities in practice. Both the Mahalanobis distance-based clustering algorithm, used to handle noise, and the Chebyshev polynomial method, used to estimate the most probable velocities and intermediate states, are shown to perform well on simulated and real-world data, compared to an ICP-based algorithm. Results show that the approach provides high fidelity, continuous-time state and trajectory estimates for complex tensegrity robot motions.

</details>


### [25] [EvoGymCM: Harnessing Continuous Material Stiffness for Soft Robot Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08258)
*Le Shen,Kangyao Huang,Wentao Zhao,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoGymCM：一个将连续材料刚度作为与形态和控制并列的一类设计变量的软机器人协同设计基准套件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软机器人协同设计平台（如EvoGym）将材料维度严格离散化，人为限制了设计空间和性能。需要建立连续材料刚度作为一类设计变量，以充分释放软机器人的物理潜力。

Method: 提出EvoGymCM基准套件，包含两种设置：EvoGymCM-R（反应性，针对可编程材料）和EvoGymCM-I（不变性，针对传统材料）。相应提出两种形态-材料-控制协同设计范式：反应性材料协同设计和不变性材料协同设计。

Result: 系统实验表明，连续材料优化能提升性能，并在形态、材料和控制之间解锁协同效应。

Conclusion: 连续材料刚度作为一类设计变量对于软机器人协同设计至关重要，EvoGymCM为这一方向提供了标准化基准，促进了形态、材料和控制的协同优化。

Abstract: In the automated co-design of soft robots, precisely adapting the material stiffness field to task environments is crucial for unlocking their full physical potential. However, mainstream platforms (e.g., EvoGym) strictly discretize the material dimension, artificially restricting the design space and performance of soft robots. To address this, we propose EvoGymCM (EvoGym with Continuous Materials), a benchmark suite formally establishing continuous material stiffness as a first-class design variable alongside morphology and control. Aligning with real-world material mechanisms, EvoGymCM introduces two settings: (i) EvoGymCM-R (Reactive), motivated by programmable materials with dynamically tunable stiffness; and (ii) EvoGymCM-I (Invariant), motivated by traditional materials with invariant stiffness fields. To tackle the resulting high-dimensional coupling, we formulate two Morphology-Material-Control co-design paradigms: (i) Reactive-Material Co-Design, which learns real-time stiffness tuning policies to guide programmable materials; and (ii) Invariant-Material Co-Design, which jointly optimizes morphology and fixed material fields to guide traditional material fabrication. Systematic experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate that continuous material optimization boosts performance and unlocks synergy across morphology, material, and control.

</details>


### [26] [EMMa: End-Effector Stability-Oriented Mobile Manipulation for Tracked Rescue Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08292)
*Yifei Wang,Hao Zhang,Jidong Huang,Shuohang Fang,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于履带式移动机械臂的协调运动生成框架，通过耦合末端执行器与移动基座状态的路径优化模型和隔离控制方案，在复杂救援场景中实现稳定的末端执行器操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在规划和控制层面忽视了末端执行器的多种运动特性，而救援任务中的履带式移动机械臂自主操作不仅需要确保运动可达性和安全性，还需在不同任务需求下维持稳定的末端执行器操作。

Method: 1) 构建协调路径优化模型，耦合末端执行器与移动基座状态；2) 设计紧凑的成本/约束表示以减轻非线性并降低计算复杂度；3) 开发具有前馈补偿和反馈调节的隔离控制方案，实现机器人协调路径跟踪。

Result: 在救援场景的大量仿真和真实世界实验中，所提框架在任务成功率和末端执行器运动稳定性等关键指标上始终优于最先进方法，验证了其在复杂移动操作任务中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该运动生成框架通过协调路径优化和隔离控制，显著提升了履带式移动机械臂在复杂救援场景中的末端执行器操作稳定性，为自主移动操作提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The autonomous operation of tracked mobile manipulators in rescue missions requires not only ensuring the reachability and safety of robot motion but also maintaining stable end-effector manipulation under diverse task demands. However, existing studies have overlooked many end-effector motion properties at both the planning and control levels. This paper presents a motion generation framework for tracked mobile manipulators to achieve stable end-effector operation in complex rescue scenarios. The framework formulates a coordinated path optimization model that couples end-effector and mobile base states and designs compact cost/constraint representations to mitigate nonlinearities and reduce computational complexity. Furthermore, an isolated control scheme with feedforward compensation and feedback regulation is developed to enable coordinated path tracking for the robot. Extensive simulated and real-world experiments on rescue scenarios demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms SOTA methods across key metrics, including task success rate and end-effector motion stability, validating its effectiveness and robustness in complex mobile manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [27] [A Unified Multi-Layer Framework for Skill Acquisition from Imperfect Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08341)
*Zi-Qi Yang,Mehrdad R. Kermani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种三层控制框架，通过基于通用机器人柔顺性的学习示教方法，实现高效、直观且安全的技能教学人机交互系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机交互技能教学系统存在碎片化问题，缺乏同时具备高效性、直观性和通用安全性的统一框架。现有方法无法提供既高效又直观且普遍安全的解决方案。

Method: 提出三层渐进式控制框架：1) 实时学习示教方法，从单次演示中学习轨迹和可变阻抗；2) 零空间优化策略，主动管理奇异性并提供一致的触觉教学体验；3) 基础零空间柔顺方法，使整个机器人身体能够柔顺适应外部交互而不影响主要任务性能。

Result: 在7自由度KUKA LWR机器人上进行了全面对比实验验证。结果表明，该系统为广泛的人机协作任务提供了更安全、更直观、更高效的一体化解决方案。

Conclusion: 该框架通过三层渐进式设计，解决了人机交互技能教学中的碎片化问题，实现了从末端执行器特定应用到通用人机交互平台的转变，为广泛的人机协作任务提供了统一、安全、直观且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Current Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) systems for skill teaching are fragmented, and existing approaches in the literature do not offer a cohesive framework that is simultaneously efficient, intuitive, and universally safe. This paper presents a novel, layered control framework that addresses this fundamental gap by enabling robust, compliant Learning from Demonstration (LfD) built upon a foundation of universal robot compliance. The proposed approach is structured in three progressive and interconnected stages. First, we introduce a real-time LfD method that learns both the trajectory and variable impedance from a single demonstration, significantly improving efficiency and reproduction fidelity. To ensure high-quality and intuitive {kinesthetic teaching}, we then present a null-space optimization strategy that proactively manages singularities and provides a consistent interaction feel during human demonstration. Finally, to ensure generalized safety, we introduce a foundational null-space compliance method that enables the entire robot body to compliantly adapt to post-learning external interactions without compromising main task performance. This final contribution transforms the system into a versatile HRI platform, moving beyond end-effector (EE)-specific applications. We validate the complete framework through comprehensive comparative experiments on a 7-DOF KUKA LWR robot. The results demonstrate a safer, more intuitive, and more efficient unified system for a wide range of human-robot collaborative tasks.

</details>


### [28] [Sumo: Dynamic and Generalizable Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08508)
*John Z. Zhang,Maks Sorokin,Jan Brüdigam,Brandon Hung,Stephen Phillips,Dmitry Yershov,Farzad Niroui,Tong Zhao,Leonor Fermoselle,Xinghao Zhu,Chao Cao,Duy Ta,Tao Pang,Jiuguang Wang,Preston Culbertson,Zachary Manchester,Simon Le Cléac'h*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种sim-to-real方法，通过测试时引导预训练全身控制策略与采样规划器结合，使腿式机器人能够动态操控大型重物


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决腿式机器人动态操控大型重物时面临的挑战，传统方法难以处理超出机器人标称负载能力或尺寸的物体

Method: 结合预训练全身控制策略与采样规划器进行测试时引导，无需额外调参或训练即可泛化到多种物体和任务，支持测试时灵活调整成本函数

Result: 在真实Spot四足机器人上成功完成多项挑战性任务，包括扶正超过机器人标称举重能力的轮胎、拖动比机器人更大更高的防暴屏障；在仿真中可泛化到人形机器人开门、推桌等任务

Conclusion: 该方法为腿式机器人动态操控大型重物提供了一种有效的sim-to-real解决方案，具有良好泛化能力和灵活性

Abstract: This paper presents a sim-to-real approach that enables legged robots to dynamically manipulate large and heavy objects with whole-body dexterity. Our key insight is that by performing test-time steering of a pre-trained whole-body control policy with a sample-based planner, we can enable these robots to solve a variety of dynamic loco-manipulation tasks. Interestingly, we find our method generalizes to a diverse set of objects and tasks with no additional tuning or training, and can be further enhanced by flexibly adjusting the cost function at test time. We demonstrate the capabilities of our approach through a variety of challenging loco-manipulation tasks on a Spot quadruped robot in the real world, including uprighting a tire heavier than the robot's nominal lifting capacity and dragging a crowd-control barrier larger and taller than the robot itself. Additionally, we show that the same approach can be generalized to humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, such as opening a door and pushing a table, in simulation. Project code and videos are available at \href{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}.

</details>


### [29] [ActiveGlasses: Learning Manipulation with Active Vision from Ego-centric Human Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08534)
*Yanwen Zou,Chenyang Shi,Wenye Yu,Han Xue,Jun Lv,Ye Pan,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveGlasses：一种通过智能眼镜采集人类第一人称视角演示来学习机器人操作的系统，实现零样本迁移和主动视觉


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据采集系统通常依赖手持设备，增加了操作负担、限制了可扩展性，且难以捕捉人类日常交互中自然协调的感知-操作行为。需要更自然的系统来忠实记录人类操作和感知行为，并实现到机器人平台的零样本迁移。

Method: 使用安装在智能眼镜上的立体相机作为唯一感知设备：操作员演示时佩戴，部署时同一相机安装在6自由度感知臂上以重现人类主动视觉。从演示中提取物体轨迹，使用以物体为中心的点云策略联合预测操作和头部运动。

Result: 在涉及遮挡和精确交互的多个挑战性任务中，ActiveGlasses实现了带主动视觉的零样本迁移，在相同硬件设置下始终优于强基线，并在两个机器人平台上展现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: ActiveGlasses提供了一种更自然、可扩展的机器人操作学习系统，通过第一人称视角演示和主动视觉实现了零样本迁移，为大规模真实世界机器人数据采集提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large-scale real-world robot data collection is a prerequisite for bringing robots into everyday deployment. However, existing pipelines often rely on specialized handheld devices to bridge the embodiment gap, which not only increases operator burden and limits scalability, but also makes it difficult to capture the naturally coordinated perception-manipulation behaviors of human daily interaction. This challenge calls for a more natural system that can faithfully capture human manipulation and perception behaviors while enabling zero-shot transfer to robotic platforms. We introduce ActiveGlasses, a system for learning robot manipulation from ego-centric human demonstrations with active vision. A stereo camera mounted on smart glasses serves as the sole perception device for both data collection and policy inference: the operator wears it during bare-hand demonstrations, and the same camera is mounted on a 6-DoF perception arm during deployment to reproduce human active vision. To enable zero-transfer, we extract object trajectories from demonstrations and use an object-centric point-cloud policy to jointly predict manipulation and head movement. Across several challenging tasks involving occlusion and precise interaction, ActiveGlasses achieves zero-shot transfer with active vision, consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same hardware setup, and generalizes across two robot platforms.

</details>


### [30] [Fail2Drive: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Driving Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08535)
*Simon Gerstenecker,Andreas Geiger,Katrin Renz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fail2Drive是首个用于CARLA中闭环自动驾驶泛化评估的配对路线基准，包含200条路线和17种新场景类别，通过隔离分布偏移效应实现定量诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶基准测试通常在测试时重复使用训练场景，导致成功可能反映的是记忆而非鲁棒驾驶行为，无法真正衡量分布偏移下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出Fail2Drive基准：1) 包含200条路线和17种新场景类别，涵盖外观、布局、行为和鲁棒性偏移；2) 每个偏移路线都匹配一个分布内对应路线，隔离偏移效应；3) 提供开源工具箱用于创建新场景并通过特权专家策略验证可解性。

Result: 评估多个最先进模型显示一致性能下降，平均成功率下降22.8%。分析揭示了意外失败模式，如忽略LiDAR中清晰可见的物体，以及未能学习自由空间和占用空间的基本概念。

Conclusion: Fail2Drive为基准测试和改进闭环驾驶泛化能力建立了可复现的基础，通过开源代码、数据和工具加速后续研究。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a central bottleneck for closed-loop autonomous driving. Although simulators like CARLA enable safe and scalable testing, existing benchmarks rarely measure true generalization: they typically reuse training scenarios at test time. Success can therefore reflect memorization rather than robust driving behavior. We introduce Fail2Drive, the first paired-route benchmark for closed-loop generalization in CARLA, with 200 routes and 17 new scenario classes spanning appearance, layout, behavioral, and robustness shifts. Each shifted route is matched with an in-distribution counterpart, isolating the effect of the shift and turning qualitative failures into quantitative diagnostics. Evaluating multiple state-of-the-art models reveals consistent degradation, with an average success-rate drop of 22.8\%. Our analysis uncovers unexpected failure modes, such as ignoring objects clearly visible in the LiDAR and failing to learn the fundamental concepts of free and occupied space. To accelerate follow-up work, Fail2Drive includes an open-source toolbox for creating new scenarios and validating solvability via a privileged expert policy. Together, these components establish a reproducible foundation for benchmarking and improving closed-loop driving generalization. We open-source all code, data, and tools at https://github.com/autonomousvision/fail2drive .

</details>


### [31] [SIM1: Physics-Aligned Simulator as Zero-Shot Data Scaler in Deformable Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08544)
*Yunsong Zhou,Hangxu Liu,Xuekun Jiang,Xing Shen,Yuanzhen Zhou,Hui Wang,Baole Fang,Yang Tian,Mulin Yu,Qiaojun Yu,Li Ma,Hengjie Li,Hanqing Wang,Jia Zeng,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIM1是一个物理对齐的真实-仿真-真实数据引擎，通过将仿真建立在物理世界基础上，将稀疏演示转化为具有接近演示保真度的规模化合成监督，实现可变形物体操作的数据高效策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作在具身学习中是一个数据密集型领域，其形状、接触和拓扑的共同演化远超刚性物体的变异性。虽然仿真有望减轻真实世界数据采集的成本，但现有的仿真到真实流水线仍基于刚体抽象，导致几何不匹配、软动力学脆弱以及不适合布料交互的运动基元。作者认为仿真失败的原因不在于其合成性，而在于其缺乏物理基础。

Method: SIM1是一个物理对齐的真实-仿真-真实数据引擎。给定有限演示，系统将场景数字化为度量一致的双胞胎，通过弹性建模校准可变形动力学，并通过基于扩散的轨迹生成和质量过滤扩展行为。该流水线将稀疏观察转化为具有接近演示保真度的规模化合成监督。

Result: 在纯合成数据上训练的策略在1:15的等价比下达到与真实数据基线相当的性能，同时在真实世界部署中实现90%的零样本成功率和50%的泛化增益。这些结果验证了物理对齐仿真作为可变形操作的可扩展监督以及数据高效策略学习的实用途径。

Conclusion: 物理对齐的仿真为可变形物体操作提供了可扩展的监督方法，是实现数据高效策略学习的实用途径。通过将仿真建立在物理世界基础上，SIM1能够将稀疏演示转化为高质量的合成数据，显著减少对真实世界数据的需求。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with deformable objects represents a data-intensive regime in embodied learning, where shape, contact, and topology co-evolve in ways that far exceed the variability of rigids. Although simulation promises relief from the cost of real-world data acquisition, prevailing sim-to-real pipelines remain rooted in rigid-body abstractions, producing mismatched geometry, fragile soft dynamics, and motion primitives poorly suited for cloth interaction. We posit that simulation fails not for being synthetic, but for being ungrounded. To address this, we introduce SIM1, a physics-aligned real-to-sim-to-real data engine that grounds simulation in the physical world. Given limited demonstrations, the system digitizes scenes into metric-consistent twins, calibrates deformable dynamics through elastic modeling, and expands behaviors via diffusion-based trajectory generation with quality filtering. This pipeline transforms sparse observations into scaled synthetic supervision with near-demonstration fidelity. Experiments show that policies trained on purely synthetic data achieve parity with real-data baselines at a 1:15 equivalence ratio, while delivering 90% zero-shot success and 50% generalization gains in real-world deployment. These results validate physics-aligned simulation as scalable supervision for deformable manipulation and a practical pathway for data-efficient policy learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [32] [FR3 for 6G Networks: A Comparative Study against FR1 and FR2 Across Diverse Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07482)
*Fahimeh Aghaei,Mehdi Monemi,Mehdi Rasti,Murat Uysal*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: FR3（7.125-24.25 GHz）作为6G和C-V2B通信的潜力频段，在覆盖和容量间提供平衡。通过射线追踪建模，研究发现FR3在同等孔径下比毫米波（FR2）为边缘用户提供更高数据率，且从车载到手持终端的切换对覆盖概率影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G发展和无线容量需求增长，FR3（上中频段）因其在毫米波大带宽和sub-6 GHz良好传播特性间的平衡而成为有前景的频谱候选。该频段有望同时提升覆盖和容量，特别是对6G系统和C-V2B通信。然而，实现这一潜力需要在多样化城市环境中进行准确、现实的信道建模。

Method: 采用射线追踪工具来表征下行链路传播，并为可靠的C-V2B链路进行详细信道建模。评估了FR1（sub-6 GHz）、FR3和FR2（毫米波）频段在不同城市环境（郊区、城市、高层城市）下的数据率性能，使用针对不同环境设计的天线阵列配置。还将单手握持行人终端模型集成到射线追踪器中，分析从车载到行人终端的切换影响。

Result: 在同等孔径尺寸下，FR3在无干扰和全干扰场景下均比FR2为小区边缘用户提供更高数据率，表明毫米波的额外阵列增益不足以完全补偿其经历的严重路径损耗。从车载到行人终端的切换在所有频段对覆盖概率影响很小（约1%-3%），其中FR3（特别是8.2 GHz）的差异最小。

Conclusion: FR3频段在6G和C-V2B通信中展现出显著优势，在覆盖和容量间提供良好平衡。其相对于毫米波频段在边缘用户性能上的优越性，以及终端类型切换对覆盖影响小的特性，使其成为未来无线系统的有力候选频段。

Abstract: Motivated by increasing wireless capacity demands and 6G advancements, the newly defined Frequency Range 3 (FR3, 7.125-24.25 GHz), also known as the upper mid-band, has emerged as a promising spectrum candidate. It offers a balance between the large bandwidth potential of millimeter-wave bands and the favorable propagation characteristics of sub-6 GHz bands. As a result, the upper mid-band presents a strong opportunity to enhance both coverage and capacity, particularly for 6G systems and Cellular Vehicle-to-Base Station (C-V2B) communications. Harnessing this potential, however, requires addressing key technical challenges through accurate and realistic channel modeling across diverse urban environments, including Suburban, Urban, and HighRise Urban scenarios. To this end, we employ a ray-tracing tool to characterize downlink propagation and enable detailed channel modeling for reliable C-V2B links. We evaluate data rate performance across FR1 (sub-6 GHz), FR3, and FR2 (mmWave) bands using antenna array configurations designed for different urban environments. The results show that, under equal aperture sizes, FR3 achieves higher data rates than FR2 for cell-edge User Equipment (UEs) in both interference-free and full-interference scenarios, indicating that the additional array gain at mmWave is insufficient to fully compensate for the severe experienced path loss. Integrating one-hand-grip pedestrian UEs model into ray tracer shows that transitioning from vehicular to pedestrian UEs results in negligible differences in coverage probability (about 1\%--3\%) across all frequencies, with the minimum differences observed in FR3, particularly at 8.2 GHz.

</details>


### [33] [Energy-Efficient Drone Logistics for Last-Mile Delivery: Implications of Payload-Dependent Routing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07514)
*Ziyue Li,Qianwen,Guo,Paul Schonfeld*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 绿色无人机路径规划问题研究：与传统距离最小化路径不同，考虑动态载荷的能源效率优化能带来反直觉的路径策略，实现显著的能源节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机配送作为最后一公里配送的经济高效替代方案正在兴起。与传统地面车辆不同，无人机的能源消耗不仅取决于行驶距离，还取决于其有效载荷。这为多站点配送带来了独特的环境挑战，因为无人机的总重量（进而其能源消耗率）在每次配送后动态变化。因此需要研究如何最大化能源效率的绿色无人机路径规划问题。

Method: 通过一系列动机示例和数值实验，研究能源感知路径规划策略。对比传统距离最小化路径，分析能源效率优化的反直觉路径策略，包括：更长路径可能消耗更少能源；单独的单客户配送可能优于多站点配送；异构无人机机队（不同尺寸无人机）通过匹配无人机容量与特定配送需求可实现更高效率。

Result: 数值研究表明，绿色路径策略在67%的实例中实现了能源节约。在这些案例中，与最小距离路径相比，平均能源节约为2.17%，最大节约达到5.97%。研究发现了三种反直觉的路径策略：1）更长路径可能消耗更少能源；2）单独单客户配送可能优于多站点配送；3）异构无人机机队通过容量匹配可实现更高效率。

Conclusion: 绿色无人机路径规划策略能够显著提高最后一公里配送的可持续性。与传统距离最小化路径相比，考虑动态载荷变化的能源效率优化能够带来反直觉但更高效的路径策略，实现可观的能源节约。这些发现强调了在无人机配送中采用绿色路径规划策略的潜力。

Abstract: Drone delivery is rapidly emerging as a cost-effective and energy efficient alternative for last-mile delivery. Unlike ground vehicles, a drone's energy consumption depends on its payload in addition to travel distance. This creates a unique environmental challenge for multi-stop delivery tours, as the drone's total weight, and therefore its energy consumption rate, dynamically changes after each delivery. This paper investigates a novel green drone routing problem focused on maximizing energy efficiency. Through a series of motivating examples and numerical experiments, we demonstrate that energy-aware routing leads to several counter-intuitive routing strategies that contradict traditional distance-minimization delivery: a longer route may actually consume less energy than a shorter one; separate single-customer tours can be superior to a multi-stop tour; and a heterogeneous fleet, with drones of varying sizes, can achieve greater efficiency by matching drone capacity to specific delivery demands. In the numerical study, the green routing strategy shows energy savings in 67% of the instances. For these cases, the average energy saving is 2.17%, with a maximum saving of 5.97%, compared to minimum distance routing. These findings highlight the potential for green drone routing strategies to improve the sustainability of last-mile delivery.

</details>


### [34] [Analytical Modeling of Dispersive Closed-loop MC Channels with Pulsatile Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08307)
*Theofilos Symeonidis,Fardad Vakilipoor,Robert Schober,Nunzio Tuccitto,Maximilian Schäfer*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个考虑脉动血流的时间变异性一维通道模型，用于分析人体心血管系统中的分子通信系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子通信模型大多假设恒定流速，而人体心血管系统中的血流本质上是脉动性的，需要更准确的模型来反映生理环境的实际特性

Method: 建立时间变异性一维通道模型，推导脉动流条件下闭环分子通信系统的信道冲激响应解析表达式，该响应遵循具有时变均值和方差的包裹正态分布

Result: 模型揭示了信道的循环平稳特性，量化了脉动对时间浓度分布的影响，并通过三维粒子模拟验证了模型的有效性，显示出良好的一致性

Conclusion: 提出的脉动流模型为人体内分子通信系统提供了更准确的表征工具，能够高效分析心血管环境中的分子传输特性

Abstract: Molecular communication (MC) is a communication paradigm in which information is conveyed through the controlled release, propagation, and reception of molecules. Many envisioned healthcare applications of MC are expected to operate inside the human body. In this environment, the cardiovascular system ( CVS) acts as the physical channel, which forms a closed-loop network where particle transport is mainly governed by the combined effects of diffusion and flow. Despite the fact that physiological flows in many parts of the human body are inherently pulsatile due to the cardiac cycle, most existing models for dispersive closed-loop MC channels assume a constant flow velocity. In this paper, we present a time-variant one-dimensional (1D ) channel model for dispersive closed-loop MC systems with pulsatile flow. We derive an analytical expression for the channel impulse response (CIR ), which follows a wrapped Normal distribution with time-variant mean and variance. The obtained model reveals the cyclostationary nature of the channel and quantifies the influence of pulsation on the temporal concentration profile compared to steady-flow systems. Finally, the model is validated by three-dimensional ( 3D ) particle-based simulations (PBS s), showing excellent agreement and enabling an efficient analytical characterization of the channel.

</details>

### [35] [Formally Guaranteed Control Adaptation for ODD-Resilient Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07414)
*Gricel Vázquez,Calum Imrie,Sepeedeh Shahbeigi,Nawshin Mannan Proma,Tian Gan,Victoria J Hodge,John Molloy,Simos Gerasimou*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应概率系统模型方法，用于处理超出设计域（ODD）的场景，同时提供定量保证，提高自主系统在意外情况下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保自主系统在超出操作设计域（ODD）的情况下仍能保持可靠性能是构建弹性自主系统的主要挑战。当前系统在面对未预见场景时缺乏有效的适应机制和形式化保证。

Method: 引入一种自适应方法，动态扩展现有系统态势能力的覆盖范围。该方法基于概率系统模型，支持在未预见情况下对系统行为进行验证和适应，同时提供定量保证。

Result: 初步结果表明，该方法能有效提高系统可靠性，即使在未预见的超出ODD情况下，也能通过自适应行为调整和提供形式化保证来维持系统性能。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应概率系统模型方法为解决自主系统在超出设计域场景下的可靠性问题提供了有效途径，通过动态扩展系统能力覆盖和提供定量保证，增强了系统在面对意外情况时的弹性。

Abstract: Ensuring reliable performance in situations outside the Operational Design Domain (ODD) remains a primary challenge in devising resilient autonomous systems. We explore this challenge by introducing an approach for adapting probabilistic system models to handle out-of-ODD scenarios while, in parallel, providing quantitative guarantees. Our approach dynamically extends the coverage of existing system situation capabilities, supporting the verification and adaptation of the system's behaviour under unanticipated situations. Preliminary results demonstrate that our approach effectively increases system reliability by adapting its behaviour and providing formal guarantees even under unforeseen out-of-ODD situations.

</details>


### [36] [When Equality Fails as a Rewrite Principle: Provenance and Definedness for Measurement-Bearing Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07626)
*David B. Hulak,Arthur F. Ramos,Ruy J. G. B. de Queiroz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种跟踪来源和定义性的统一语义，用于处理带有测量的表达式，解决了代数等式在测量表达式中不适用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通代数等式对于带有测量的表达式不是可靠的改写原则，因为相同观测的重用会影响结果，除法操作会使代数相等的形式在定义域上产生差异。

Method: 提出了令牌敏感包围语义，跟踪来源和定义性；通过可容许域细化获得域安全改写判断；支持相对变体连接局部和全局可容许性；使用Lean 4形式化所有定义和定理。

Result: 还原定理在普遍可容许支持上恢复基于包围的理论；恢复定理内化了消去、背景减法和正区间自除法；严格性定理显示可达奇点使简化单向化；不足性定理显示擦除令牌身份会丢失定义性无法恢复的区别。

Conclusion: 需要同时跟踪来源和定义性来处理测量表达式的可靠改写，令牌敏感语义提供了处理代数等式在测量上下文中局限性的理论基础。

Abstract: Ordinary algebraic equality is not a sound rewrite principle for measurement-bearing expressions. Reuse of the same observation matters, and division can make algebraically equal forms differ on where they are defined. We present a unified semantics that tracks both provenance and definedness. Token-sensitive enclosure semantics yields judgments for one-way rewriting and interchangeability. An admissible-domain refinement yields a domain-safe rewrite judgment, and support-relative variants connect local and global admissibility. Reduction theorems recover the enclosure-based theory on universally admissible supports. Recovery theorems internalize cancellation, background subtraction, and positive-interval self-division. Strictness theorems show that reachable singularities make simplification one-way and make common-domain equality too weak for licensed replacement. An insufficiency theorem shows that erasing token identity collapses distinctions that definedness alone cannot recover. All definitions and theorems are formalized in sorry-free Lean 4.

</details>
