### [37] [IPEK: Intelligent Priority-Aware Event-Based Trust with Asymmetric Knowledge for Resilient Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07532)
*İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出IPKE系统，通过事件感知和位置感知的不对称信任机制，结合Dempster-Shafer理论，有效检测车载自组网中的智能攻击者，在保持0%误报率的同时达到高召回率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车载自组网信任模型对交通事件采用同质化处理，无法检测智能攻击者的策略性攻击。攻击者通过正确报告低优先级事件积累信誉，然后在安全关键情况下注入虚假数据，现有方法因忽略事件严重性和位置关键性而无法识别此类攻击。

Method: 1. 首次正式定义并模拟事件感知和位置感知的智能攻击模型；2. 提出不对称局部信任机制，惩罚随事件和位置严重性缩放，奖励采用渐近模型，使信任难以恢复；3. 采用Dempster-Shafer理论进行全局信任融合，使用Yager组合规则将冲突证据分配给不确定性，结合顺序源可靠性排序和不对称风险强调机制。

Result: 使用OMNeT++、Veins和SUMO进行仿真，在15-35%攻击者密度下，IPKE系统在所有场景中保持0%误报率（无诚实车辆被错误撤销），同时召回率保持在75%以上，F1分数超过0.86，显著优于MDT和TCEMD方法。

Conclusion: 将上下文感知集成到攻击建模和信任评估中，显著优于对称方法对抗策略性对手。不对称信任机制和Dempster-Shafer融合能有效检测智能攻击，同时避免误报诚实车辆。

Abstract: Vehicular Ad Hoc Networks (VANETs) are vulnerable to intelligent attackers who exploit the homogeneous treatment of traffic events in existing trust models. These attackers accumulate reputation by reporting correctly on low-priority events and then inject false data during safety-critical situations - a strategy that current approaches cannot detect because they ignore event severity and location criticality in trust calculations. This paper addresses this gap through three contributions. First, it introduces event-aware and location-aware intelligent attack models, which have not been formally defined or simulated in prior work. Second, it proposes an asymmetric local trust mechanism where penalties scale with event and location severity while rewards follow an asymptotic model, making trust difficult to regain after misuse. Third, it adapts Dempster-Shafer Theory for global trust fusion using Yager's combination rule - assigning conflicting evidence to uncertainty rather than forcing premature decisions - combined with sequential source-reliability ordering and an asymmetric risk accentuation mechanism. Simulations using OMNeT++, Veins, and SUMO compare the proposed system (IPEK) against MDT and TCEMD under attacker densities of 15-35 percent. IPEK maintained 0 percent False Positive Rate across all scenarios, meaning no honest vehicle was wrongly revoked, while sustaining Recall above 75 percent and F1-scores exceeding 0.86. These results demonstrate that integrating context-awareness into both attack modeling and trust evaluation significantly outperforms symmetric approaches against strategic adversaries.

</details>


### [38] [LCMP: Distributed Long-Haul Cost-Aware Multi-Path Routing for Inter-Datacenter RDMA Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07836)
*Dong-Yang Yu,Yuchao Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Jun Wang,Wenfei Wu,Haipeng Yao,Wendong Wang,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: LCMP是一个分布式长距离成本感知多路径路由框架，旨在将RDMA流放置在多个数据中心间路径上，实现低成本、低延迟和拥塞响应式传输


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMA赋能的云服务在多路径数据中心间部署时面临路径不对称、拥塞信号延迟和同时流路由冲突等新特性，导致现有路由方法失效

Method: LCMP结合控制平面路径质量评分和紧凑的交换机拥塞信号，前者统一评估不对称路径质量，后者实现对路径拥塞的响应式反应；通过过滤高成本候选路径并在缩减集合中执行多样性保持哈希来解决同时流决策冲突问题

Result: 在8个数据中心的测试平台上，LCMP相比最先进的DCN路由策略，将中位数和尾部FCT减速分别降低了76%和64%；在2000公里数据中心间场景的大规模NS-3仿真中确认了类似的改进

Conclusion: LCMP通过创新的多路径路由框架有效解决了RDMA在多路径数据中心间环境中的传输挑战，显著提升了性能指标

Abstract: RDMA-empowered cloud services are gradually deployed across datacenters (DCs) with multiple paths, which exhibit new properties of path asymmetry, delayed congestion signals, and simultaneous flow routing collisions, and further fail existing routing methods.
  We present LCMP, a distributed long-haul cost-aware multi-path routing framework that aims to place RDMA flows on multiple inter-DC paths, achieving low-cost, low-latency, and congestion-responsive transmission. LCMP combines a control-plane path-quality score with compact on-switch congestion signals, where the former unifies quality assessment for asymmetric paths and the latter enables responsive reaction to path congestion. LCMP further resolves the simultaneous flow decision collision problem by filtering high-cost candidates, and performing a diversity-preserving hash inside the reduced set. On an 8-DC testbed, LCMP reduces median and tail FCT slowdown by up to 76% and 64%, respectively compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) DCN routing strategies. And large-scale NS-3 simulations under the 2000 km inter-DC scenario confirm similar improvements.

</details>


### [39] [LITE: Lightweight Channel Gain Estimation with Reduced X-Haul CSI Signaling in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08458)
*David Goez,Marco Piazzola,Giulia Costa,Achiel Colpaert,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Esra Aycan Beyazit,Nina Slamnik-Krijestorac,Johann M. Marquez-Barja,Miguel Camelo Botero*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: LITE：面向O-RAN中CF-MaMIMO系统的轻量级CSI压缩与预测管道，通过1-D卷积自编码器压缩CSI和SE增强型BiLSTM预测器，在降低83.39%模型复杂度的同时提升5%准确率，实现X-haul高效、低延迟的信道增益预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CF-MaMIMO在O-RAN中虽能提供高频谱效率，但频繁的CSI交换会严重消耗X-haul带宽，现有基于未压缩CSI或重型预测器的方法无法满足实际部署的传输和处理预算限制。

Method: 提出LITE轻量级管道：在O-DU部署1-D卷积自编码器进行50% CSI压缩，在Near-RT-RIC部署SE增强型双向LSTM预测器进行短时程轨迹无关预测；采用压缩感知训练和TensorRT优化实现。

Result: 实现50% CSI压缩，模型复杂度降低83.39%，预测准确率相比基线BiLSTM提升5%；压缩感知训练仅带来6%准确率损失；TensorRT优化实现147k QPS，吞吐量提升4.6倍。

Conclusion: LITE能够提供X-haul高效、低延迟且部署就绪的信道增益预测方案，兼容O-RAN分割架构，解决了CF-MaMIMO系统中CSI交换带来的带宽和计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Cell-Free Massive Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (CF-MaMIMO) in Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) promises high spectral efficiency but is limited by frequent Channel State Information (CSI) exchanges, which strain fronthaul/midhaul/backhaul (X-haul) bandwidth and exceed the capabilities of existing approaches relying on uncompressed CSI or heavy predictors. To overcome these constraints, we propose LITE, a lightweight pipeline combining a 1-D convolutional Autoencoder (AE) at the O-RAN Distributed Unit (O-DU) with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)-enhanced Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) predictor at the Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT-RIC), enabling short-horizon trajectory-unaware forecasting under strict transport and processing budgets. LITE applies 50% CSI compression and an asymmetric SE-BiLSTM, reducing model complexity by 83.39% while improving accuracy by 5% relative to a baseline BiLSTM. With compression-aware training, the Lightweight Intelligent Trajectory Estimator (LITE) incurs only 6% accuracy loss versus the BiLSTM baseline, outperforming independent and end-to-end strategies. A TensorRT-optimized implementation achieves 147k Queries per Second (QPS), a 4.6x throughput gain. These results demonstrate that LITE delivers X-haul-efficient, low-latency, and deployment-ready channel-gain prediction compatible with O-RAN splits.

</details>

### [40] [Label Leakage Attacks in Machine Unlearning: A Parameter and Inversion-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07386)
*Weidong Zheng,Kongyang Chen,Yao Huang,Yuanwei Guo,Yatie Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对机器学习中的类别遗忘场景，分析了遗忘数据类别泄露问题，提出了四种基于模型参数和模型反演的攻击方法，能够有效推断目标模型遗忘的类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能技术在面部识别等领域的广泛应用，数据隐私安全问题受到广泛关注，特别是隐私保护法律强调的"被遗忘权"。现有技术提出了各种遗忘方法，但可能无意中泄露遗忘数据的类别信息。本文聚焦于类别遗忘场景，分析遗忘数据类别泄露的潜在问题。

Method: 从不同知识背景的攻击者角度，提出了四种攻击方法：1）模型参数层面：通过计算目标模型与在保留数据子集和无关数据上训练的辅助模型参数之间的点积或向量差，构建判别特征，然后使用k-means聚类、Youden指数和决策树算法准确识别遗忘类别；2）模型反演领域：设计了基于梯度优化的白盒攻击和基于遗传算法的黑盒攻击来重建类别原型样本，通过阈值准则和信息熵准则分析合成样本的预测分布来推断遗忘类别。

Result: 在四个标准数据集上针对五种最先进的遗忘算法评估了所提出的攻击方法，提供了每种方法优势和局限性的详细分析。实验结果表明，该方法能够有效推断目标模型遗忘的类别。

Conclusion: 本文提出的攻击方法能够有效识别机器学习模型中遗忘的类别，揭示了现有遗忘方法在隐私保护方面的潜在漏洞，为设计更安全的遗忘算法提供了重要参考。

Abstract: With the widespread application of artificial intelligence technologies in face recognition and other fields, data privacy security issues have received extensive attention, especially the \textit{right to be forgotten} emphasized by numerous privacy protection laws. Existing technologies have proposed various unlearning methods, but they may inadvertently leak the categories of unlearned data. This paper focuses on the category unlearning scenario, analyzes the potential problems of category leakage of unlearned data in multiple scenarios, and proposes four attack methods from the perspectives of model parameters and model inversion based on attackers with different knowledge backgrounds. At the level of model parameters, we construct discriminative features by computing either dot products or vector differences between the parameters of the target model and those of auxiliary models trained on subsets of retained data and unrelated data, respectively. These features are then processed via k-means clustering, Youden's Index, and decision tree algorithms to achieve accurate identification of the forgotten class. In the model inversion domain, we design a gradient optimization-based white-box attack and a genetic algorithm-based black-box attack to reconstruct class-prototypical samples. The prediction profiles of these synthesized samples are subsequently analyzed using a threshold criterion and an information entropy criterion to infer the forgotten class. We evaluate the proposed attacks on four standard datasets against five state-of-the-art unlearning algorithms, providing a detailed analysis of the strengths and limitations of each method. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach can effectively infer the classes forgotten by the target model.

</details>


### [41] [Interpreting the Error of Differentially Private Median Queries through Randomization Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07581)
*Thomas Humphries,Tim Li,Shufan Zhang,Karl Knopf,Xi He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PostRI方法，在差分隐私中位数估计后计算随机化区间，相比现有方法提升14%-850%的效用，同时保持窄区间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私统计中，由于添加噪声导致结果难以解释。现有方法为获得高质量的随机化区间（RI）会降低中位数本身的质量，特别是对于噪声分布依赖输入的查询（如中位数）

Method: 提出PostRI方法，先估计中位数，然后计算随机化区间。这种方法允许在获得高质量中位数估计的同时，也能提供描述差分隐私误差的置信区间

Result: PostRI方法相比相关工作，中位数估计的效用提升14%-850%，同时能够维持较窄的随机化区间

Conclusion: PostRI方法解决了差分隐私中位数估计中效用与解释性之间的权衡问题，能够在保持高质量中位数估计的同时提供准确的误差区间

Abstract: It can be difficult for practitioners to interpret the quality of differentially private (DP) statistics due to the added noise. One method to help analysts understand the amount of error introduced by DP is to return a Randomization Interval (RI), along with the statistic. A RI is a type of confidence interval that bounds the error introduced by DP. For queries where the noise distribution depends on the input, such as the median, prior work degrades the quality of the median itself to obtain a high-quality RI. In this work, we propose PostRI, a solution to compute a RI after the median has been estimated. PostRI enables a median estimation with 14%-850% higher utility than related work, while maintaining a narrow RI.

</details>


### [42] [Private Seeds, Public LLMs: Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07486)
*Qian Ma,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RPSG是一种利用差分隐私和私有种子生成逼真且保护隐私的合成文本数据的方法，在隐私保护和数据保真度之间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已成为合成数据生成的有力工具，但生成包含个人信息的私有文本副本时，需要在隐私保护和数据实用性之间进行仔细权衡。现有方法难以同时实现高保真度和强隐私保护。

Method: 提出RPSG方法，结合隐私保护机制（包括形式化差分隐私）和私有种子（包含个人信息的文本），生成逼真的合成数据。该方法利用隐私保护技术确保数据隐私，同时保持合成数据的真实性和实用性。

Result: 与最先进的私有合成数据生成方法相比，RPSG在保持对私有数据高保真度的同时，提供了强大的隐私保护。综合实验证明了该方法在隐私和效用平衡方面的优越性能。

Conclusion: RPSG方法成功解决了私有文本合成数据生成中的隐私-效用权衡问题，为实际应用提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够在保护个人隐私的同时生成高质量的合成数据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for synthetic data generation. A particularly important use case is producing synthetic replicas of private text, which requires carefully balancing privacy and utility. We propose Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation (RPSG), which leverages privacy-preserving mechanisms, including formal differential privacy (DP); and private seeds, in particular text containing personal information, to generate realistic synthetic data. Comprehensive experiments against state-of-the-art private synthetic data generation methods demonstrate that RPSG achieves high fidelity to private data while providing strong privacy protection.

</details>


### [43] [TRUSTDESC: Preventing Tool Poisoning in LLM Applications via Trusted Description Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07536)
*Hengkai Ye,Zhechang Zhang,Jinyuan Jia,Hong Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRUSTDESC是一个防止工具中毒攻击的框架，通过从工具实现自动生成可信的工具描述，解决现有防御对隐式攻击无效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）依赖外部工具执行任务，但工具集成引入了新的提示注入攻击面——工具中毒攻击（TPAs）。攻击者通过操纵工具描述嵌入恶意指令（显式TPAs）或误导性声明（隐式TPAs）来影响模型行为和工具选择。现有防御主要检测异常指令，对隐式TPAs无效。

Method: TRUSTDESC采用三阶段流水线：1) SliceMin：通过可达性感知的静态分析和LLM引导的代码精简提取最小工具相关代码切片；2) DescGen：从这些切片合成描述，同时缓解误导或对抗性代码伪影；3) DynVer：通过执行合成任务和验证行为声明进行动态验证，进一步精炼描述。

Result: 在52个跨多个工具生态系统的真实世界工具上评估TRUSTDESC。结果显示，TRUSTDESC能生成准确工具描述，提高任务完成率，同时从根本上缓解隐式TPAs，且时间和金钱开销最小。

Conclusion: TRUSTDESC是首个通过从实现自动生成可信描述来防止工具中毒的框架，有效解决了现有防御对隐式攻击无效的问题，为LLM工具集成提供了更安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to perform time-sensitive tasks and real-world actions. While tool integration expands LLM capabilities, it also introduces a new prompt-injection attack surface: tool poisoning attacks (TPAs). Attackers manipulate tool descriptions by embedding malicious instructions (explicit TPAs) or misleading claims (implicit TPAs) to influence model behavior and tool selection. Existing defenses mainly detect anomalous instructions and remain ineffective against implicit TPAs. In this paper, we present TRUSTDESC, the first framework for preventing tool poisoning by automatically generating trusted tool descriptions from implementations. TRUSTDESC derives implementation-faithful descriptions through a three-stage pipeline. SliceMin performs reachability-aware static analysis and LLM-guided debloating to extract minimal tool-relevant code slices. DescGen synthesizes descriptions from these slices while mitigating misleading or adversarial code artifacts. DynVer refines descriptions through dynamic verification by executing synthesized tasks and validating behavioral claims. We evaluate TRUSTDESC on 52 real-world tools across multiple tool ecosystems. Results show that TRUSTDESC produces accurate tool descriptions that improve task completion rates while mitigating implicit TPAs at their root, with minimal time and monetary overhead.

</details>


### [44] [MEV-ACE: Identity-Authenticated Fair Ordering for Proposer-Controlled MEV Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07568)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MEV ACE是一个公平排序协议，通过注册经济身份、认证提交/开放消息和可验证延迟随机性，消除区块生产者对交易排序的单方面控制权，防止前置交易、三明治攻击和审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV（最大可提取价值）对区块链公平性构成结构性威胁，因为区块生产者可以观察待处理交易并单方面决定其排序或包含。现有缓解措施存在两个未解决的缺陷：承诺未通过可惩罚身份进行认证，包含义务没有可转移的证据供其他验证者验证。

Method: MEV ACE结合三种机制：1) 使用注册经济身份，其认证密钥从ACE GF框架确定性派生并在链上绑定；2) 使用带验证者接收阈值的认证提交和开放消息，使可接纳性和包含义务可独立审计；3) 使用基于可验证延迟的随机性，在可接纳承诺集固定后才确定交易顺序。

Result: 在拜占庭容错验证者模型中形式化协议，在标准假设下证明三个属性：可接纳集锁定后的顺序不可预测性、签名不可伪造性下的承诺真实性、获得阈值提交和开放收据的交易的可问责包含性。当生产者和用户保证金超过无效执行或选择性不开放的单个时隙收益时，MEV ACE消除了提议者对前置交易、三明治攻击和已接纳交易审查的单方面自由裁量权。

Conclusion: MEV ACE协议在结构上保持单时隙，不需要阈值解密委员会，兼容后量子签名方案如ML-DSA-44，有效解决了MEV带来的公平性问题。

Abstract: Maximal Extractable Value, or MEV, remains a structural threat to blockchain fairness because a block producer can often observe pending transactions and unilaterally decide their ordering or inclusion. Existing mitigations hide transaction contents or outsource ordering, but they often leave two gaps unresolved. First, commitments are not authenticated by slashable identities. Second, inclusion obligations are not backed by transferable evidence that other validators can verify. This paper presents MEV ACE, a fair ordering protocol for proposer controlled ordering MEV. MEV ACE combines three mechanisms. First, it uses registered economic identities whose authentication keys are deterministically derived from the ACE GF framework and bonded on chain. Second, it uses authenticated commit and open messages with validator receipt thresholds, which make admissibility and inclusion obligations independently auditable. Third, it uses verifiable delay based randomness to determine transaction order only after the admissible commitment set is fixed. We formalize the protocol in a Byzantine fault tolerant validator model with threshold receipts and show three properties under standard assumptions: order unpredictability after the admissible set is locked, commitment authenticity under signature unforgeability, and accountable inclusion for transactions that obtain threshold commit and open receipts. Under these conditions, and when producer and user bonds exceed the one slot gain from invalid execution or selective non opening, MEV ACE removes unilateral proposer discretion over front running, sandwich attacks, and censorship against admitted transactions. The protocol remains single slot in structure, requires no threshold decryption committee, and is compatible with post quantum signature schemes such as ML DSA 44.

</details>


### [45] [TrajGuard: Streaming Hidden-state Trajectory Detection for Decoding-time Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07727)
*Cheng Liu,Xiaolei Liu,Xingyu Li,Bangzhou Xin,Kangyi Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrajGuard：基于解码过程中隐藏状态轨迹的实时越狱防御框架，无需模型修改，通过滑动窗口聚合隐藏状态来量化风险，在风险持续超过阈值时触发轻量级语义裁决，实现即时中断或约束后续解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱防御范式主要依赖对提示、输出或内部状态的静态检测，往往忽略了解码过程中风险的动态演变。这导致解码轨迹中嵌入的风险信号未被充分利用，构成当前防御系统的关键盲点。

Method: 提出TrajGuard训练免费的解码时防御框架：1）实证发现解码阶段关键层的隐藏状态比输入越狱提示携带更强、更稳定的风险信号；2）通过滑动窗口聚合隐藏状态轨迹来实时量化风险；3）仅当局部窗口内风险持续超过阈值时触发轻量级语义裁决；4）实现即时中断或约束后续解码。

Result: 在12种越狱攻击和各种开源LLM上的广泛实验表明：TrajGuard平均防御率达到95%，检测延迟降至5.2毫秒/令牌，同时保持误报率低于1.5%。

Conclusion: 解码过程中的隐藏状态轨迹能有效支持实时越狱检测，为无需模型修改的防御开辟了有前景的方向。TrajGuard展示了利用解码动态信息进行高效防御的可行性。

Abstract: Existing jailbreak defense paradigms primarily rely on static detection of prompts, outputs, or internal states, often neglecting the dynamic evolution of risk during decoding. This oversight leaves risk signals embedded in decoding trajectories underutilized, constituting a critical blind spot in current defense systems. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that hidden states in critical layers during the decoding phase carry stronger and more stable risk signals than input jailbreak prompts. Specifically, the hidden representations of tokens generated during jailbreak attempts progressively approach high-risk regions in the latent space. Based on this observation, we propose TrajGuard, a training-free, decoding-time defense framework. TrajGuard aggregates hidden-state trajectories via a sliding window to quantify risk in real time, triggering a lightweight semantic adjudication only when risk within a local window persistently exceeds a threshold. This mechanism enables the immediate interruption or constraint of subsequent decoding. Extensive experiments across 12 jailbreak attacks and various open-source LLMs show that TrajGuard achieves an average defense rate of 95%. Furthermore, it reduces detection latency to 5.2 ms/token while maintaining a false positive rate below 1.5%. These results confirm that hidden-state trajectories during decoding can effectively support real-time jailbreak detection, highlighting a promising direction for defenses without model modification.

</details>


### [46] [The Art of (Mis)alignment: How Fine-Tuning Methods Effectively Misalign and Realign LLMs in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07754)
*Rui Zhang,Hongwei Li,Yun Shen,Xinyue Shen,Wenbo Jiang,Guowen Xu,Yang Liu,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型的安全对齐问题，揭示了攻击与防御之间的机制不对称性，发现ORPO在破坏对齐方面最有效，而DPO在重新对齐方面表现最佳但会牺牲模型实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的部署引发了重大的伦理和安全担忧。虽然采用LLM对齐技术来提高模型的安全性和可信度，但攻击者可以利用这些技术破坏安全性以实现恶意目的，导致"错位"。错位的LLM可能在开放平台上发布以放大危害，因此在部署不受信任的第三方LLM之前需要进行额外的安全对齐（重新对齐）。

Method: 通过评估四种监督微调（SFT）和两种偏好微调（PFT）方法在四个流行的安全对齐LLM上的表现，研究错位、重新对齐及其相互作用的效应。具体方法包括Odds Ratio Preference Optimization（ORPO）和Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）等。

Result: 揭示了攻击与防御之间的机制不对称性：ORPO在错位（破坏对齐）方面最有效，而DPO在重新对齐方面表现最佳，但会牺牲模型实用性。还发现了模型特定的抵抗性、多轮对抗动态的残留效应等值得注意的发现。

Conclusion: 这些发现强调了需要强大的安全保障措施和定制的安全对齐策略，以减轻LLM部署中的潜在风险。研究结果对LLM安全实践具有重要启示，表明需要针对不同场景选择适当的对齐方法。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) raises significant ethical and safety concerns. While LLM alignment techniques are adopted to improve model safety and trustworthiness, adversaries can exploit these techniques to undermine safety for malicious purposes, resulting in \emph{misalignment}. Misaligned LLMs may be published on open platforms to magnify harm. To address this, additional safety alignment, referred to as \emph{realignment}, is necessary before deploying untrusted third-party LLMs. This study explores the efficacy of fine-tuning methods in terms of misalignment, realignment, and the effects of their interplay. By evaluating four Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and two Preference Fine-Tuning (PFT) methods across four popular safety-aligned LLMs, we reveal a mechanism asymmetry between attack and defense. While Odds Ratio Preference Optimization (ORPO) is most effective for misalignment, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) excels in realignment, albeit at the expense of model utility. Additionally, we identify model-specific resistance, residual effects of multi-round adversarial dynamics, and other noteworthy findings. These findings highlight the need for robust safeguards and customized safety alignment strategies to mitigate potential risks in the deployment of LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangrui4041/The-Art-of-Mis-alignment.

</details>


### [47] [Anamorphic Encryption with CCA Security: A Standard Model Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07771)
*Shujun Wang,Jianting Ning,Qinyi Li,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个在标准模型下通用的CCA安全变形加密方案，通过形式化变形密钥封装机制（AKEM）解决了现有研究中依赖CPA安全的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变形加密研究主要依赖CPA安全性，在标准模型下构建通用的CCA安全变形加密方案一直是一个未解决的开放性问题。特别是在接收者变形设置中，用户需要在被迫交出密钥时仍能保护隐藏信息，但现有方案无法抵抗CCA攻击。

Method: 形式化了变形密钥封装机制（AKEM），包括公钥变形KEM（PKAKEM）和对称密钥变形KEM（SKAKEM）两种变体。提出了通用构造方法，可以使用任何支持随机数恢复的KEM进行实例化。该方法基于KEM的注入性质，确保密文和随机数之间的唯一映射关系，并将变形机制集成到KEM-DEM范式中。

Result: 实现了强IND-CCA（sIND-CCA）安全性，能够抵抗控制解封装密钥的"独裁者"攻击。在标准模型中提供了严格的形式化证明，显著增强了隐蔽信道在现代密码基础设施中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过形式化AKEM并构建通用的CCA安全变形加密方案，解决了长期存在的开放性问题，为隐蔽通信提供了更强的安全保障，特别是在后妥协场景下能够有效保护隐藏信息。

Abstract: Anamorphic encryption serves as a vital tool for covert communication, maintaining secrecy even during post-compromise scenarios. Particularly in the receiver-anamorphic setting, a user can shield hidden messages even when coerced into surrendering their secret keys. However, a major bottleneck in existing research is the reliance on CPA-security, leaving the construction of a generic, CCA-secure anamorphic scheme in the standard model as a persistent open challenge. To bridge this gap, we formalize the Anamorphic Key Encapsulation Mechanism (AKEM), encompassing both Public-Key (PKAKEM) and Symmetric-Key (SKAKEM) variants. We propose generic constructions for these primitives, which can be instantiated using any KEM that facilitates randomness recovery. Notably, our framework achieves strong IND-CCA (sIND-CCA) security for the covert channel. We provide a rigorous formal proof in the standard model, demonstrating resilience against a "dictator" who controls the decapsulation key. The security of our approach is anchored in the injective property of the base KEM, which ensures a unique mapping between ciphertexts and randomness. By integrating anamorphism into the KEM-DEM paradigm, our work significantly enhances the practical utility of covert channels within modern cryptographic infrastructures.

</details>


### [48] [Post-Quantum Cryptographic Analysis of Message Transformations Across the Network Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08480)
*Ashish Kundu,Vishal Chakraborty,Ramana Kompella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 开发了一个分析跨层加密转换的正式框架，用于评估无线通信协议栈在量子计算威胁下的安全性，发现单层后量子加密足以保护载荷机密性，但所有层都必须迁移才能实现完整认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信中消息在协议栈各层经历多种加密转换，每层使用不同算法且对量子计算机的脆弱性不同。整体安全性取决于所有层的组合，而非单一层。需要系统分析跨层加密转换的后量子密码准备状态。

Method: 开发了正式的跨层加密转换分析框架：1) 将每层加密操作分为四个量子脆弱性类别；2) 定义各层后量子状态在全消息转换链中的组合方式；3) 证明这种组合形成有界格，机密性通过join(max)算子组合，认证通过meet(min)算子组合。应用于Linux和iOS平台的五个通信场景。

Result: 1) WPA2-Personal的后量子态势严格优于WPA3-Personal和WPA2-Enterprise；2) 单层后量子加密足以保护载荷机密性，但完整认证需要所有层都迁移到后量子密码；3) 元数据保护仅取决于最外层；4) 识别了若干研究挑战。

Conclusion: 提出了首个分析跨层加密转换后量子准备状态的正式框架，揭示了无线通信安全中重要的组合特性：机密性和认证在跨层组合中遵循不同规则，这对后量子迁移策略有重要指导意义。

Abstract: When a user sends a message over a wireless network, the message does not travel as-is. It is encrypted, authenticated, encapsulated, and transformed as it descends the protocol stack from the application layer to the physical medium. Each layer may apply its own cryptographic operations using its own algorithms, and these algorithms differ in their vulnerability to quantum computers. The security of the overall communication depends not on any single layer but on the \emph{composition} of transformations across all layers.
  We develop a preliminary formal framework for analyzing these cross-layer cryptographic transformations with respect to post-quantum cryptographic (PQC) readiness. We classify every per-layer cryptographic operation into one of four quantum vulnerability categories, define how per-layer PQC statuses compose across the full message transformation chain, and prove that this composition forms a bounded lattice with confidentiality composing via the join (max) operator and authentication via the meet (min). We apply the framework to five communication scenarios spanning Linux and iOS platforms, and identify several research challenges. Among our findings: WPA2-Personal provides strictly better PQC posture than both WPA3-Personal and WPA2-Enterprise; a single post-quantum layer suffices for payload confidentiality but \emph{every} layer must migrate for complete authentication; and metadata protection depends solely on the outermost layer.

</details>


### [49] [BRASP: Boolean Range Queries over Encrypted Spatial Data with Access and Search Pattern Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07797)
*Jing Zhang,Ganxuan Yang,Yifei Yang,Siqi Wen,Zhengyang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BRASP是一种支持加密空间数据布尔范围查询的可搜索加密方案，通过希尔伯特曲线编码和双服务器架构隐藏搜索模式和访问模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可搜索加密方案主要针对关键词查询，且会泄露搜索模式和访问模式，无法有效支持加密空间数据的布尔范围查询，存在隐私泄露风险

Method: 结合希尔伯特曲线前缀编码与加密的前缀-ID和关键词-ID倒排索引，支持空间范围过滤和连接关键词匹配；采用双服务器架构，通过索引洗牌和ID字段重分布隐藏搜索和访问模式

Result: 实验结果表明BRASP能有效保护查询隐私，同时保持相对较低的计算和通信开销；支持动态更新并实现前向安全性

Conclusion: BRASP为加密空间数据的布尔范围查询提供了一种安全高效的解决方案，通过双服务器架构和索引洗牌机制有效保护了查询隐私，源代码已公开以促进可重复性和进一步研究

Abstract: Searchable Encryption (SE) enables users to query outsourced encrypted data while preserving data confidentiality. However, most efficient schemes still leak the search pattern and access pattern, which may allow an honest-but-curious cloud server to infer query contents, user interests, or returned records from repeated searches and observed results. Existing pattern-hiding solutions mainly target keyword queries and do not naturally support Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. This paper presents BRASP, a searchable encryption scheme for Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. BRASP combines Hilbert-curve-based prefix encoding with encrypted prefix--ID and keyword--ID inverted indexes to support efficient spatial range filtering and conjunctive keyword matching. To hide the search pattern and access pattern under a dual-server setting, BRASP integrates index shuffling for encrypted keyword and prefix entries with ID-field redistribution across two non-colluding cloud servers. BRASP also supports dynamic updates and achieves forward security. We formalize the security of BRASP through confidentiality, shuffle indistinguishability, query unforgeability, and forward-security analyses, and we evaluate its performance experimentally on a real-world dataset. The results show that BRASP effectively protects query privacy while incurring relatively low computation and communication overhead. To facilitate reproducibility and further research, the source code of BRASP is publicly available at https://github.com/Egbert-Lannister/BRASP

</details>


### [50] [A Hardware-Anchored Privacy Middleware for PII Sharing Across Heterogeneous Embedded Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07839)
*Aditya Sabbineni,Pravin Nagare,Devendra Dahiphale,Preetam Dedu,Willison Lopes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: UDSS框架：面向物联网和智能家居设备的平台无关用户数据共享系统，通过上下文范围执行机制减少PII过度暴露风险，降低用户注册延迟65%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和智能家居生态系统的快速扩张导致用户数据管理在智能电视、游戏机、机顶盒等消费电子产品中呈现碎片化。当前设备上的用户注册流程存在高摩擦（手动数据输入）和不透明的数据共享实践，需要一种适用于共享设备环境的隐私优先解决方案。

Method: 提出用户数据共享系统（UDSS），这是一个平台无关的框架，用于在设备平台和第三方应用之间进行安全的、隐私优先的PII交换。系统实现上下文范围执行（CSE）机制，根据用户意图（区分登录和注册工作流）编程式限制数据暴露。采用分层访问模型平衡开发者需求和法规合规性（GDPR/CCPA）。在基于ARMv8 Linux的中间件上进行了概念验证实现。

Result: 概念验证实现表明，UDSS将用户注册延迟降低了65%，并通过协议强制执行的数据最小化显著减少了PII过度暴露风险。该系统为异构消费电子市场提供了标准化的身份管理方法。

Conclusion: UDSS框架为共享设备环境中的用户数据交换提供了安全、隐私优先的解决方案，特别适用于无法假设持久用户-设备绑定的消费电子产品。通过上下文感知的数据最小化和分层访问控制，该系统在改善用户体验的同时满足了隐私法规要求。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and smart home ecosystems has led to a fragmented landscape of user data management across consumer electronics (CE) such as Smart TVs, gaming consoles, and set-top boxes. Current onboarding processes on these devices are characterized by high friction due to manual data entry and opaque data-sharing practices. This paper introduces the User Data Sharing System (UDSS), a platform-agnostic framework designed to facilitate secure, privacy-first PII (Personally Identifiable Information) exchange between device platforms and third-party applications. Our system implements a Contextual Scope Enforcement (CSE) mechanism that programmatically restricts data exposure based on user intent - specifically distinguishing between Sign-In and Sign-Up workflows. Unlike cloud-anchored identity standards such as FIDO2/WebAuthn, UDSS is designed for shared, device-centric CE environments where persistent user-to-device binding cannot be assumed. We further propose a tiered access model that balances developer needs with regulatory compliance (GDPR/CCPA). A proof-of-concept implementation on a reference ARMv8 Linux-based middleware demonstrates that UDSS reduces user onboarding latency by 65% and measurably reduces PII over-exposure risk through protocol-enforced data minimization. This framework provides a standardized approach to identity management in the heterogeneous CE market.

</details>


### [51] [xDup: Privacy-Preserving Deduplication for Humanitarian Organizations using Fuzzy PSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08019)
*Tim Rausch,Sylvain Chatel,Wouter Lueks*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: xDup：一种用于人道主义组织跨组织去重的隐私保护系统，比现有解决方案快两个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义组织在危机情况下帮助确保人们的生计，通常多个组织在同一地区运作。为了确保有限预算能帮助尽可能多的人，组织需要进行跨组织去重以检测重复注册并确保受助者最多从一个组织获得援助。当前的去重方法存在隐私风险，可能将脆弱受助者的数据与其他组织共享。

Method: 提出了xDup系统，基于模糊PSI（私有集合交集）技术。开发了otFPSI协议，这是一种针对汉明空间的具体高效模糊PSI协议，无需输入假设。该系统满足人道主义组织的实际需求。

Result: xDup比现有解决方案快两个数量级。otFPSI协议比现有模糊PSI协议更高效。系统满足人道主义组织对隐私友好型跨组织去重的实际需求。

Conclusion: xDup是一个实用的去重系统，能够保护脆弱受助者的隐私，同时确保人道主义援助资源的有效分配。该系统基于高效的模糊PSI协议，适合实际人道主义任务应用。

Abstract: Humanitarian organizations help to ensure people's livelihoods in crisis situations. Typically, multiple organizations operate in the same region. To ensure that the limited budget of these organizations can help as many people as possible, organizations perform cross-organizational deduplication to detect duplicate registrations and ensure recipients receive aid from at most one organization. Current deduplication approaches risk privacy harm to vulnerable aid recipients by sharing their data with other organizations. We analyzed the needs of humanitarian organizations to identify the requirements for privacy-friendly cross-organizational deduplication fit for real-life humanitarian missions. We present xDup, a new practical deduplication system that meets the requirements of humanitarian organizations and is two orders of magnitude faster than current solutions. xDup builds on Fuzzy PSI, and we present otFPSI, a concretely efficient Fuzzy PSI protocol for Hamming Space without input assumptions. We show that it is more efficient than existing Fuzzy PSI protocols.

</details>


### [52] [TADP-RME: A Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy Framework for Enhancing Reliability of Data-Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08113)
*Labani Halder,Payel Sadhukhan,Sarbani Palit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TADP-RME是一个信任自适应差分隐私框架，通过逆信任分数动态调整隐私预算，并使用反向流形嵌入破坏局部几何结构，在异构用户信任环境下提升对抗性攻击下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方案使用固定隐私预算，导致效用-隐私权衡僵化，无法适应异构用户信任水平。同时，传统噪声差分隐私保留了数据的几何结构，使得推理攻击能够利用这些结构导致隐私泄露。

Method: 提出TADP-RME框架：1) 引入[0,1]范围内的逆信任分数，自适应调节隐私预算，实现效用与隐私的平滑过渡；2) 采用反向流形嵌入技术，通过非线性变换破坏局部几何关系，同时通过后处理保持形式化的差分隐私保证。

Result: 理论和实证结果表明，该方法改善了隐私-效用权衡，将攻击成功率降低高达3.1%，且没有显著效用损失。在对抗推理攻击方面，该框架持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TADP-RME为对抗性环境中的可靠学习提供了统一方法，通过信任自适应隐私预算调节和几何结构破坏，有效增强了系统在异构用户信任下的可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring reliability in adversarial settings necessitates treating privacy as a foundational component of data-driven systems. While differential privacy and cryptographic protocols offer strong guarantees, existing schemes rely on a fixed privacy budget, leading to a rigid utility-privacy trade-off that fails under heterogeneous user trust. Moreover, noise-only differential privacy preserves geometric structure, which inference attacks exploit, causing privacy leakage.
  We propose TADP-RME (Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy with Reverse Manifold Embedding), a framework that enhances reliability under varying levels of user trust. It introduces an inverse trust score in the range [0,1] to adaptively modulate the privacy budget, enabling smooth transitions between utility and privacy. Additionally, Reverse Manifold Embedding applies a nonlinear transformation to disrupt local geometric relationships while preserving formal differential privacy guarantees through post-processing.
  Theoretical and empirical results demonstrate improved privacy-utility trade-offs, reducing attack success rates by up to 3.1 percent without significant utility degradation. The framework consistently outperforms existing methods against inference attacks, providing a unified approach for reliable learning in adversarial environments.

</details>


### [53] [Towards Identification and Intervention of Safety-Critical Parameters in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08297)
*Weiwei Qi,Zefeng Wu,Tianhang Zheng,Zikang Zhang,Xiaojun Jia,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ESI框架量化LLM安全参数影响，揭示不同架构的安全关键模式，并基于此开发SET和SPA两种针对性干预方法提升和保持模型安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对LLM安全机制的清晰理解，阻碍了开发精确可靠的安全干预方法。需要更好地理解和控制LLM安全性，以应对多样化任务中的安全问题。

Method: 提出Expected Safety Impact (ESI)框架量化参数对安全性的影响；基于ESI分析发现密集LLM的安全关键参数主要位于中间层的value矩阵和MLPs，而MoE模型则集中在后期层MLPs；开发Safety Enhancement Tuning (SET)和Safety Preserving Adaptation (SPA)两种干预范式。

Result: SET仅更新1%的模型权重（100次迭代）就能将未对齐LLM的攻击成功率降低50%以上；SPA在1000次迭代指令微调后能将已对齐LLM的安全性能下降控制在1%以内。

Conclusion: ESI框架为理解和控制LLM安全性提供了量化工具，基于ESI的SET和SPA方法能够有效提升和保持模型安全性，为LLM安全干预提供了精确可靠的方法论。

Abstract: Ensuring Large Language Model (LLM) safety is crucial, yet the lack of a clear understanding about safety mechanisms hinders the development of precise and reliable methodologies for safety intervention across diverse tasks. To better understand and control LLM safety, we propose the Expected Safety Impact (ESI) framework for quantifying how different parameters affect LLM safety. Based on ESI, we reveal distinct safety-critical patterns across different LLM architectures: In dense LLMs, many safety-critical parameters are located in value matrices (V) and MLPs in middle layers, whereas in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, they shift to the late-layer MLPs. Leveraging ESI, we further introduce two targeted intervention paradigms for safety enhancement and preservation, i.e., Safety Enhancement Tuning (SET) and Safety Preserving Adaptation (SPA). SET can align unsafe LLMs by updating only a few safety-critical parameters, effectively enhancing safety while preserving original performance. SPA safeguards well-aligned LLMs during capability-oriented intervention (e.g., instruction tuning) by preventing disruption of safety-critical weights, allowing the LLM to acquire new abilities and maintain safety capabilities. Extensive evaluations on different LLMs demonstrate that SET can reduce the attack success rates of unaligned LLMs by over 50% with only a 100-iteration update on 1% of model weights. SPA can limit the safety degradation of aligned LLMs within 1% after a 1,000-iteration instruction fine-tuning on different tasks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ZJU-LLM-Safety/SafeWeights-ACL.

</details>


### [54] [Securing Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Taxonomy of Attacks, Defenses, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08304)
*Yuming Xu,Mingtao Zhang,Zhuohan Ge,Haoyang Li,Nicole Hu,Jason Chen Zhang,Qing Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出RAG安全的核心在于外部知识访问管道的安全性，通过建立操作边界区分LLM固有风险与RAG引入/放大的威胁，系统化分析RAG工作流程中的安全表面和防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM固有风险与RAG特有风险混为一谈，缺乏对RAG引入的新型安全风险的清晰界定和系统分析，需要建立专门针对RAG知识访问管道的安全框架。

Method: 建立操作边界分离LLM固有缺陷与RAG引入/放大的威胁；将RAG工作流程抽象为六个阶段；围绕三个信任边界和四个主要安全表面组织文献分析；系统回顾攻击、防御、修复机制和评估基准。

Result: 揭示了当前防御机制主要处于被动和碎片化状态；识别了RAG特有的四个安全表面：检索前知识污染、检索时访问操纵、下游上下文利用和知识泄露；建立了系统化的RAG安全分析框架。

Conclusion: 需要面向整个知识访问生命周期的分层、边界感知保护机制；未来研究方向应关注跨阶段的协同防御和主动安全策略，以应对RAG特有的安全挑战。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) significantly enhances large language models (LLMs) but introduces novel security risks through external knowledge access. While existing studies cover various RAG vulnerabilities, they often conflate inherent LLM risks with those specifically introduced by RAG. In this paper, we propose that secure RAG is fundamentally about the security of the external knowledge-access pipeline. We establish an operational boundary to separate inherent LLM flaws from RAG-introduced or RAG-amplified threats. Guided by this perspective, we abstract the RAG workflow into six stages and organize the literature around three trust boundaries and four primary security surfaces, including pre-retrieval knowledge corruption, retrieval-time access manipulation, downstream context exploitation, and knowledge exfiltration. By systematically reviewing the corresponding attacks, defenses, remediation mechanisms, and evaluation benchmarks, we reveal that current defenses remain largely reactive and fragmented. Finally, we discuss these gaps and highlight future directions toward layered, boundary-aware protection across the entire knowledge-access lifecycle.

</details>


### [55] [PIArena: A Platform for Prompt Injection Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08499)
*Runpeng Geng,Chenlong Yin,Yanting Wang,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PIArena：一个统一可扩展的提示注入评估平台，用于系统评估攻击与防御方法，发现现有防御存在泛化性不足、易受自适应攻击等关键局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前提示注入攻击研究面临关键缺口：缺乏统一的评估平台，导致难以可靠比较防御方法、理解其真实鲁棒性、评估跨任务泛化能力。许多防御方法最初报告有效，但后来发现在多样化数据集和攻击下鲁棒性有限。

Method: 提出PIArena统一可扩展平台，支持用户轻松集成最先进的攻击和防御方法，并在各种现有和新基准上进行评估。同时设计基于策略的动态攻击方法，根据防御反馈自适应优化注入提示。

Result: 通过PIArena的全面评估，揭示了最先进防御方法的关键局限性：跨任务泛化能力有限、易受自适应攻击、以及当注入任务与目标任务对齐时的根本性挑战。

Conclusion: PIArena填补了提示注入评估的关键空白，为系统评估攻击与防御提供了统一平台，揭示了现有防御方法的实质性局限性，强调了开发更鲁棒防御的必要性。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks pose serious security risks across a wide range of real-world applications. While receiving increasing attention, the community faces a critical gap: the lack of a unified platform for prompt injection evaluation. This makes it challenging to reliably compare defenses, understand their true robustness under diverse attacks, or assess how well they generalize across tasks and benchmarks. For instance, many defenses initially reported as effective were later found to exhibit limited robustness on diverse datasets and attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce PIArena, a unified and extensible platform for prompt injection evaluation that enables users to easily integrate state-of-the-art attacks and defenses and evaluate them across a variety of existing and new benchmarks. We also design a dynamic strategy-based attack that adaptively optimizes injected prompts based on defense feedback. Through comprehensive evaluation using PIArena, we uncover critical limitations of state-of-the-art defenses: limited generalizability across tasks, vulnerability to adaptive attacks, and fundamental challenges when an injected task aligns with the target task. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena.

</details>

### [56] [Towards Counterfactual Explanation and Assertion Inference for CPS Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07679)
*Zaid Ghazal,Hadiza Yusuf,Khouloud Gaaloul*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeCaF是一个用于CPS调试的反事实引导解释和基于断言的表征框架，通过生成最小化输入信号修改来将失败测试转变为通过测试，并推断可解释的断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS调试技术虽然能定位异常到特定模型组件，但无法提供触发违规的输入信号值、时序条件，或防止失败所需的最小精确时间修改。需要一种能解释失败原因并提供修复指导的方法。

Method: DeCaF框架包含三个核心步骤：1) 给定失败测试输入，生成反事实修改使测试从失败转为通过；2) 设计最小、必要且充分的修改来精确恢复正确性；3) 推断输入上的逻辑断言，以可解释形式概括恢复条件，无需访问内部模型细节。结合三种反事实生成器和两种因果模型，并推断成功断言。

Result: 在三个CPS案例研究中，DeCaF的最佳成功率通过KD-Tree最近邻结合M5模型树实现，而遗传算法结合随机森林在成功率和因果精度之间提供了最强的平衡。

Conclusion: DeCaF为CPS调试提供了有效的反事实引导解释框架，能够生成最小化修复修改并推断可解释的断言，帮助工程师理解失败原因和修复条件，无需深入了解内部模型细节。

Abstract: Verification and validation of cyber-physical systems (CPS) via large-scale simulation often surface failures that are hard to interpret, especially when triggered by interactions between continuous and discrete behaviors at specific events or times. Existing debugging techniques can localize anomalies to specific model components, but they provide little insight into the input-signal values and timing conditions that trigger violations, or the minimal, precisely timed changes that could have prevented the failure. In this article, we introduce DeCaF, a counterfactual-guided explanation and assertion-based characterization framework for CPS debugging. Given a failing test input, DeCaF generates counterfactual changes to the input signals that transform the test from failing to passing. These changes are designed to be minimal, necessary, and sufficient to precisely restore correctness. Then, it infers assertions as logical predicates over inputs that generalize recovery conditions in an interpretable form engineers can reason about, without requiring access to internal model details. Our approach combines three counterfactual generators with two causal models, and infers success assertions. Across three CPS case studies, DeCaF achieves its best success rate with KD-Tree Nearest Neighbors combined with M5 model tree, while Genetic Algorithm combined with Random Forest provides the strongest balance between success and causal precision.

</details>


### [57] [MVOS_HSI: A Python Library for Preprocessing Agricultural Crop Hyperspectral Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07656)
*Rishik Aggarwal,Krisha Joshi,Pappu Kumar Yadav,Jianwei Qin,Thomas F. Burks,Moon S. Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MVOS_HSI是一个开源Python库，为叶片级高光谱图像处理提供端到端工作流，从原始ENVI文件校准到基于植被指数的叶片检测与裁剪，包含数据增强和可视化工具，旨在提高植物表型研究的可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像能无损研究植物性状，但数据处理常依赖实验室特定的MATLAB或Python脚本，导致工作流难以共享、结果难以复现。需要统一工具来标准化处理流程。

Method: 开发开源Python库MVOS_HSI，提供端到端工作流：包括原始ENVI文件校准、基于多种植被指数（NDVI、CIRedEdge、GCI）的叶片检测与裁剪、数据增强（为机器学习创建训练时变体）以及光谱剖面可视化工具。可作为Python库导入或命令行运行。

Result: MVOS_HSI将常见任务整合到单一软件包中，使研究人员能够生成一致且可重复的结果。代码和文档已在GitHub上开源提供。

Conclusion: MVOS_HSI通过提供标准化、可共享的高光谱图像处理工作流，解决了植物表型研究中数据处理碎片化的问题，促进了研究结果的一致性和可重复性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) allows researchers to study plant traits non-destructively. By capturing hundreds of narrow spectral bands per pixel, it reveals details about plant biochemistry and stress that standard cameras miss. However, processing this data is often challenging. Many labs still rely on loosely organized collections of lab-specific MATLAB or Python scripts, which makes workflows difficult to share and results difficult to reproduce. MVOS_HSI is an open-source Python library that provides an end-to-end workflow for processing leaf-level HSI data. The software handles everything from calibrating raw ENVI files to detecting and clipping individual leaves based on multiple vegetation indices (NDVI, CIRedEdge and GCI). It also includes tools for data augmentation to create training-time variations for machine learning and utilities to visualize spectral profiles. MVOS_HSI can be used as an importable Python library or run directly from the command line. The code and documentation are available on GitHub. By consolidating these common tasks into a single package, MVOS_HSI helps researchers produce consistent and reproducible results in plant phenotyping

</details>


### [58] [Externalization in LLM Agents: A Unified Review of Memory, Skills, Protocols and Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08224)
*Chenyu Zhou,Huacan Chai,Wenteng Chen,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Yuanyi Song,Tianyi Xu,Yingxuan Yang,Aofan Yu,Weiming Zhang,Congming Zheng,Jiachen Zhu,Zeyu Zheng,Zhuosheng Zhang,Xingyu Lou,Changwang Zhang,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"外部化"框架，解释LLM智能体从依赖模型权重转向依赖运行时基础设施（内存、技能、协议、执行框架）的发展趋势，认为外部认知基础设施对实际智能体进步至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体开发越来越不依赖改变模型权重，而是围绕模型重组运行时基础设施。论文旨在通过"外部化"视角分析这一转变，解释为什么实用智能体进步不仅需要更强的模型，还需要更好的外部认知基础设施。

Method: 采用"认知人工制品"概念框架，将智能体基础设施视为外部化认知负担的工具。分析历史发展（从权重到上下文到执行框架），将内存、技能和协议作为三种耦合的外部化形式进行系统分析，并探讨它们在智能体系统中的相互作用。

Result: 提出了一个系统级框架，解释外部基础设施如何将困难的认知负担转化为模型能更可靠解决的形式：内存外部化跨时间状态，技能外部化程序性专业知识，协议外部化交互结构，执行框架工程作为协调层。

Conclusion: 实用智能体进步日益依赖于更好的外部认知基础设施，而不仅仅是更强的模型。论文识别了参数化与外部化能力之间的权衡，讨论了自演化执行框架、共享智能体基础设施等新兴方向，以及评估、治理、模型与外部基础设施长期共同演化等开放挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly built less by changing model weights than by reorganizing the runtime around them. Capabilities that earlier systems expected the model to recover internally are now externalized into memory stores, reusable skills, interaction protocols, and the surrounding harness that makes these modules reliable in practice. This paper reviews that shift through the lens of externalization. Drawing on the idea of cognitive artifacts, we argue that agent infrastructure matters not merely because it adds auxiliary components, but because it transforms hard cognitive burdens into forms that the model can solve more reliably. Under this view, memory externalizes state across time, skills externalize procedural expertise, protocols externalize interaction structure, and harness engineering serves as the unification layer that coordinates them into governed execution. We trace a historical progression from weights to context to harness, analyze memory, skills, and protocols as three distinct but coupled forms of externalization, and examine how they interact inside a larger agent system. We further discuss the trade-off between parametric and externalized capability, identify emerging directions such as self-evolving harnesses and shared agent infrastructure, and discuss open challenges in evaluation, governance, and the long-term co-evolution of models and external infrastructure. The result is a systems-level framework for explaining why practical agent progress increasingly depends not only on stronger models, but on better external cognitive infrastructure.

</details>


### [59] [MIMIC-Py: An Extensible Tool for Personality-Driven Automated Game Testing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07752)
*Yifei Chen,Sarra Habchi,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MIMIC-Py是一个基于Python的自动化游戏测试工具，将基于人格的LLM智能体转化为可重用、可扩展的框架，支持通过API或合成代码与游戏交互，实现跨游戏复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频游戏是复杂、非确定性的系统，难以大规模自动测试。现有基于人格的LLM智能体工具大多是研究原型，缺乏跨游戏可重用性，需要将研究原型转化为实用的自动化游戏测试工具。

Method: MIMIC-Py采用模块化架构，将规划、执行和记忆与游戏特定逻辑解耦，将人格特质作为可配置输入，支持通过暴露的API或合成代码与游戏交互，实现最小工程投入即可部署到新游戏环境。

Result: MIMIC-Py提供了一个可重用和可扩展的框架，能够以最小工程努力部署到新游戏环境，弥合了研究原型与实用自动化游戏测试之间的差距。

Conclusion: MIMIC-Py通过将基于人格的LLM智能体转化为可重用框架，解决了自动化游戏测试工具的跨游戏复用问题，为实用的自动化游戏测试提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Modern video games are complex, non-deterministic systems that are difficult to test automatically at scale. Although prior work shows that personality-driven Large Language Model (LLM) agents can improve behavioural diversity and test coverage, existing tools largely remain research prototypes and lack cross-game reusability.
  This tool paper presents MIMIC-Py, a Python-based automated game-testing tool that transforms personality-driven LLM agents into a reusable and extensible framework. MIMIC-Py exposes personality traits as configurable inputs and adopts a modular architecture that decouples planning, execution, and memory from game-specific logic. It supports multiple interaction mechanisms, enabling agents to interact with games via exposed APIs or synthesized code. We describe the design of MIMIC-Py and show how it enables deployment to new game environments with minimal engineering effort, bridging the gap between research prototypes and practical automated game testing.
  The source code and a demo video are available on our project webpage: https://mimic-persona.github.io/MIMIC-Py-Home-Page/.

</details>


### [60] [An Empirical Study on Influence-Based Pretraining Data Selection for Code Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07769)
*Chengli Xing,Zhengran Zeng,Gexiang Fang,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据影响力评分过滤方法在编程数据集上的有效性，通过预训练10亿参数代码大模型，发现基于验证集损失的数据影响力评分过滤能提升模型编程性能，且不同下游任务对有益训练数据的标准存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于预训练数据过滤的研究主要集中在通用数据集上，对编程数据集的关注不足。虽然代码大模型在编程任务上表现出色，但预训练数据质量对模型性能至关重要，因此需要探索通用数据过滤技术在编程数据集上的有效性。

Method: 1. 提出计算生成式编程任务数据影响力评分的方法：将多种下游编码任务转化为验证集，使用模型在这些验证集上的损失作为性能指标；2. 从零开始预训练一个10亿参数的代码大模型，使用1000亿代码标记的数据集；3. 进行广泛的实证研究，评估数据影响力评分过滤方法的有效性。

Result: 1. 基于验证集损失的数据影响力评分过滤能够提升模型的编程性能；2. 有益训练数据的标准在不同下游编程任务间存在显著差异；3. 探索了基于预测的数据影响力评分过滤方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 数据影响力评分过滤方法在编程数据集上有效，但不同编程任务对训练数据的需求不同，需要针对特定任务定制数据过滤策略。这为代码大模型的预训练数据优化提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Recent advancements in code large language models (Code-LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in resolving programming related tasks. Meanwhile, researchers have recognized that the quality of pre-training data is crucial for improving LLM performance. However, most of the existing research on pre-training data filtering has focused on general datasets, and little attention for programming datasets. In this paper, we aim to address this gap by exploring the effectiveness of a widely used general data filtering technique, i.e., data-influence-score filtering, within the context of programming-related datasets. To this end, we first introduce a method for calculating data-influence-score for generative programming tasks which involves transforming a variety of downstream coding tasks into validation sets and using the models loss on these sets as a performance metric. Next, we pre-train a Code-LLMs with 1 billion parameters from scratch on a dataset of 100 billion code tokens. Based on it, we conduct an extensive empirical study to evaluate the effectiveness of data-influence-score filtering methods. Specifically, we examine how well this technique improves model performance, investigate how the characteristics of beneficial training data vary across different training stages and programming tasks, and assess the feasibility of prediction-based data-influence-score filtering method. Our findings show that data-influence-score filtering based on validation-set-loss can enhance models programming performance. Moreover, we observe that the criteria of beneficial training data differ significantly across various downstream programming tasks.

</details>


### [61] [To Copilot and Beyond: 22 AI Systems Developers Want Built](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07830)
*Rudrajit Choudhuri,Christian Bird,Carmen Badea,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开发者希望AI工具不仅辅助编码，更要支持编码之外的工作流程，通过"有界委托"模式吸收组装工作而非核心技艺，同时强调质量信号、权限范围、溯源和不确定性表达等约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI工具主要关注编码环节（仅占开发者工作时间的十分之一），但开发者需要AI支持编码之外的工作流程。研究旨在了解开发者希望AI在哪些方面提供支持，以及在哪些方面应保持距离。

Method: 对860名微软开发者进行问卷调查，采用人机协同、多模型委员会的主题分析方法，识别出5个任务类别中的22个AI系统需求。

Result: 识别出开发者期望的22个AI系统，涵盖五个任务类别。发现AI辅助开发中存在"右移负担"现象：开发者希望系统在加速代码生成的同时，更早嵌入质量信号，并强制执行明确的权限范围、溯源、不确定性表达和最小权限访问。

Conclusion: 提出了"有界委托"模式：开发者希望AI吸收围绕其技艺的组装工作，而非技艺本身。这一边界反映了专业身份认同，表明AI工具的价值不仅在于它能做什么，更在于它在何处以及如何精确地停止。

Abstract: Developers spend roughly one-tenth of their workday writing code, yet most AI tooling targets that fraction. This paper asks what should be built for the rest. We surveyed 860 Microsoft developers to understand where they want AI support, and where they want it to stay out. Using a human-in-the-loop, multi-model council-based thematic analysis, we identify 22 AI systems that developers want built across five task categories. For each, we describe the problem it solves, what makes it hard to build, and the constraints developers place on its behavior. Our findings point to a growing right-shift burden in AI-assisted development: developers wanted systems that embed quality signals earlier in their workflow to keep pace with accelerating code generation, while enforcing explicit authority scoping, provenance, uncertainty signaling, and least-privilege access throughout. This tension reveals a pattern we call "bounded delegation": developers wanted AI to absorb the assembly work surrounding their craft, never the craft itself. That boundary tracks where they locate professional identity, suggesting that the value of AI tooling may lie as much in where and how precisely it stops as in what it does.

</details>


### [62] [ZeroCoder: Can LLMs Improve Code Generation Without Ground-Truth Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07864)
*Lishui Fan,Mouxiang Chen,Tingwei Zhu,Kui Liu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ZeroCoder是一个完全无标签的协同进化框架，通过联合训练代码生成器和测试生成器，利用自生成代码-测试交互的执行反馈来改进代码生成，无需人工标注的测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法依赖人工标注的测试用例，这成为改进的瓶颈。虽然已有工作尝试使用自生成测试，但由于测试生成模型性能不佳导致缺乏判别性测试，限制了效果。需要一种无需真实监督的方法来协同进化代码和测试生成。

Method: ZeroCoder框架包含：1）通过执行采样解决方案与采样测试形成通过矩阵；2）使用可插拔选择算法识别可能正确的解决方案和一致测试的共识子集；3）推导角色特定奖励。采用基于排名的预过滤确保奖励质量，测试生成器使用平衡有效性和突变驱动判别性的课程学习。针对选择器漂移问题，引入DyB4贝叶斯选择器，仅需10个标注实例即可动态重新校准先验。

Result: 在三个模型和六个基准测试中，ZeroCoder持续改进代码生成和测试生成。在完全无标签设置下，在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上代码生成改进达14.5%。使用DyB4后，增益达到21.6%，测试生成改进24.3%，接近有监督性能。

Conclusion: ZeroCoder通过协同进化代码和测试生成，利用自生成交互提供渐进式监督，有效解决了无监督代码生成的挑战。DyB4选择器解决了选择器漂移问题，使框架在少量标注下也能保持校准。该方法为无监督代码生成提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Code generation is important in software engineering, and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a powerful paradigm to improve it through execution-based feedback. However, most RLVR pipelines rely on human-curated tests, making progress bottlenecked by scarce and costly supervision. Existing work tried to use self-generated tests to ground rewards, but the lack of discriminative tests constrains the effect due to the sub-optimal performance of the model on test generation. We aim to improve code generation without ground-truth supervision by co-evolving code and test generation, so that their interactions yield progressively more informative supervision. To this end, we present ZeroCoder, a fully label-free co-evolutionary framework that jointly trains a Coder and a Tester using execution feedback from self-generated code-test interactions. For each problem, ZeroCoder executes sampled solutions against sampled tests to form a passing matrix, identifies a consensus subset of likely-correct solutions and consistent tests via a pluggable selection algorithm, and derives role-specific rewards. To ensure reward quality, ZeroCoder filters low-information instances via rank-based pre-filtering and trains the Tester with a curriculum balancing validity and mutation-driven discriminativeness. We further identify selector drift, the progressive miscalibration of fixed selection rules during co-evolution, and introduce DyB4, a Bayesian selector that uses as few as 10 labeled instances to recalibrate its priors dynamically. Across three models and six benchmarks, ZeroCoder consistently improves code generation and test generation. In the fully label-free setting, it improves code generation by up to 14.5% over the base model on Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct. With DyB4, the gain reaches 21.6%, while test generation improves by 24.3%, approaching oracle-supervised performance.

</details>


### [63] [Investigating Code Reuse in Software Redesign: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07919)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Huaien Zhang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究针对跨仓库软件重构中代码和测试重用的挑战，提出基于克隆检测的语义对齐方法，在Soot/SootUp和FindBugs/SpotBugs案例中显著减少无关克隆并提高映射精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件重构需要保持功能同时提升质量属性，但跨仓库重构中手动重用代码和测试成本高且易出错，特别是在静态分析器这类常需跨仓库重构的场景中。现有方法缺乏有效的代码和测试追踪机制。

Method: 采用行动研究方法，结合实证调查和开源贡献验证。通过改造克隆检测技术来推导原始项目与重构项目间的代码映射，提出语义对齐启发式和可扩展的分层检测策略。在Soot/SootUp和FindBugs/SpotBugs两个重构项目对上评估。

Result: 在SAS阈值为0.5时，方法平均减少33-99%的可能无关克隆；在1,749个样本的基准测试中，精度提升至86%。向重构项目提交了5个问题和10个拉取请求，其中8个已被合并。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了跨仓库重构中的关键挑战是代码和测试的追踪问题，提出的基于克隆检测的语义对齐方法能有效支持双向重用，减少迁移错误，并为实际项目贡献了改进。

Abstract: Software redesign preserves functionality while improving quality attributes, but manual reuse of code and tests is costly and error-prone, especially in crossrepository redesigns. Focusing on static analyzers where cross-repo redesign needs often arise, we conduct a bidirectional study of the ongoing Soot/SootUp redesign case using an action research methodology that combines empirical investigation with validated open-source contributions. Our study reveals: (1) non-linear migration which necessitates bidirectional reuse, (2) deferred reuse via TODOs, (3) neglected test porting, and (4) residual bug propagation during migrations. We identify tracking corresponding code and tests as the key challenge, and address it by retrofitting clone detection to derive code mappings between original and redesigned projects. Guided by semantic reuse patterns derived in our study, we propose Semantic Alignment Heuristics and a scalable hierarchical detection strategy. Evaluations on two redesigned project pairs (Soot/SootUp and FindBugs/SpotBugs) show that our approach achieves an average reduction of 33-99% in likely irrelevant clones at a SAS threshold of 0.5 across all tool results, and improves precision up to 86% on our benchmark of 1,749 samples. Moreover, we contribute to the redesigned projects by submitting five issues and 10 pull requests, of which eight have been merged.

</details>


### [64] [Log-based, Business-aware REST API Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08007)
*Ding Yang,Ruixiang Qian,Zhao Wei,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LoBREST：基于日志的业务感知REST API测试技术，利用历史请求日志生成包含业务约束的测试用例，优于现有工具


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于REST规范的测试技术主要针对简单的业务不敏感功能（如CRUD操作），但缺乏对复杂业务敏感功能的测试能力，因为这些功能需要额外的业务约束，而这些约束通常不在REST规范中

Method: LoBREST采用三阶段方法：1) 使用局部性切片策略将历史请求日志分区为更小的切片；2) 增强切片：添加缺失操作的切片并补全切片内缺失的资源；3) 使用增强切片作为初始种子进行业务感知模糊测试

Result: 在17个真实服务上优于8个工具（包括Arat-rl、Morest和Deeprest），在16个服务上获得最佳操作覆盖率，在15个服务上获得最佳行覆盖率，平均比第二名提升2.1倍和1.2倍，检测到108个5XX错误，其中38个是其他工具未发现的

Conclusion: LoBREST通过利用历史请求日志中的业务约束信息，有效解决了现有REST API测试技术在业务敏感功能测试方面的不足，显著提升了测试覆盖率和错误检测能力

Abstract: REST APIs enable collaboration among microservices. A single fault in a REST API can bring down the entire microservice system and cause significant financial losses, underscoring the importance of REST API testing. Effectively testing REST APIs requires thoroughly exercising the functionalities behind them. To this end, existing techniques leverage REST specifications (e.g., Swagger or OpenAPI) to generate test cases. Using the resource constraints extracted from specifications, these techniques work well for testing simple, business-insensitive functionalities, such as resource creation, retrieval, update, and deletion. However, for complex, business-sensitive functionalities, these specification-based techniques often fall short, since exercising such functionalities requires additional business constraints that are typically absent from REST specifications. In this paper, we present LoBREST, a log-based, business-aware REST API testing technique that leverages historical request logs (HRLogs) to effectively exercise the business-sensitive functionalities behind REST APIs. To obtain compact operation sequences that preserve clean and complete business constraints, LoBREST first employs a locality-slicing strategy to partition HRLogs into smaller slices. Then, to ensure the effectiveness of the obtained slices, LoBREST enhances them in two steps: (1) adding slices for operations missing from HRLogs, and (2) completing missing resources within the slices. Finally, to improve test adequacy, LoBREST uses these enhanced slices as initial seeds to perform business-aware fuzzing. LoBREST outperformed eight tools (including Arat-rl, Morest, and Deeprest) across 17 real-world services. It achieved top operation coverage on 16 services and line coverage on 15, averaging 2.1x and 1.2x improvements over the runner-up. LoBREST detected 108 5XX bugs, including 38 found by no other tool.

</details>


### [65] [A Comparative Study of Semantic Log Representations for Software Log-based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08028)
*Yuqing Wang,Ying Song,Xiaozhou Li,Nana Reinikainen,Mika V. Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对日志异常检测中的语义表示方法进行基准测试，发现BERT方法效果更好但效率低，静态词嵌入方法效率高但效果差。作者提出QTyBERT方法，使用轻量级BERT变体和量化技术，在保持检测效果的同时显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的日志异常检测方法通常将语义日志表示作为端到端管道的一部分进行评估，但不同语义表示方法的影响尚未得到充分理解。需要系统评估各种语义表示方法在效率和效果上的权衡。

Method: 作者首先对广泛使用的语义日志表示方法进行基准测试，包括静态词嵌入方法（Word2Vec、GloVe、FastText）和基于BERT的上下文嵌入方法。然后提出QTyBERT方法，该方法使用SysBE（轻量级BERT变体）结合系统特定量化，以及CroSysEh（通过多系统无标签日志无监督训练）来增强日志嵌入的语义表达能力。

Result: 在三个公开日志数据集（BGL、Thunderbird、Spirit）上的实验表明，QTyBERT生成的日志嵌入在深度学习模型中能达到与BERT生成嵌入相当或更好的检测效果，同时将日志嵌入生成时间缩短到接近静态词嵌入方法的水平。

Conclusion: QTyBERT在日志异常检测的语义表示方法中实现了效果与效率的良好平衡，解决了BERT方法效率低和静态词嵌入方法效果不足的问题，为实际部署提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent deep learning (DL) methods for log anomaly detection increasingly rely on semantic log representation methods that convert the textual content of log events into vector embeddings as input to DL models. However, these DL methods are typically evaluated as end-to-end pipelines, while the impact of different semantic representation methods is not well understood. In this paper, we benchmark widely used semantic log representation methods, including static word embedding methods (Word2Vec, GloVe, and FastText) and the BERT-based contextual embedding method, across diverse DL models for log-event level anomaly detection on three publicly available log datasets: BGL, Thunderbird, and Spirit. We identify an effectiveness--efficiency trade off under CPU deployment settings: the BERT-based method is more effective, but incurs substantially longer log embedding generation time, limiting its practicality; static word embedding methods are efficient but are generally less effective and may yield insufficient detection performance. Motivated by this finding, we propose QTyBERT, a novel semantic log representation method that better balances this trade-off. QTyBERT uses SysBE, a lightweight BERT variant with system-specific quantization, to efficiently encode log events into vector embeddings on CPUs, and leverages CroSysEh to enhance the semantic expressiveness of these log embeddings. CroSysEh is trained unsupervisedly using unlabeled logs from multiple systems to capture the underlying semantic structure of the BERT model's embedding space. We evaluate QTyBERT against existing semantic log representation methods. Our results show that, for the DL models, using QTyBERT-generated log embeddings achieves detection effectiveness comparable to or better than BERT-generated log embeddings, while bringing log embedding generation time closer to that of static word embedding methods.

</details>


### [66] [Can LLMs Deobfuscate Binary Code? A Systematic Analysis of Large Language Models into Pseudocode Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08083)
*Li Hu,Xiuwei Shang,Jieke Shi,Shaoyin Cheng,Junqi Zhang,Gangyang Li,Zhou Yang,Weiming Zhang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BinDeObfBench是首个评估LLM在二进制反混淆性能的综合基准，涵盖预编译、编译时和编译后多个阶段的混淆变换。研究发现反混淆性能更依赖推理能力和领域专业知识而非模型规模，任务特定监督微调优于广泛领域预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制反混淆是逆向工程中的基础挑战，混淆技术被广泛用于阻碍分析和隐藏程序逻辑。尽管大语言模型在从混淆二进制中恢复语义方面显示出潜力，但缺乏对其有效性的系统性评估。

Method: 提出了BinDeObfBench基准，用于评估基于LLM的二进制反混淆，涵盖预编译、编译时和编译后三个阶段的多样化混淆变换。评估了不同LLM在反混淆任务上的表现，包括标准模型和推理模型，并比较了监督微调与预训练的效果。

Result: 反混淆性能更依赖推理能力和领域专业知识而非模型规模；任务特定监督微调始终优于广泛领域预训练；推理模型在严重混淆下能保持鲁棒性，并能泛化到不同指令集架构和优化级别；上下文学习对标准模型有益但对推理模型增益有限。

Conclusion: 研究强调了任务特定微调和推理驱动策略的重要性，BinDeObfBench为未来二进制反混淆研究提供了基础。推理能力和领域专业知识是反混淆成功的关键因素，而非单纯依赖模型规模。

Abstract: Deobfuscating binary code remains a fundamental challenge in reverse engineering, as obfuscation is widely used to hinder analysis and conceal program logic. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in recovering semantics from obfuscated binaries, a systematic evaluation of their effectiveness is still lacking. In this work, we present BinDeObfBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for assessing LLM-based binary deobfuscation across diverse transformations spanning pre-compilation, compile-time, and post-compilation stages. Our evaluation shows that deobfuscation performance depends more on reasoning capability and domain expertise than on model scale, and that task-specific supervised fine-tuning consistently outperforms broad domain pre-training. Reasoning models can maintain robustness under severe obfuscation, generalize across different instruction set architectures (ISAs) and optimization levels. In-context learning benefits standard models but yields limited gains for reasoning models. Overall, our study highlights the importance of task-specific fine-tuning and reasoning-driven strategies, and positions BinDeObfBench as a basis for future work in binary deobfuscation.

</details>


### [67] [GALA: Multimodal Graph Alignment for Bug Localization in Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08089)
*Zhuoyao Liu,Zhengran Zeng,Shu-Dong Huang,Yang Liu,Shikun Zhang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GALA框架通过图像UI图与代码结构的层次化对齐，解决多模态程序修复中视觉元素到代码组件的精确定位问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的APR方法在多模态场景（GUI截图报告bug）中表现不佳，因为图像转文本会丢失关键空间关系，导致视觉观察与代码组件脱节，定位退化为不精确的关键词匹配

Method: GALA框架包含四个阶段：1) 构建图像UI图捕获视觉元素及其结构关系；2) 文件级对齐，通过交叉引用UI图与仓库级结构定位候选文件；3) 函数级对齐，通过细粒度代码依赖推理将视觉元素精确映射到代码组件；4) 在已对齐的代码上下文中生成补丁

Result: 在SWE-bench Multimodal基准测试中，GALA实现了最先进的性能，证明了层次化结构对齐的有效性

Conclusion: GALA通过系统性地强制执行跨模态的语义和关系一致性，建立了高度准确的视觉到代码映射，将多模态APR从隐式语义猜测转向显式结构推理

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair (APR) has shown strong potential on textual benchmarks, yet struggles in multimodal scenarios where bugs are reported with GUI screenshots. Existing methods typically convert images into plain text, which discards critical spatial relationships and causes a severe disconnect between visual observations and code components, leading localization to degrade into imprecise keyword matching. To bridge this gap, we propose GALA (Graph Alignment for Localization in APR), a framework that shifts multimodal APR from implicit semantic guessing to explicit structural reasoning. GALA operates in four stages: it first constructs an Image UI Graph to capture visual elements and their structural relationships; then performs file-level alignment by cross-referencing this UI graph with repository-level structures (e.g., file references) to locate candidate files; next conducts function-level alignment by reasoning over fine-grained code dependencies (e.g., call graphs) to precisely ground visual elements to corresponding code components; and finally performs patch generation within the grounded code context based on the aligned files and functions. By systematically enforcing both semantic and relational consistency across modalities, GALA establishes a highly accurate visual-to-code mapping. Evaluations on the SWE-bench Multimodal benchmark demonstrate that GALA achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical structural alignment.

</details>


### [68] [Test-Oriented Programming: rethinking coding for the GenAI era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08102)
*Jorge Melegati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Test-Oriented Programming (TOP)新范式，开发者只需检查基于自然语言规范生成的测试代码，将生产代码委托给大语言模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编程工具主要关注代码自动补全或多智能体协作，但未能充分利用LLM潜力实现更高层次的抽象。作者认为LLM可以支持新的编程范式，让开发者从编写生产代码中解放出来。

Method: 提出Test-Oriented Programming (TOP)范式，开发者仅需编写或检查基于自然语言需求生成的测试代码，生产代码由LLM自动生成。开发了概念验证工具，使用两种不同LLM生成小型命令行程序。

Result: 获得了有希望的结果，验证了TOP范式的可行性。同时识别了在实际项目中应用该范式面临的挑战。

Conclusion: LLM能够支持更高层次的编程抽象，Test-Oriented Programming (TOP)是一种可行的新范式，但需要解决实际应用中的挑战才能用于真实项目。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown astonishing capability of generating software code, leading to its use to support developers in programming. Proposed tools have relied either on assistants for improved auto-complete or multi-agents, in which different model instances are orchestrated to perform parts of a problem to reach a complete solution. We argue that LLMs can enable a higher-level of abstraction, a new paradigm we called Test-Oriented Programming (TOP). Within this paradigm, developers only have to check test code generated based on natural language specifications, rather than focusing on production code, which could be delegated to the LLMs. To evaluate the feasibility of this proposal, we developed a proof-of-concept tool and used it to generate a small command-line program employing two different LLMs. We obtained promising results and identified challenges for the use of this paradigm for real projects.

</details>


### [69] [Towards Improving the External Validity of Software Engineering Experiments with Transportability Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08200)
*Julian Frattini,Richard Torkar,Robert Feldt,Carlo Furia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出在软件工程研究中采用可迁移性方法，通过结合实验数据和观察数据来提高受控实验的外部效度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中受控实验面临参与者代表性不足的问题，限制了实验结果的外部效度，而研究者通常拥有大量观察数据但未充分利用

Method: 引入可迁移性方法及其前提条件，通过模拟演示其潜力，概述软件工程研究场景应用，制定路线图和实用指南

Result: 可迁移性方法能够将实验结果从实验样本"迁移"到更广泛、更具代表性的目标群体，增强受控实验的外部效度

Conclusion: 在软件工程研究中采用可迁移性方法可以加强受控实验的外部效度，帮助该领域产生更可靠且在实践中更有用的结果

Abstract: Controlled experiments are a core research method in software engineering (SE) for validating causal claims. However, recruiting a sample of participants that represents the intended target population is often difficult or expensive, which limits the external validity of experimental results. At the same time, SE researchers often have access to much larger amounts of observational than experimental data (e.g., from repositories, issue trackers, logs, surveys and industrial processes). Transportability methods combine these data from experimental and observational studies to "transport" results from the experimental sample to a broader, more representative sample of the target population. Although the ability to combine observational and experimental data in a principled way could substantially benefit empirical SE research, transportability methods have - to our knowledge - not been adopted in SE. In this vision, we aim to help make that adoption possible. To that end, we introduce transportability methods, their prerequisites, and demonstrate their potential through a simulation. We then outline several SE research scenarios in which these methods could apply, e.g., how to effectively use students as substitutes for developers. Finally, we outline a road map and practical guidelines to support SE researchers in applying them. Adopting transportability methods in SE research can strengthen the external validity of controlled experiments and help the field produce results that are both more reliable and more useful in practice.

</details>


### [70] [Empirical Evaluation of Taxonomic Trace Links: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08207)
*Waleed Abdeen,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Peter Löwenadler,Parisa Yousefi,Krzysztof Wnuk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TTL方法通过领域特定分类学连接源和目标工件，在工业环境中评估显示对某些场景有用，但面临分类学开发、异构工件管理和分类器精度等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可追溯性是知识密集型任务中工件的关键质量属性，有助于提高软件质量，但在实践中常被忽视，主要挑战包括：痕迹粒度、缺乏通用工件结构、责任不明确。TTL方法旨在解决这些常见的可追溯性挑战。

Method: 在爱立信公司进行混合方法研究，涉及一个软件产品。收集了两个可追溯性用例的定量和定性数据。建立了463个业务用例、64个测试用例和277个ISO标准需求之间的追溯链接。此外，与从业者进行了三次焦点小组讨论。

Result: 识别了两个实际相关的可追溯性需求场景。从业者认为TTL对其中一个场景是有用的解决方案，但对另一个场景用处较小。开发领域特定分类学和管理异构工件结构是显著挑战。用于创建追溯链接的分类器精度需要提高才能使解决方案实用。

Conclusion: TTL是一种有前景的方法，可以在实践中采用并支持可追溯性用例。然而，TTL不是传统追溯链接的替代品，而是补充它们以支持更多可追溯性用例并鼓励早期创建追溯链接。

Abstract: Context: Traceability is a key quality attribute of artifacts that are used in knowledge-intensive tasks and supports software engineers in producing higher-quality software. Despite its clear benefits, traceability is often neglected in practice due to challenges such as granularity of traces, lack of a common artifact structure, and unclear responsibility. The Taxonomic Trace Links (TTL) approach connects source and target artifacts through a domain-specific taxonomy, aiming to address these common traceability challenges. Objective: In this study, we empirically evaluate TTL in an industrial setting to identify its strengths and weaknesses for real-world adoption. Method: We conducted a mixed-methods study at Ericsson involving one of its software products. Quantitative and qualitative data were collected across two traceability use cases. We established trace links between 463 business use cases, 64 test cases, and 277 ISO-standard requirements. Additionally, we held three focus group sessions with practitioners. Results: We identified two practically relevant scenarios where traceability is required and evaluated TTL in each. Overall, practitioners found TTL to be a useful solution for one of the scenarios, while less useful for the other. However, developing a domain-specific taxonomy and managing heterogeneous artifact structures were noted as significant challenges. Moreover, the precision of the classifier that is used to create trace links needs to be improved to make the solution practical. Conclusion: TTL is a promising approach that can be adopted in practice and enables traceability use cases. However, TTL is not a replacement for traditional trace links, but rather complements them to enable more traceability use cases and encourage the early creation of trace links.

</details>


### [71] [Security Concerns in Generative AI Coding Assistants: Insights from Online Discussions on GitHub Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08352)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Monika Swetha Gurupathi,Zadia Codabux,Nalin Arachchilage,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析在线论坛讨论，识别了开发者对GenAI编码助手（如GitHub Copilot）的四大安全担忧：数据泄露、代码许可、对抗攻击和不安全代码建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然生成式AI已成为软件开发工具的核心组件，但当前研究主要关注其技术性能评估，较少关注开发者特别是安全领域的新兴担忧。本研究旨在探索开发者对GenAI编码助手的安全关切。

Method: 从Stack Overflow、Reddit和Hacker News三个平台检索关于GitHub Copilot安全问题的帖子、评论和讨论线程，使用BERTopic进行聚类，并通过主题分析合成识别安全关切类别。

Result: 识别出四大主要安全关切领域：潜在数据泄露、代码许可问题、对抗攻击（如提示注入）和不安全代码建议，这些反映了对GenAI在软件工程中局限性和权衡的批判性反思。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于更广泛理解开发者如何感知和与GenAI编码助手互动，同时突出了改进其内置安全功能的关键领域，为未来研究和工具开发提供方向。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) has become a central component of many development tools (e.g., GitHub Copilot) that support software practitioners across multiple programming tasks, including code completion, documentation, and bug detection. However, current research has identified significant limitations and open issues in GenAI, including reliability, non-determinism, bias, and copyright infringement. While prior work has primarily focused on assessing the technical performance of these technologies for code generation, less attention has been paid to emerging concerns of software developers, particularly in the security realm. OBJECTIVE: This work explores security concerns regarding the use of GenAI-based coding assistants by analyzing challenges voiced by developers and software enthusiasts in public online forums. METHOD: We retrieved posts, comments, and discussion threads addressing security issues in GitHub Copilot from three popular platforms, namely Stack Overflow, Reddit, and Hacker News. These discussions were clustered using BERTopic and then synthesized using thematic analysis to identify distinct categories of security concerns. RESULTS: Four major concern areas were identified, including potential data leakage, code licensing, adversarial attacks (e.g., prompt injection), and insecure code suggestions, underscoring critical reflections on the limitations and trade-offs of GenAI in software engineering. IMPLICATIONS: Our findings contribute to a broader understanding of how developers perceive and engage with GenAI-based coding assistants, while highlighting key areas for improving their built-in security features.

</details>


### [72] [CIAO - Code In Architecture Out - Automated Software Architecture Documentation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08293)
*Marco De Luca,Tiziano Santilli,Domenico Amalfitano,Anna Rita Fasolino,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CIAO：基于LLM的自动化系统级架构文档生成流程，可直接从GitHub仓库生成符合标准模板的架构文档


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构文档对系统理解至关重要，但往往缺失或不完整。现有LLM技术主要关注局部代码分析，缺乏生成连贯系统级架构描述的能力，需要一种能够自动生成系统级架构文档的方法。

Method: 提出CIAO（Code In Architecture Out）流程，基于LLM的工作流，从GitHub仓库输入，按照ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010、SEI Views & Beyond和C4模型的标准模板生成系统级架构文档，可直接添加到目标仓库。

Result: 通过22名开发者的评估研究显示，生成的文档被认为有价值、可理解且与源代码基本准确。文档生成仅需几分钟且成本低廉，但存在图表质量、高层上下文建模和部署视图方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 结构化、标准导向的方法能有效指导LLM生成既实用又经济高效的系统级架构文档，为自动化架构文档生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Software architecture documentation is essential for system comprehension, yet it is often unavailable or incomplete. While recent LLM-based techniques can generate documentation from code, they typically address local artifacts rather than producing coherent, system-level architectural descriptions. This paper presents a structured process for automatically generating system-level architectural documentation directly from GitHub repositories using Large Language Models. The process, called CIAO (Code In Architecture Out), defines an LLM-based workflow that takes a repository as input and produces system-level architectural documentation following a template derived from ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010, SEI Views \& Beyond, and the C4 model. The resulting documentation can be directly added to the target repository. We evaluated the process through a study with 22 developers, each reviewing the documentation generated for a repository they had contributed to. The evaluation shows that developers generally perceive the produced documentation as valuable, comprehensible, and broadly accurate with respect to the source code, while also highlighting limitations in diagram quality, high-level context modeling, and deployment views. We also assessed the operational cost of the process, finding that generating a complete architectural document requires only a few minutes and is inexpensive to run.
