Moreover, our algorithm allows explicit minimization of the mean square error for each specific utility function. Accordingly, we introduce the first adaptive, linear-time, linear-space randomized algorithm, Adalina, that theoretically achieves improved mean square error. All of our theoretical findings are experimentally validated.

</details>


### [151] [Less Approximates More: Harmonizing Performance and Confidence Faithfulness via Hybrid Post-Training for High-Stakes Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08454)
*Haokai Ma,Lee Yan Zhen,Gang Yang,Yunshan Ma,Ee-Chien Chang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyTuning：一种混合后训练框架，通过渐进推理增益度量自适应调整推理蒸馏和内部反馈强化学习，在有限监督下提升准确性和置信度忠实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在高风险任务中部署时，错误但自信的推断可能导致严重现实危害，需要解决置信度忠实性问题。现有联合优化方法面临高质量训练语料稀缺、事实无根据的过度自信和错误更新放大等挑战。

Method: 提出渐进推理增益度量推理步骤是否逐步增强对最终答案的支持；引入HyTuning混合后训练框架，通过PRG风格度量自适应重新加权推理蒸馏和内部反馈强化学习，使用稀缺监督推理轨迹作为稳定锚点，同时利用大量未标记查询实现可扩展性。

Result: 在多个领域特定和通用基准测试中，HyTuning在有限监督下提高了准确性，同时实现了置信度忠实性，支持"更少近似于更多"的实际效果。

Conclusion: HyTuning通过渐进推理增益度量和自适应混合优化，有效解决了大型语言模型置信度忠实性问题，在有限监督资源下实现了准确性和置信校准的平衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes tasks, where confident yet incorrect inferences may cause severe real-world harm, bringing the previously overlooked issue of confidence faithfulness back to the forefront. A promising solution is to jointly optimize unsupervised Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) with reasoning-trace-guided Reasoning Distillation (RD), which may face three persistent challenges: scarcity of high-quality training corpora, factually unwarranted overconfidence and indiscriminate fusion that amplifies erroneous updates. Inspired by the human confidence accumulation from uncertainty to certainty, we propose Progressive Reasoning Gain (PRG) to measure whether reasoning steps progressively strengthen support for the final answer. Furthermore, we introduce HyTuning, a hybrid post-training framework that adaptively reweights RD and RLIF via a PRG-style metric, using scarce supervised reasoning traces as a stable anchor while exploiting abundant unlabeled queries for scalability. Experiments on several domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that HyTuning improves accuracy while achieving confidence faithfulness under limited supervision, supporting a practical "Less Approximates More" effect.

</details>


### [152] [A Machine Learning Framework for Turbofan Health Estimation via Inverse Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08460)
*Milad Leyli-Abadi,Lucas Thil,Sebastien Razakarivony,Guillaume Doquet,Jesse Read*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种从涡轮风扇发动机传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标的新方法，引入包含维护事件和使用变化的新数据集，比较稳态与非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器以及自监督学习方法，为健康状态估计问题建立实用基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计是一个具有挑战性的不适定逆问题，受限于稀疏传感和复杂非线性热力学。现有研究存在碎片化问题，使用不现实的数据集且对时间信息利用不足。需要在真实退化与维护模式下从操作传感器数据恢复部件级健康指标。

Method: 1) 引入包含行业导向复杂性（维护事件和使用变化）的新数据集；2) 建立基准比较稳态与非稳态数据驱动模型、贝叶斯滤波器；3) 引入自监督学习方法，在无法获取真实健康标签的情况下学习潜在表示；4) 比较无监督表示与直接预测基线的下游估计性能。

Result: 传统滤波器仍然是强基线，自监督学习方法揭示了健康估计的内在复杂性，突显了对更先进且可解释推理策略的需求。研究为健康状态估计问题建立了实用下界，并提供了可复现的数据集和实现。

Conclusion: 该研究通过新数据集和系统基准测试，揭示了涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计问题的复杂性。传统方法仍具竞争力，但自监督学习方法展示了无监督表示的潜力，同时强调了开发更先进、可解释推理策略的必要性。数据集和代码的公开促进了该领域的可复现研究。

Abstract: Estimating the health state of turbofan engines is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem, hindered by sparse sensing and complex nonlinear thermodynamics. Research in this area remains fragmented, with comparisons limited by the use of unrealistic datasets and insufficient exploration of the exploitation of temporal information. This work investigates how to recover component-level health indicators from operational sensor data under realistic degradation and maintenance patterns. To support this study, we introduce a new dataset that incorporates industry-oriented complexities such as maintenance events and usage changes. Using this dataset, we establish an initial benchmark that compares steady-state and nonstationary data-driven models, and Bayesian filters, classic families of methods used to solve this problem. In addition to this benchmark, we introduce self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches that learn latent representations without access to true health labels, a scenario reflective of real-world operational constraints. By comparing the downstream estimation performance of these unsupervised representations against the direct prediction baselines, we establish a practical lower bound on the difficulty of solving this inverse problem. Our results reveal that traditional filters remain strong baselines, while SSL methods reveal the intrinsic complexity of health estimation and highlight the need for more advanced and interpretable inference strategies. For reproducibility, both the generated dataset and the implementation used in this work are made accessible.

</details>


### [153] [TTVS: Boosting Self-Exploring Reinforcement Learning via Test-time Variational Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08468)
*Sikai Bai,Haoxi Li,Jie Zhang,Yongjiang Liu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTVS框架通过动态合成测试查询的语义等价变体，让大型推理模型在无标注测试数据上自我进化，克服了传统测试时方法依赖静态查询集和RLVR需要昂贵监督的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在专业或新领域面临监督成本过高的问题，而现有测试时方法又受限于静态查询集，容易过拟合文本模式，需要一种能在无标注测试数据上实现自适应的方法。

Method: TTVS包含两个协同模块：1）在线变分合成，将静态测试查询转化为动态的语义等价变体流，迫使模型学习底层问题逻辑而非表面模式；2）测试时混合探索，在合成变体间平衡准确性驱动的利用和一致性驱动的探索。

Result: 在八种模型架构上的广泛实验表明，TTVS仅使用无标注测试数据就超越了其他测试时适应方法，甚至优于使用大量高质量标注数据训练的监督RL技术。

Conclusion: TTVS通过动态合成语义等价变体实现了大型推理模型在无监督测试环境下的有效自适应，为解决专业领域监督稀缺问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) driven by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), this paradigm is fundamentally limited in specialized or novel domains where such supervision is prohibitively expensive or unavailable, posing a key challenge for test-time adaptation. While existing test-time methods offer a potential solution, they are constrained by learning from static query sets, risking overfitting to textual patterns. To address this gap, we introduce Test-Time Variational Synthesis (TTVS), a novel framework that enables LRMs to self-evolve by dynamically augmenting the training stream from unlabeled test queries. TTVS comprises two synergistic modules: (1) Online Variational Synthesis, which transforms static test queries into a dynamic stream of diverse, semantically-equivalent variations, enforcing the model to learn underlying problem logic rather than superficial patterns; (2) Test-time Hybrid Exploration, which balances accuracy-driven exploitation with consistency-driven exploration across synthetic variants. Extensive experiments show TTVS yields superior performance across eight model architectures. Notably, using only unlabeled test-time data, TTVS not only surpasses other test-time adaptation methods but also outperforms state-of-the-art supervised RL-based techniques trained on vast, high-quality labeled data.

</details>


### [154] [Quantization Impact on the Accuracy and Communication Efficiency Trade-off in Federated Learning for Aerospace Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08474)
*Abdelkarim Loukili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了联邦学习中对称均匀量化对航空航天预测性维护模型的影响，发现INT4量化在保持与FP32统计无差异精度的同时，将梯度通信成本降低8倍，而INT2量化则因极端量化导致不稳定而不适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）能够在分布式航空航天机队中实现隐私保护的预测性维护，但梯度通信开销限制了其在带宽受限的物联网节点上的部署。需要研究量化技术如何在保持模型精度的同时降低通信成本。

Method: 使用对称均匀量化（b ∈ {32,8,4,2} bits）对自定义设计的轻量级1-D卷积模型（AeroConv1D，9,697参数）进行联邦学习训练。在NASA C-MAPSS基准数据集上，采用现实的非独立同分布（Non-IID）客户端划分，并进行严格的多次随机种子评估（N=10 seeds）。通过IID与Non-IID划分的直接比较，揭示量化在真实场景下的稳定性表现。

Result: INT4量化在FD001（p=0.341）和FD002（p=0.264 MAE，p=0.534 NASA评分）数据集上达到与FP32统计无差异的精度，同时将每轮梯度通信成本从37.88 KiB降低到4.73 KiB（8倍减少）。INT2量化虽然在某些指标上表现更好，但导致NASA评分极度不稳定（变异系数45.8% vs FP32的22.3%），在异构操作条件下不可重现。Xilinx ZCU102 FPGA资源分析显示INT4可在硬件约束内实现（85.5% DSP利用率）。

Conclusion: INT4量化是航空航天联邦学习部署的可行方案，在保持精度的同时显著降低通信开销。研究强调了正确评估方法的重要性：非IID客户端划分能揭示极端量化在实际部署中的不稳定性，而IID划分会人为抑制方差。INT2量化因导致不可接受的性能不稳定而不适用于实际部署。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving predictive maintenance across distributed aerospace fleets, but gradient communication overhead constrains deployment on bandwidth-limited IoT nodes. This paper investigates the impact of symmetric uniform quantization ($b \in \{32,8,4,2\}$ bits) on the accuracy--efficiency trade-off of a custom-designed lightweight 1-D convolutional model (AeroConv1D, 9\,697 parameters) trained via FL on the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark under a realistic Non-IID client partition. Using a rigorous multi-seed evaluation ($N=10$ seeds), we show that INT4 achieves accuracy \emph{statistically indistinguishable} from FP32 on both FD001 ($p=0.341$) and FD002 ($p=0.264$ MAE, $p=0.534$ NASA score) while delivering an $8\times$ reduction in gradient communication cost (37.88~KiB $\to$ 4.73~KiB per round). A key methodological finding is that naïve IID client partitioning artificially suppresses variance; correct Non-IID evaluation reveals the true operational instability of extreme quantization, demonstrated via a direct empirical IID vs.\ Non-IID comparison. INT2 is empirically characterized as unsuitable: while it achieves lower MAE on FD002 through extreme quantization-induced over-regularization, this apparent gain is accompanied by catastrophic NASA score instability (CV\,=\,45.8\% vs.\ 22.3\% for FP32), confirming non-reproducibility under heterogeneous operating conditions. Analytical FPGA resource projections on the Xilinx ZCU102 confirm that INT4 fits within hardware constraints (85.5\% DSP utilization), potentially enabling a complete FL pipeline on a single SoC. The full simulation codebase and FPGA estimation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/therealdeadbeef/aerospace-fl-quantization.

</details>


### [155] [What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08524)
*Stephen Cheng,Sarah Wiegreffe,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过多令牌激活修补框架分析LLM中转向向量的因果机制，发现不同转向方法在相同层使用功能可互换的电路，转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，可稀疏化90-99%仍保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管转向向量是大型语言模型对齐的高效技术，但缺乏对其工作原理的可解释性理解——具体影响哪些内部机制以及如何导致不同模型输出。研究旨在揭示转向向量有效性的因果机制。

Method: 提出多令牌激活修补框架，以拒绝行为为案例进行综合研究。分析不同转向方法在相同层的功能可互换电路，数学分解转向OV电路以揭示语义可解释概念，并测试转向向量稀疏化效果。

Result: 发现转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而基本忽略QK电路——冻结所有注意力分数仅使性能下降8.75%。转向向量可稀疏化90-99%仍保持大部分性能，不同转向方法在重要维度子集上一致。

Conclusion: 转向向量通过特定电路机制影响模型行为，主要作用于OV电路而非QK电路，具有高度稀疏性潜力。这些发现为理解转向向量工作机制提供了可解释性框架，并展示了优化转向向量的可能性。

Abstract: Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Optimization of 32-bit Unsigned Division by Constants on 64-bit Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07902)
*Shigeo Mitsunari,Takashi Hoshino*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出针对64位CPU的32位无符号整数除以常数的优化方法，相比现有32位CPU优化的GM方法，在64位架构上实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GM方法及其改进版本主要针对32位CPU设计，在64位CPU上无法充分利用硬件能力。例如，对于x/7这样的操作，现有编译器生成的代码仍然是32位优化的，没有发挥64位架构的优势。

Method: 提出针对64位CPU的32位无符号整数除以常数的优化方法，为LLVM和GCC编译器实现补丁，专门优化64位架构上的32位除法操作。

Result: 在Intel Xeon w9-3495X (Sapphire Rapids)上实现1.67倍加速，在Apple M4 (Apple M-series SoC)上实现1.98倍加速。LLVM补丁已合并到llvm:main主干，证明了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 针对64位CPU重新设计32位无符号整数除以常数的优化方法是有效的，能够显著提升性能，并且已被主流编译器采纳，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Granlund and Montgomery proposed an optimization method for unsigned integer division by constants [3]. Their method (called the GM method in this paper) was further improved in part by works such as [1] and [7], and is now adopted by major compilers including GCC, Clang, Microsoft Compiler, and Apple Clang. However, for example, for x/7, the generated code is designed for 32-bit CPUs and therefore does not fully exploit 64-bit capabilities. This paper proposes an optimization method for 32-bit unsigned division by constants targeting 64-bit CPUs. We implemented patches for LLVM/GCC and achieved speedups of 1.67x on Intel Xeon w9-3495X (Sapphire Rapids) and 1.98x on Apple M4 (Apple M-series SoC) in the microbenchmark described later. The LLVM patch has already been merged into llvm:main [6], demonstrating the practical applicability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [157] [PG-MDP: Profile-Guided Memory Dependence Prediction for Area-Constrained Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08445)
*Luke Panayi,Johan Jino,Sebastian S. Kim,Alberto Ros,Alexandra Jimborean,Jim Whittaker,Martin Berger,Paul Kelly*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: PG-MDP通过软件协同设计标记内存独立加载指令，使其绕过MDP查询，减少小面积核心中MDP表的假依赖问题，实现接近大预测器性能而无面积开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面积受限核心（如能效或边缘系统）的MDP表容量有限，导致内存独立加载与无关预测器条目产生别名冲突，造成高假依赖率和处理器流水线不必要停顿。传统增大预测器规模的方法不适用于面积受限核心。

Method: 提出基于配置文件的MDP（PG-MDP）软件协同设计方法：通过配置文件分析识别持续内存独立的加载指令，根据其操作码进行标记，从MDP工作集中移除这些指令。标记的加载指令在调度时绕过MDP查询，始终尽快发出。

Result: 在SPEC2017 CPU intspeed基准测试中，PG-MDP将MDP查询率降低79%，假依赖减少77%，小型模拟核心的几何平均IPC提升1.47%（接近使用16倍预测器条目的性能，差距仅0.5%），无面积成本和额外指令带宽开销。

Conclusion: 针对预测器工作集进行优化与增大预测器规模同样有效。PG-MDP通过软件协同设计识别并移除内存独立加载指令，使小预测器实现接近大预测器性能，解决了面积受限核心的MDP假依赖问题。

Abstract: Memory Dependence Prediction (MDP) is a speculative technique to determine which stores, if any, a given load will depend on. Area-constrained cores are increasingly relevant in various applications such as energy-efficient or edge systems, and often have limited space for MDP tables. This leads to a high rate of false dependencies as memory independent loads alias with unrelated predictor entries, causing unnecessary stalls in the processor pipeline.
  The conventional way to address this problem is with greater predictor size or complexity, but this is unattractive on area-constrained cores. This paper proposes that targeting the predictor working set is as effective as growing the predictor, and can deliver performance competitive with large predictors while still using very small predictors. This paper introduces profile-guided memory dependence prediction (PG-MDP), a software co-design to label consistently memory independent loads via their opcode and remove them from the MDP working set. These loads bypass querying the MDP when dispatched and always issue as soon as possible. Across SPEC2017 CPU intspeed, PG-MDP reduces the rate of MDP queries by 79%, false dependencies by 77%, and improves geomean IPC for a small simulated core by 1.47% (to within 0.5% of using 16x the predictor entries), with no area cost and no additional instruction bandwidth.

</details>


<div id='cs.GL'></div>

# cs.GL [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Jean-Raymond Abrial: A Scientific Biography of a Formal Methods Pioneer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07353)
*Jonathan P. Bowen,Henri Habrias*

Main category: cs.GL

TL;DR: 关于Jean-Raymond Abrial学术传记的论文，追溯其形式化方法贡献从Z、B方法到Event-B的演进历程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Abrial是软件和系统工程形式化方法发展的核心人物，在五十多年的职业生涯中，他在Z规范表示法、B方法和Event-B的创建以及工业应用方面发挥了决定性作用。本文旨在提供对其生平与工作的学术传记性记述。

Method: 采用学术传记方法，追溯Abrial思想从早期实时语言和数据库工作，到形式化规范、精化和证明的基础性贡献，再到工业级工具支持（如Atelier B和Rodin平台）的发展历程。将Abrial的贡献置于历史、学术和工业背景中进行考察。

Result: 论文系统梳理了Abrial在形式化方法领域的完整发展轨迹，展示了其思想从理论到工业实践的演进过程，包括对Z、B方法和Event-B等关键技术的决定性贡献。

Conclusion: Abrial的贡献对软件工程和程序形式化推理产生了持久影响，他的工作成功地将形式化方法从理论概念转化为工业可用的工具和方法论，推动了形式化方法在大型工业系统中的应用。

Abstract: Jean-Raymond Abrial is one of the central figures in the development of formal methods for software and systems engineering. Over a career spanning more than five decades, he has played a decisive role in the creation of the Z specification notation, the B-Method, and Event-B, and in demonstrating their applicability to large-scale industrial systems. This paper presents a scholarly biographical account of Abrial's life and work, tracing the evolution of his ideas from early work on real-time languages and databases, through foundational contributions to formal specification, refinement, and proof, to the development of industrial-strength tool support such as the Atelier~B and the Rodin platform. The paper situates Abrial's contributions within their historical, intellectual, and industrial contexts, and assesses their lasting impact on software engineering and formal reasoning about programs.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Sima 1.0: A Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Documentary Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07721)
*Zhao Song*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Sima 1.0是一个多智能体系统，用于优化长纪录片视频的周更生产流程，通过人机协作显著减少人工工作量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要视频分享平台的内容创作需要大量人工劳动，特别是时长1-2小时的长纪录片视频，现有制作流程效率低下，难以维持严格的周更发布计划

Method: 采用多智能体系统框架，将生产过程划分为11步流水线，分配给混合劳动力。基础创意任务和物理录制由人类操作员执行，而耗时的编辑、字幕精炼和补充资产集成则委托给专门的初级和高级AI智能体

Result: 通过系统化从脚本注释到最终资产导出的任务，Sima 1.0显著减少了生产工作量，使单个创作者能够高效地维持严格的周更发布计划

Conclusion: Sima 1.0成功构建了一个人机协作的视频生产系统，通过任务分工和自动化优化了长纪录片视频的生产流程，为创作者提供了可持续的高效内容生产解决方案

Abstract: Content creation for major video-sharing platforms demands significant manual labor, particularly for long-form documentary videos spanning one to two hours. In this work, we introduce Sima 1.0, a multi-agent system designed to optimize the weekly production pipeline for high-quality video generation. The framework partitions the production process into an 11-step pipeline distributed across a hybrid workforce. While foundational creative tasks and physical recording are executed by a human operator, time-intensive editing, caption refinement, and supplementary asset integration are delegated to specialized junior and senior-level AI agents. By systematizing tasks from script annotation to final asset exportation, Sima 1.0 significantly reduces the production workload, empowering a single creator to efficiently sustain a rigorous weekly publishing schedule.

</details>


### [160] [ORACLE-SWE: Quantifying the Contribution of Oracle Information Signals on SWE Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07789)
*Kenan Li,Qirui Jin,Liao Zhu,Xiaosong Huang,Yijia Wu,Yikai Zhang,Xin Zhang,Zijian Jin,Yufan Huang,Elsie Nallipogu,Chaoyun Zhang,Yu Kang,Saravan Rajmohan,Qingwei Lin,Wenke Lee,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出Oracle-SWE方法，用于分离和提取软件工程基准中的oracle信息信号，量化每个信号对智能体性能的影响，指导自主编码系统的研究优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究提出了多种智能体工作流程和训练策略，并分析了智能体系统在软件工程任务上的失败模式，重点关注了多个上下文信息信号（如复制测试、回归测试、编辑位置、执行上下文、API使用等）。然而，每个信号对整体成功的个体贡献仍未充分探索，特别是在完美获取中间信息时各信号的理想贡献。

Method: 引入Oracle-SWE方法：1）从软件工程基准中分离和提取oracle信息信号；2）量化每个信号对智能体性能的影响；3）评估强语言模型提取的信号提供给基础智能体时的性能增益，近似真实世界任务解决场景。

Result: 通过Oracle-SWE方法能够量化各信息信号对智能体性能的贡献程度，评估强语言模型提取信号的实际效果，为自主编码系统的研究优先级提供指导。

Conclusion: Oracle-SWE方法填补了现有研究的空白，通过系统性地分离和评估各信息信号对智能体性能的影响，为自主编码系统的研发提供了重要的指导框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in language model (LM) agents have significantly improved automated software engineering (SWE). Prior work has proposed various agentic workflows and training strategies as well as analyzed failure modes of agentic systems on SWE tasks, focusing on several contextual information signals: Reproduction Test, Regression Test, Edit Location, Execution Context, and API Usage. However, the individual contribution of each signal to overall success remains underexplored, particularly their ideal contribution when intermediate information is perfectly obtained. To address this gap, we introduce Oracle-SWE, a unified method to isolate and extract oracle information signals from SWE benchmarks and quantify the impact of each signal on agent performance. To further validate the pattern, we evaluate the performance gain of signals extracted by strong LMs when provided to a base agent, approximating real-world task-resolution settings. These evaluations aim to guide research prioritization for autonomous coding systems.

</details>


### [161] [Open-Ended Video Game Glitch Detection with Agentic Reasoning and Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07818)
*Muyang Zheng,Tong Zhou,Geyang Wu,Zihao Lin,Haibo Wang,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了开放世界视频游戏故障检测任务，要求从连续游戏视频中识别故障、用自然语言描述并定位发生时间，并引入了首个带时间定位的基准VideoGlitchBench和GliDe智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏故障理解任务主要局限于图像级识别或封闭式问答，无法处理连续游戏视频中需要理解游戏特定动态（如机制、物理、渲染、动画、状态转换）并区分真实故障与异常但有效游戏事件的复杂场景。

Method: 提出了GliDe智能体框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 游戏感知上下文记忆用于知情推理；2) 基于辩论的反射器实现多视角故障检测与验证；3) 事件级定位模块从碎片化时间证据中恢复完整故障区间。同时设计了VideoGlitchBench基准，包含5,238个来自120款游戏的视频，每个都标注了详细故障描述和精确时间跨度。

Result: 实验表明该任务对当前多模态模型极具挑战性，而GliDe框架相比对应的基线模型实现了显著更强的性能。论文还设计了专门的任务评估协议，联合衡量语义保真度和时间准确性。

Conclusion: 开放世界视频游戏故障检测是一个需要复杂推理的新兴任务，VideoGlitchBench为统一评估语义理解和时间定位提供了首个基准，GliDe框架通过游戏感知记忆、辩论式验证和事件级定位组件有效提升了性能，为游戏质量保证和玩家体验分析开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Open-ended video game glitch detection aims to identify glitches in gameplay videos, describe them in natural language, and localize when they occur. Unlike conventional game glitch understanding tasks which have largely been framed as image-level recognition or closed-form question answering, this task requires reasoning about game-specific dynamics such as mechanics, physics, rendering, animation, and expected state transitions directly over continuous gameplay videos and distinguishing true glitches from unusual but valid in-game events. To support this task, we introduce VideoGlitchBench, the first benchmark for open-ended video game glitch detection with temporal localization. VideoGlitchBench contains 5,238 gameplay videos from 120 games, each annotated with detailed glitch descriptions and precise temporal spans, enabling unified evaluation of semantic understanding and temporal grounding. We further propose GliDe, an agentic framework with three key components: a game-aware contextual memory for informed reasoning, a debate-based reflector for multi-perspective glitch detection and verification, and an event-level grounding module that recovers complete glitch intervals from fragmented temporal evidence. We also design a task-specific evaluation protocol that jointly measures semantic fidelity and temporal accuracy. Experiments show that this task remains highly challenging for current multimodal models, while GliDe achieves substantially stronger performance than corresponding vanilla model baselines.

</details>


### [162] [More Capable, Less Cooperative? When LLMs Fail At Zero-Cost Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07821)
*Advait Yadav,Sid Black,Oliver Sourbut*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM智能体在多智能体系统中合作失败的研究：即使帮助他人无成本且有明确指令，智能体仍不合作，能力与合作性无关，需要专门设计合作机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM智能体在多智能体系统中的合作行为，特别是在帮助他人既无个人成本也无个人收益的情况下，智能体是否会遵循明确指令进行合作。现实中许多协调问题（如知识共享、代码文档）都存在类似情况，但LLM智能体在此类无摩擦环境中的合作行为尚不清楚。

Method: 构建专门研究合作行为的多智能体实验环境，消除合作的所有战略复杂性。通过因果分解方法自动化智能体通信的一方，分离合作失败与能力失败。分析智能体推理过程，测试针对性干预措施（如明确协议、微小共享激励）。

Result: 能力不预测合作性：OpenAI o3仅达到最优集体性能的17%，而OpenAI o3-mini达到50%，尽管有相同的最大化群体收益指令。明确协议使低能力模型性能翻倍，微小共享激励改善弱合作模型。合作失败与能力失败通过推理分析可追溯其根源。

Conclusion: 仅扩展智能无法解决多智能体系统中的协调问题，即使帮助他人无成本，也需要有意识的合作设计。合作失败在LLM智能体中普遍存在，需要专门机制而非仅依赖指令或能力提升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly coordinate in multi-agent systems, yet we lack an understanding of where and why cooperation failures may arise. In many real-world coordination problems, from knowledge sharing in organizations to code documentation, helping others carries negligible personal cost while generating substantial collective benefits. However, whether LLM agents cooperate when helping neither benefits nor harms the helper, while being given explicit instructions to do so, remains unknown. We build a multi-agent setup designed to study cooperative behavior in a frictionless environment, removing all strategic complexity from cooperation. We find that capability does not predict cooperation: OpenAI o3 achieves only 17% of optimal collective performance while OpenAI o3-mini reaches 50%, despite identical instructions to maximize group revenue. Through a causal decomposition that automates one side of agent communication, we separate cooperation failures from competence failures, tracing their origins through agent reasoning analysis. Testing targeted interventions, we find that explicit protocols double performance for low-competence models, and tiny sharing incentives improve models with weak cooperation. Our findings suggest that scaling intelligence alone will not solve coordination problems in multi-agent systems and will require deliberate cooperative design, even when helping others costs nothing.

</details>


### [163] [Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping: Agent-Triggered Focus Sessions for Isolated Per-Agent Steering in Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07911)
*Nickson Patel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DACS是一种动态注意力上下文范围机制，通过两种不对称模式解决多智能体LLM编排系统中的上下文污染问题，显著提升决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM编排系统存在上下文污染问题：当N个并发智能体竞争编排器的上下文窗口时，每个智能体的任务状态、部分输出和待处理问题会污染其他智能体的引导交互，从而降低决策质量。

Method: 提出动态注意力上下文范围（DACS）机制，编排器在两种不对称模式下运行：注册模式（Registry mode）仅保存轻量级智能体状态摘要（≤200个标记）；当智能体发出SteeringRequest时，编排器进入Focus(a_i)模式，注入智能体a_i的完整上下文，同时压缩其他智能体到其注册条目。上下文隔离是智能体触发的、不对称且确定性的。

Result: 在四个实验阶段共200次试验中，DACS在8个合成场景中实现90.0-98.4%的引导准确率，而扁平上下文基线仅为21.0-60.0%（p<0.0001）。错误智能体污染从28-57%降至0-14%，上下文效率比高达3.53倍。准确率优势随N和D增长而增加，LLM-as-judge验证关键词匹配（平均kappa=0.909）。

Conclusion: DACS通过动态、智能体触发的上下文范围机制有效解决了多智能体LLM编排中的上下文污染问题，显著提升了决策质量，且优势随智能体数量和决策密度增加而增强。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM orchestration systems suffer from context pollution: when N concurrent agents compete for the orchestrator's context window, each agent's task state, partial outputs, and pending questions contaminate the steering interactions of every other agent, degrading decision quality. We introduce Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping (DACS), a mechanism in which the orchestrator operates in two asymmetric modes. In Registry mode it holds only lightweight per-agent status summaries (<=200 tokens each), remaining responsive to all agents and the user. When an agent emits a SteeringRequest, the orchestrator enters Focus(a_i) mode, injecting the full context of agent a_i while compressing all other agents to their registry entries. Context isolation is agent-triggered, asymmetric, and deterministic: the context window contains exactly F(a_i) + R_{-i} during steering, eliminating cross-agent contamination without requiring context compression or retrieval. We evaluate DACS across four experimental phases totalling 200 trials: Phase 1 tests N in {3,5,10} (60 trials); Phase 2 tests agent heterogeneity and adversarial dependencies (60 trials); Phase 3 tests decision density up to D=15 (40 trials); Phase 4 uses autonomous LLM agents for free-form questions (40 trials, Claude Haiku 4.5). Across all 8 synthetic scenarios, DACS achieves 90.0--98.4% steering accuracy versus 21.0--60.0% for a flat-context baseline (p < 0.0001 throughout), with wrong-agent contamination falling from 28--57% to 0--14% and context efficiency ratios of up to 3.53x. The accuracy advantage grows with N and D; keyword matching is validated by LLM-as-judge across all phases (mean kappa=0.909). DACS outperforms the flat-context baseline by +17.2pp at N=3 (p=0.0023) and +20.4pp at N=5 (p=0.0008) in Phase 4, with the advantage growing with N confirmed by two independent judges.

</details>


### [164] ["Theater of Mind" for LLMs: A Cognitive Architecture Based on Global Workspace Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08206)
*Wenlong Shang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: GWA（全局工作空间智能体）是一种受全局工作空间理论启发的认知架构，将多智能体协调从被动数据结构转变为主动的事件驱动离散动力系统，通过熵驱动的内在动机机制和双层记忆分叉策略实现持续的自导向LLM智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型（LLMs）本质上是有限输入有限输出（BIBO）系统，处于被动状态直到被明确提示，仅计算局部响应而缺乏内在时间连续性。虽然对孤立任务有效，但这种反应式范式对工程化自主人工智能构成关键瓶颈。现有的多智能体框架尝试分配认知负荷，但通常依赖静态内存池和被动消息传递，导致在长时间执行中出现认知停滞和同质化死锁。

Method: 提出全局工作空间智能体（GWA）架构：1）将多智能体协调转变为主动的事件驱动离散动力系统；2）通过中央广播枢纽与功能受限的异质智能体群耦合，维持连续认知循环；3）引入基于熵的内在驱动机制，数学量化语义多样性，动态调节生成温度以自主打破推理死锁；4）采用双层记忆分叉策略确保长期认知连续性。

Result: GWA提供了一个稳健、可复现的工程框架，用于实现持续、自导向的LLM智能。该架构解决了现有多智能体系统的结构限制，能够避免认知停滞和同质化死锁，支持长时间的自主运行。

Conclusion: GWA通过将全局工作空间理论应用于多智能体协调，创建了一个主动的、事件驱动的认知架构，结合熵驱动的内在动机和双层记忆策略，为工程化可持续的自主LLM智能提供了系统化解决方案，克服了传统反应式范式的局限性。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) operate fundamentally as Bounded-Input Bounded-Output (BIBO) systems. They remain in a passive state until explicitly prompted, computing localized responses without intrinsic temporal continuity. While effective for isolated tasks, this reactive paradigm presents a critical bottleneck for engineering autonomous artificial intelligence. Current multi-agent frameworks attempt to distribute cognitive load but frequently rely on static memory pools and passive message passing, which inevitably leads to cognitive stagnation and homogeneous deadlocks during extended execution. To address this structural limitation, we propose Global Workspace Agents (GWA), a cognitive architecture inspired by Global Workspace Theory. GWA transitions multi-agent coordination from a passive data structure to an active, event-driven discrete dynamical system. By coupling a central broadcast hub with a heterogeneous swarm of functionally constrained agents, the system maintains a continuous cognitive cycle. Furthermore, we introduce an entropy-based intrinsic drive mechanism that mathematically quantifies semantic diversity, dynamically regulating generation temperature to autonomously break reasoning deadlocks. Coupled with a dual-layer memory bifurcation strategy to ensure long-term cognitive continuity, GWA provides a robust, reproducible engineering framework for sustained, self-directed LLM agency.

</details>


### [165] [MemCoT: Test-Time Scaling through Memory-Driven Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08216)
*Haodong Lei,Junming Liu,Yirong Chen,Ding Wang,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MemCoT是一个测试时内存扩展框架，通过将长上下文推理转化为迭代式、有状态的信息搜索过程，解决了LLM在长上下文推理中的幻觉和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理大规模、碎片化长上下文时存在严重幻觉和灾难性遗忘问题，现有内存机制通常将检索视为静态、单步的被动匹配过程，导致语义稀释和上下文碎片化。

Method: MemCoT引入多视角长期记忆感知模块（Zoom-In证据定位和Zoom-Out上下文扩展），以及任务条件化的双短期记忆系统（语义状态记忆和情节轨迹记忆），将长上下文推理转化为迭代式、有状态的信息搜索过程。

Result: MemCoT在LoCoMo基准和LongMemEval-S基准上实现了最先进的性能，多个开源和闭源模型在MemCoT赋能下达到了SOTA水平。

Conclusion: MemCoT通过重新定义推理过程，将长上下文推理转化为迭代式信息搜索，有效解决了LLM在长上下文推理中的幻觉和遗忘问题，为长上下文处理提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still suffer from severe hallucinations and catastrophic forgetting during causal reasoning over massive, fragmented long contexts. Existing memory mechanisms typically treat retrieval as a static, single-step passive matching process, leading to severe semantic dilution and contextual fragmentation. To overcome these fundamental bottlenecks, we propose MemCoT, a test-time memory scaling framework that redefines the reasoning process by transforming long-context reasoning into an iterative, stateful information search. MemCoT introduces a multi-view long-term memory perception module that enables Zoom-In evidence localization and Zoom-Out contextual expansion, allowing the model to first identify where relevant evidence resides and then reconstruct the surrounding causal structure necessary for reasoning. In addition, MemCoT employs a task-conditioned dual short-term memory system composed of semantic state memory and episodic trajectory memory. This short-term memory records historical search decisions and dynamically guides query decomposition and pruning across iterations. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemCoT establishes a state-of-the-art performance. Empowered by MemCoT, several open- and closed-source models achieve SOTA performance on the LoCoMo benchmark and LongMemEval-S benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [When Switching Algorithms Helps: A Theoretical Study of Online Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07473)
*Denis Antipov,Carola Doerr*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 首次理论证明在线算法选择能在OneMax问题上实现渐进加速，通过切换(1+λ) EA和(1+(λ,λ)) GA达到O(n log log n)期望时间，优于单独使用任一算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然在线算法选择在实证研究中表现优异，但缺乏理论证明其能实现渐进加速，且缺少理论指导何时及如何切换算法。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在OneMax问题上，研究(1+λ) EA和(1+(λ,λ)) GA两种算法的切换策略。首先在理想切换时间下分析性能，然后提出实际可行的切换策略，结合固定起点和固定目标视角分析。

Result: 证明通过适当选择两种算法的种群规模，并在合适时间切换，可以达到O(n log log n)期望运行时间，优于单独使用任一算法的最优调参性能Θ(n√(log n log log log n / log log n))。

Conclusion: 首次为在线算法选择提供了理论上的渐进加速证明，展示了不同算法在优化过程不同阶段的优势，为深入理解在线算法选择开辟了有前景的研究路径。

Abstract: Online algorithm selection (OAS) aims to adapt the optimization process to changes in the fitness landscape and is expected to outperform any single algorithm from a given portfolio. Although this expectation is supported by numerous empirical studies, there are currently no theoretical results proving that OAS can yield asymptotic speedups (apart from some artificial examples for hyper-heuristics). Moreover, theory-based guidelines for when and how to switch between algorithms are largely missing.
  In this paper, we present the first theoretical example in which switching between two algorithms -- the $(1+λ)$ EA and the $(1+(λ,λ))$ GA -- solves the OneMax problem asymptotically faster than either algorithm used in isolation. We show that an appropriate choice of population sizes for the two algorithms allows the optimum to be reached in $O(n\log\log n)$ expected time, faster than the $Θ(n\sqrt{\frac{\log n \log\log\log n}{\log\log n}})$ runtime of the best of these two algorithms with optimally tuned parameters.
  We first establish this bound under an idealized switching rule that changes from the $(1+λ)$ to the $(1+(λ,λ))$ GA at the optimal time. We then propose a realistic switching strategy that achieves the same performance. Our analysis combines fixed-start and fixed-target perspectives, illustrating how different algorithms dominate at different stages of the optimization process. This approach offers a promising path toward a deeper theoretical understanding of OAS.

</details>


### [167] [Analysis of Search Heuristics in the Multi-Armed Bandit Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08109)
*Jasmin Brandt,Barbara Hammer,Timo Kötzing,Jurek Sander*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文分析了进化算法和分布估计算法在双人赌博机问题中识别Condorcet赢家的能力，发现(1+1)EA表现较差，而基于最大最小蚂蚁系统的EDA表现更好，并提出通过重复对决来改进(1+1)EA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同搜索启发式算法在探索/利用权衡中的表现，特别是在双人赌博机设置下识别Condorcet赢家的能力。进化算法和分布估计算法都通过比较不同选项（个体/臂）来工作，因此需要理解它们在识别最优解方面的差异。

Method: 采用双人赌博机设置，每个臂对其他臂有固定的获胜概率。分析(1+1)进化算法和基于最大最小蚂蚁系统的分布估计算法在识别Condorcet赢家方面的性能。特别研究了当Condorcet赢家以概率1-p击败其他臂时的算法表现。

Result: (1+1)EA表现较差：即使Condorcet赢家以1-p的概率击败其他臂，当p=Ω(1/n)时，其平稳分布中选择Condorcet赢家的概率仅为常数。相比之下，基于最大最小蚂蚁系统的EDA在其维护的分布中选择Condorcet赢家的概率为1-Θ(p)。通过重复对决可以显著提高(1+1)EA在平稳分布中选择Condorcet赢家的概率。

Conclusion: 进化算法在识别Condorcet赢家方面表现不佳，而分布估计算法表现更好。对于(1+1)EA，通过重复对决可以显著改善其性能。这为理解不同搜索启发式算法在探索/利用权衡中的行为提供了理论见解。

Abstract: We consider the classic Multi-Armed Bandit setting to understand the exploration/exploitation tradeoffs made by different search heuristics. Since many search heuristics work by comparing different options (in evolutionary algorithms called "individuals"; in the Bandit literature called "arms"), we work with the "Dueling Bandits" setting. In each iteration, a comparison between different arms can be made; in the binary stochastic setting, each arm has a fixed winning probability against any other arm. A Condorcet winner is any arm that beats every other arm with a probability strictly higher than $1/2$.
  We show that evolutionary algorithms are rather bad at identifying the Condorcet winner: Even if the Condorcet winner beats every other arm with a probability $1-p$, the (1+1) EA, in its stationary distribution, chooses the Condorcet winner only with constant probability if $p=Ω(1/n)$. By contrast, we show that a simple EDA (based on the Max-Min Ant System with iteration-best update) will choose the Condorcet winner in its maintained distribution with probability $1-Θ(p)$. As a remedy for the (1+1) EA, we show how repeated duels can significantly boost the probability of the Condorcet winner in the stationary distribution.

</details>


### [168] [Exploration of Pareto-preserving Search Space Transformations in Multi-objective Test Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08173)
*Diedeerick Vermetten,Jeroen Rook*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文强调在多目标优化基准问题中引入搜索空间变换的重要性，通过参数化双射变换创建不同实例，并分析其对算法性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化基准问题设计主要关注目标空间结构，而搜索空间结构相对被忽视。单目标优化中已广泛采用搜索空间变换来防止算法利用意外结构，但在多目标优化中这一做法尚未得到足够重视。需要解决在边界约束问题中添加变换而不影响目标空间结构的挑战。

Method: 使用两种参数化的双射变换来创建流行基准问题的不同实例。这些变换旨在改变搜索空间结构而不影响目标空间结构。同时还将参数化变换应用于目标空间，并比较各自的性能影响。

Result: 展示了搜索空间变换如何影响各种多目标优化算法的性能。通过变换创建的基准问题实例能够揭示算法对搜索空间结构的敏感性，为算法评估提供更全面的视角。

Conclusion: 搜索空间变换在多目标优化基准设计中至关重要，能够防止算法利用意外结构，提供更公平和全面的算法评估。参数化变换方法可同时应用于搜索空间和目标空间，为基准问题设计提供了灵活的工具。

Abstract: Benchmark problems are an important tool for gaining understanding of optimization algorithms. Since algorithms often aim to perform well on benchmarks, biases in benchmark design provide misleading insights. In single-objective optimization, for example, many problems used to have their optimum in the center of the search domain. To remedy these issues, search space transformations have been widely adopted by benchmark suites, preventing algorithms from exploiting unintended structure.
  In multi-objective optimization, problem design has focused primarily on the objective space structure. While this focus addresses important aspects of the multi-objective nature of the problems, the search space structures of these problems have received comparatively limited attention. In this work, we re-emphasize the importance of transformations in the search space, and address the challenges inherent in adding transformations to boundary constraints problems without impacting the structure of the objective space. We utilized two parameterized, bijective transformations to create different instantiations of popular benchmark problems, and show how these changes impact the performance of various multi-objective optimization algorithms. In addition to the search space transformations, we show that such parameterized transformations can also be applied to the objective space, and compare their respective performance impacts.

</details>


### [169] [Robust Multi-Objective Optimization for Bicycle Rebalancing in Shared Mobility Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08296)
*Diego Daniel Pedroza-Perez,Gabriel Luque,Sergio Nesmachnow,Jamal Toutouh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 研究基于码头自行车共享系统的静态夜间再平衡问题，考虑需求不确定性，通过三目标优化最小化总行驶距离、期望未满足需求和高峰需求场景下的鲁棒性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 码头自行车共享系统存在供需空间失衡问题，通常通过夜间卡车再平衡解决。现有方法需要处理需求不确定性，并平衡运营成本和服务质量。

Method: 将问题建模为三目标优化问题，使用非支配排序遗传算法II（NSGA-II）求解，采用置换-分区编码和领域特定的重定位算子，包括基于偏置最佳改进的站点重定位算子。

Result: 在巴塞罗那Bicing系统（460个站点）上的实验显示，算法能生成分布良好的帕累托解集，对参考非支配集有显著贡献。贪婪构造基线主要产生极端解且常被支配。

Conclusion: 提出的多目标优化方法能有效处理自行车共享系统再平衡问题中的需求不确定性，在运营成本、期望服务质量和鲁棒性之间提供有意义的权衡方案。

Abstract: Dock-based bike-sharing systems exhibit spatial imbalances between bicycle supply and user demand, often addressed through overnight truck-based rebalancing. This work studies static overnight rebalancing under demand uncertainty modeled as a tri-objective optimization problem. The objectives minimize total travel distance, expected unmet demand, and a robustness-oriented unmet demand measure over high-demand scenarios.
  Route plans are evaluated via a recourse simulation that enforces truck loads and station capacity constraints across multiple demand realizations. The robustness objective supports selecting plans that reduce peak-demand service degradation. Trade-off solutions are approximated with Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II using a permutation--partition encoding and domain-specific relocation operators, including a biased best-improvement move for station relocation.
  Experiments on the real Barcelona Bicing system with 460 stations show well-distributed Pareto sets and substantial contributions to the reference non-dominated set. Greedy constructive baselines mainly yield extreme solutions and are often dominated.

</details>


### [170] [Multi-Modal Learning meets Genetic Programming: Analyzing Alignment in Latent Space Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08324)
*Benjamin Léger,Kazem Meidani,Christian Gagné*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 研究发现SNIP模型在符号回归中的跨模态对齐过于粗糙，无法实现有效的对齐引导优化，揭示了细粒度对齐是未来工作的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNIP模型通过对比预训练将符号表达式映射到连续空间，声称能够实现符号和数值编码器在共享潜在空间中的对齐，从而在数值空间进行优化来隐式指导符号搜索。但类似CLIP的模型研究表明这种对齐通常是粗粒度的，需要验证SNIP是否真正实现了有效的双模态优化。

Method: 通过实验研究SNIP模型在符号回归中的表现，具体分析：1）优化过程中跨模态对齐是否改善；2）SNIP学习的对齐是否足够精细以在符号空间进行有原则的搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）即使适应度提高，跨模态对齐在优化过程中并未改善；2）SNIP学习的对齐过于粗糙，无法在符号空间进行高效的、有原则的搜索。

Conclusion: 虽然多模态潜在空间优化在符号回归中具有显著潜力，但有效的对齐引导优化在实践中尚未实现。细粒度对齐是实现这一目标的关键方向，需要未来研究解决。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) aims to discover mathematical expressions from data, a task traditionally tackled using Genetic Programming (GP) through combinatorial search over symbolic structures. Latent Space Optimization (LSO) methods use neural encoders to map symbolic expressions into continuous spaces, transforming the combinatorial search into continuous optimization. SNIP (Meidani et al., 2024), a contrastive pre-training model inspired by CLIP, advances LSO by introducing a multi-modal approach: aligning symbolic and numeric encoders in a shared latent space to learn the phenotype-genotype mapping, enabling optimization in the numeric space to implicitly guide symbolic search. However, this relies on fine-grained cross-modal alignment, whereas literature on similar models like CLIP reveals that such an alignment is typically coarse-grained. In this paper, we investigate whether SNIP delivers on its promise of effective bi-modal optimization for SR. Our experiments show that: (1) cross-modal alignment does not improve during optimization, even as fitness increases, and (2) the alignment learned by SNIP is too coarse to efficiently conduct principled search in the symbolic space. These findings reveal that while multi-modal LSO holds significant potential for SR, effective alignment-guided optimization remains unrealized in practice, highlighting fine-grained alignment as a critical direction for future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [SynQL: A Controllable and Scalable Rule-Based Framework for SQL Workload Synthesis for Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08021)
*Kahan Mehta,Amit Mankodi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SynQL是一个确定性SQL工作负载合成框架，通过遍历数据库外键图生成结构多样、可执行的SQL查询，解决了真实查询数据获取困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私法规限制，获取真实SQL工作负载越来越困难，现有合成工具存在模式幻觉和拓扑塌陷问题，无法为学习型查询优化器提供足够的训练数据。

Method: 放弃概率文本生成，采用确定性方法遍历数据库外键图填充抽象语法树，通过配置向量Θ显式控制连接拓扑、分析强度和谓词选择性，保证模式有效性。

Result: 在TPC-H和IMDb上实验显示，SynQL生成接近最大多样性的工作负载（拓扑熵H=1.53比特），基于树的成本模型在合成测试集上达到R²≥0.79，推理延迟低于毫秒。

Conclusion: SynQL为生产日志不可访问时生成训练数据提供了有效基础，能够生成结构多样、模式有效的SQL工作负载，填补了学习型查询优化器的训练数据缺口。

Abstract: Database research and the development of learned query optimisers rely heavily on realistic SQL workloads. Acquiring real-world queries is increasingly difficult, however, due to strict privacy regulations, and publicly released anonymised traces typically strip out executable query text to preserve confidentiality. Existing synthesis tools fail to bridge this training data gap: traditional benchmarks offer too few fixed templates for statistical generalisation, while Large Language Model (LLM) approaches suffer from schema hallucination fabricating non-existent columns and topological collapse systematically defaulting to simplistic join patterns that fail to stress-test query optimisers. We propose SynQL, a deterministic workload synthesis framework that generates structurally diverse, execution-ready SQL workloads. As a foundational step toward bridging the training-data gap, SynQL targets the core SQL fragment -- multi-table joins with projections, aggregations, and range predicates -- which dominates analytical workloads. SynQL abandons probabilistic text generation in favour of traversing the live database's foreign-key graph to populate an Abstract Syntax Tree (AST), guaranteeing schema and syntactic validity by construction. A configuration vector $Θ$ provides explicit, parametric control over join topology (Star, Chain, Fork), analytical intensity, and predicate selectivity. Experiments on TPC-H and IMDb show that SynQL produces near-maximally diverse workloads (Topological Entropy $H = 1.53$ bits) and that tree-based cost models trained on the synthetic corpus achieve $R^2 \ge 0.79$ on held-out synthetic test sets with sub-millisecond inference latency, establishing SynQL as an effective foundation for generating training data when production logs are inaccessible.

</details>
